TL;DR: 在用 activation probe 做 LLM agent 运行时监控时，在用户输入后附加一句"分类指令"后缀能显著提升分布外检测效果（最高约 4 个 AUC 点），且增益来自"分类格式"本身而非具体准则措辞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 越来越依赖 activation probe 作为运行时监控来拦截 prompt injection、jailbreak 和不安全请求，读取模型隐状态在行动前发现有害输入。借鉴 LLM-as-judge，常见做法是在用户轮次后附加一句简短分类指令以锐化探针信号，成本极低。但该后缀的措辞是否重要、其收益在训练时未见过的攻击类型（真实部署场景）下是否仍成立，尚不清楚，需严格评估。

Method: 构建后用户后缀的受控梯度（ladder），在严格 leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) 协议下评估，覆盖 13 个安全基准（jailbreak、injection、benign chat）和三个开源模型家族（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-12B）。对比单位置探针与生产用多位置池化探针（attention、multi-max、MLP），并通过 KV-cache fork 部署。

Result: 在单位置探针上，分类后缀一致提升分布外检测（最高约 4 AUC 点）；要求模型对输入分类（即使是无内容标签）可靠获胜，离题或仅"专注"的后缀帮助很小。增益源于分类格式而非命名准则：无内容后缀可媲美真实恶意/良性后缀，准则仅在严格阈值下增加精确度。该收益延续到多位置池化探针，但最佳后缀依赖读出方式。

Conclusion: 分类后缀是任何 activation-probe monitor 的低成本即插即用增强，经 KV-cache fork 服务时开销极小；但并非自动获胜——哪个后缀有效、提升多少，取决于模型与读出方式。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on activation probes as runtime monitors for prompt injection, jailbreaks, and unsafe requests, reading the model's own hidden state to catch a harmful input before the agent acts on it. A cheap, increasingly common move, borrowed from LLM-as-judge prompting, is to append a short classification instruction after the user's turn and read the probe at that point, to sharpen it: the instruction asks the model to represent the incoming request as a class, concentrating the signal the probe must separate, at negligible serving cost. But does the wording of that suffix matter, and does its benefit hold in the wild, on attack types the probe never saw in training, the regime a deployed monitor faces? We test this with a controlled ladder of post-user suffixes under strict leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) evaluation across 13 safety benchmarks (jailbreak, injection, and benign chat) and three open-weight model families (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-12B). On a single-position probe, a classification suffix consistently improves out-of-distribution detection over no suffix (up to ~4 AUC points); yet which suffix matters: prompting the model to classify the input, even into content-free labels, reliably wins; an off-topic or merely-attentive suffix helps little. The gain comes from the classification format, not the named criterion: a content-free suffix matches the real malicious/benign one, with the criterion adding precision only at strict thresholds. This is not an artifact of the single-position read: the benefit carries to the multi-position pooling probes used in production (attention, multi-max, MLP), though the best-performing suffix there is readout-dependent. Served through a KV-cache fork, it is a cheap drop-in for any activation-probe monitor, though not an automatic win: which suffix helps, and by how much, depends on the model and the readout.

</details>


### [37] [The AI Theorist reveals excitonic structure in $α$-RuCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02417)
*Hongjian Zhou,Xianfan Nie,Sean Wu,Tarun Patel,Jinge Wu,Andrew Liu,Adam Wei Tsen,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI Theorist"——一个由AI智能体组成的系统，可自主完成假设生成、第一性原理计算与证据驱动的模型修正，并被成功应用于α-RuCl₃的光谱研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验仪器与自动化的进步产生了日益丰富的数据集，但将实验观测转化为微观物理理解仍是科学发现的瓶颈。作者希望加速这一过程，实现物理模型的自主发现。

Method: 构建AI Theorist多智能体系统，通过假设生成、第一性原理计算和证据驱动的迭代修正来发现物理模型；并将其应用于Kitaev量子自旋液体候选材料α-RuCl₃，结合光学光谱研究其电子结构，使用第一性原理电子结构与多体计算。

Result: AI Theorist对光学与光电流观测给出了全新解读，识别出具有对比性光学选择定则和实空间分布的不同激子态。据作者所知，这是首个AI系统自主发展物理模型、解释量子材料中此前未发表实验观测的演示。

Conclusion: 该工作为材料科学中的自主理论发现开辟了路径：AI智能体可利用第一性原理计算，将实验观测转化为物理模型和可检验的预测。

Abstract: Advances in experimental instrumentation and automation generate increasingly rich datasets, but turning experimental observations into microscopic understanding remains a bottleneck in scientific discovery. To accelerate this process, we introduce AI Theorist, a system of artificial intelligence (AI) agents for autonomous discovery of physical models through hypothesis generation, first-principles calculations and evidence-driven refinement. We apply the framework to $α$-RuCl$_3$, a leading candidate material for realizing a Kitaev quantum spin liquid, to investigate its electronic structure through optical spectra. AI Theorist develops a new interpretation of the optical and photocurrent observations, identifying distinct excitonic states with contrasting optical selection rules and real-space distributions. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an AI system autonomously developing a physical model to explain previously unpublished experimental observations in a quantum material, utilizing first-principles electronic-structure and many-body calculations. Our results establish a route to autonomous theoretical discovery in materials science, in which AI agents use first-principles calculations to turn experimental observations into physical models and testable predictions.

</details>


### [38] [Geometry-Aware Time Reparameterization for Flow-Map Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02427)
*Félix Dedek,Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何感知的时间重参数化，在保持教师几何路径与终端分布的同时，将更多学生时间分配给法向加速度大的轨迹区域，并用于拉格朗日流图蒸馏，提升少步生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流图蒸馏通过学习预训练生成式ODE的有限时间转移，实现一步或少步生成。作者假设法向加速度大的轨迹段更难蒸馏，因此希望改变教师的时间参数化，使这些难以学习的转移段获得更多学生时间，从而更易学习。

Method: 提出几何感知的时间重参数化：在适当假设下推导一个共享时钟，使总体法向加速度统计量趋于均衡；再从教师轨迹上的稳健、正则化估计构造实用近似。该时钟在蒸馏前一次性估计，不改变教师的几何路径和终端分布，也不需要重训练教师、增加学生参数或推理时的额外网络评估，最后将其嵌入拉格朗日流图蒸馏，用变换后的时间坐标条件化学生网络。

Result: 在合成数据、CIFAR-10和CelebA-64上，匹配推理预算时样本质量优于恒等时间蒸馏，包括一步和两步图像生成均有提升；其中一步生成无法调整中间采样时间，其增益突出了时间重参数化的作用。

Conclusion: 改变教师的时间参数化可让流图蒸馏更易学习，几何感知时钟在无额外训练与推理成本下改善了少步生成质量。

Abstract: Flow-map distillation enables one- and few-step generation by learning finite-time transitions of a pretrained generative ODE. We investigate whether changing the teacher's time parameterization can make these transitions easier to learn. Motivated by the hypothesis that trajectory segments with large normal acceleration are harder to distill, we propose a geometry-aware time reparameterization that allocates more student time to these regions while preserving the teacher's geometric paths and terminal distribution. We derive a shared clock that equalizes a population normal-acceleration statistic under suitable assumptions, and construct a practical approximation from robust, regularized estimates across teacher trajectories. We incorporate this clock into Lagrangian flow-map distillation, using the transformed time coordinate to condition the student. The clock is estimated once before distillation and requires neither teacher retraining nor additional student parameters or inference-time network evaluations. Experiments on synthetic data, CIFAR-10, and CelebA-64 show improved sample quality over identity-time distillation at matched inference budgets, including improvements in one- and two-step image generation. The gains in one-step generation, where no intermediate sampling times can be adjusted, highlight the benefits of time reparameterization during distillation.

</details>


### [39] [CRISP: A Framework for Clause-Reconstructed Interpretable NeuroSymbolic Propositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02431)
*Alex Chan,Shafi Muhtasim Chowdhury,Ekin Can Erkuş,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Alex Yakovlev,Rishad Shafik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP 将二值神经教师的最后一层激活向量重构为 Tsetlin Machine 子句，实现从布尔化输入阈值到教师神经元的符号化追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽精度高，但决策证据编码在隐藏激活中而非显式规则里，导致决策难以审计。本文旨在为二值神经教师提供可解释的最终隐藏表征通路。

Method: 对教师倒数第二层预 logit 激活做符号二值化，为 LLAV 每个神经元分配一个独立 TM（ITM）；每个重构隐藏位用布尔化输入特征上的命题子句表示；并对二值阈值、温度计编码与分位数分箱在多比特深度下进行额外研究。

Result: 在 MNIST、KMNIST、FMNIST、SVHN、CIFAR10 上，以 BCCNN 与全二值 BNN 为教师：LLAV 符号二值化在测试的 BNN 设置下不降低教师头部精度，ITM 重构误差是主要限制因素；四分位单位布尔化在 SVHN 上取得 87.52% 测试保真度，在 CIFAR10 上具竞争力，重构 LLAV 在 FMNIST 上保留 78.41% 教师头部精度；池化子句证据可视化显示所学 ITM 文字集中于居中基准的物体区域。

Conclusion: CRISP 为检查二值神经教师的最终隐藏表征提供了一条子句级途径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve high accuracy through layered numerical transformations, yet their decisions remain difficult to audit because decision evidence is encoded in hidden activations rather than explicit rules. This paper introduces CRISP, a framework that reconstructs the last-layer activation vector (LLAV) of binary neural teachers as Tsetlin Machine (TM) clauses. CRISP sign-binarizes the teacher's penultimate pre-logit activations, and assigns one Individual TM (ITM) to each LLAV neuron. Each reconstructed hidden bit is represented by propositional clauses over Booleanized input features, which gives a direct symbolic trace from named input thresholds to a named teacher neuron. CRISP is evaluated on MNIST, KMNIST, FashionMNIST (FMNIST), SVHN, and CIFAR10 using a BinaryConnect convolutional neural network (BCCNN) teacher and a fully binary neural network (BNN) teacher, with an additional study on binary thresholding, thermometer encoding, and quartile binning at multiple bit depths. The results show that LLAV sign-binarization does not reduce teacher-head accuracy in the tested BNN setting, while ITM reconstruction error is the main limiting factor. Quartile one-bit Booleanization gives the strongest reconstruction fidelity on SVHN at 87.52% test fidelity and is competitive on CIFAR10, and the reconstructed LLAV preserves 78.41% teacher-head accuracy on FMNIST. Pooled clause-evidence visualizations show that the learned ITM literals concentrate on the object region in centered benchmarks. CRISP therefore provides a clause-level route for inspecting the final hidden representation of binary neural teachers.

</details>


### [40] [Are you Synthesizing or Recalling? Evaluating LLMs on Algorithmic Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02438)
*Nickil Maveli,Antonio Vergari,Shay B. Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将著名算法的代码生成重新定义为"参数化代码检索"能力，并构建AlgoREval基准（599题、77个算法、14领域、7语言、4种图表示），评测15个7B–34B模型在零样本下的检索准确率，发现语言与输入表示带来显著差异，并比较了提示增强、SFT与GRPO三种改进手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成流程是不透明的，未能显式区分"回忆相关算法知识"与"推理如何应用"这两个成分。作者认为，对于经典实现广泛存在于预训练语料中的知名算法，代码生成更适合被衡量为参数化代码检索，即从内部知识中复现指定算法，而非合成新算法，因此需要一个能孤立评测该能力的基准。

Method: 构建AlgoREval基准：599道题目，覆盖14个领域的77个经典算法、7种编程语言、4种图输入表示。在零样本设置下评测15个模型（7B–34B参数）。此外比较三种改进策略：带检索代码片段或结构化算法提示的提示增强、有监督微调（SFT）、以及GRPO强化学习方法。

Result: 即使在文档充分的知名算法上，检索准确率在不同编程语言和不同图输入表示之间仍存在显著差异。提示增强（检索代码片段或结构化算法提示）可提升复杂算法上的准确率；SFT带来更广泛的语言层面提升；GRPO在特定语言上获得更大的单语言增益。

Conclusion: 研究确立了参数化代码检索是一种独立且可测量的能力，并警示在缺乏系统验证的情况下不应直接部署AI生成的算法代码。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in code generation, where success depends on both recalling relevant algorithmic knowledge and reasoning about how to apply it. However, existing LLM pipelines are opaque, with no explicit separation between these two components. We argue that for well-known algorithms whose canonical implementations are widely accessible in pretraining corpora, code generation is better measured as \textit{parametric code retrieval}: reproducing a named algorithm from internalised knowledge rather than synthesizing a novel one. We introduce AlgoREval, a benchmark of 599 problems spanning classical 77 algorithms across 14 domains, 7 programming languages, and 4 graph-input representations to evaluate this capability in isolation, and assess 15 models (7B--34B parameters) in a zero-shot setting. We find substantial variation in retrieval accuracy across languages and input representations, even for widely documented algorithms and show that prompt augmentation with retrieved code snippets or structured algorithmic hints improve accuracy on complex algorithms, while SFT achieves broader language gains and GRPO achieves larger per-language gains on specific languages. Together, our results establish parametric code retrieval as a distinct, measurable capability and caution against deploying AI-generated algorithmic code without systematic validation.\footnote{Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Nickil21/AlgoREval

</details>


### [41] [A Generative Model of Complex Networks Using Graphons and Neural Inverse Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02439)
*Wooseong Choi,Italo'Ivo Lima Dias Pinto,Chen Sun,Gaurav Gupta,Dong Song,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在函数空间中统一机制模型与深度生成模型的生成图框架：以多重分形阶梯图子(multifractal step graphon)紧凑参数化复杂网络，并用神经逆算子恢复参数，实现可解释且可摊销的推断，支持训练规模之外的图尺寸，可迁移到真实网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成图模型存在互补的优劣：机制模型可解释性强但依赖针对具体实例的估计方法；深度生成模型支持摊销推断，却牺牲可解释性且基本局限于训练时见过的图规模。科学应用需要同时保留两种范式优势的框架。

Method: 将生成模型与参数恢复统一在函数空间中建模：提出多重分形阶梯图子，用递归构造在标准阶梯图子基础上紧凑参数化复杂网络；该表述允许引入神经逆算子来恢复其参数，从而可在未见过的图规模上进行推断。模型仅在合成的多重分形阶梯图子样本上训练。

Result: 与在真实网络上预训练的图基础模型相比，在零样本图生成基准的四项指标中有三项取得最佳平均表现，说明可迁移到真实图。在单观测网络上（深度模型难以涉足的设定），表现与逐图优化的实例专用方法相当。在多被试EEG案例中，推断参数比传统网络统计量更灵敏地追踪可逆的脑状态变化。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性与摊销推断可以在同一生成图模型中被有效统一，从而增强对复杂网络的理解。

Abstract: Generative graph models are central to understanding and simulating complex networks. However, existing approaches have complementary strengths and limitations. Mechanistic models offer interpretability but rely on instance-specific estimation methods. Deep generative models, on the other hand, offer amortized inference at the cost of interpretability and are largely limited to graph sizes seen during training. Scientific applications motivate a framework that retains the strengths of both paradigms. We bridge them by formulating both the generative model and parameter recovery in function space. A multifractal step graphon extends standard step graphons with a recursive construction that compactly parameterizes complex networks. This formulation admits a neural inverse operator to recover its parameters, enabling inference on unseen graph sizes. We evaluate our model, trained only on synthetic multifractal step graphon realizations, against both paradigms. Against a graph foundation model pretrained on empirical networks, our method achieves the best average performance on three of four metrics in a zero-shot graph-generation benchmark, indicating that the model transfers to real-world graphs. We also apply our method to single-observation networks, a regime largely inaccessible to deep models that require training corpora, where it performs comparably to an instance-specific method that optimizes on each graph. In a multi-subject EEG case study, the inferred parameters track a reversible change in brain state more sensitively than traditional network statistics. Together, these results indicate that mechanistic interpretability and amortized inference can be effectively unified in a generative graph model to enhance our understanding of complex networks.

</details>


### [42] [Bandits via Additive Quantized Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02440)
*Ami Tavory,Noam Touitou,Tal Sarig,Frank Cheng,Ido Guy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出残差量化（RQ）作为表示层，将连续上下文映射为多层级离散质心分配，使加性bandit算法在严格有界内存下获得非线性表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文bandit需要在非线性奖励建模与在线效率之间取得平衡。树集成和神经方法能捕捉非线性，但需要周期性重训练和大型回放缓冲区；线性模型可高效地逐观测更新且内存为O(1)，却受限于线性奖励结构。作者希望弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出残差量化（RQ）作为表示层：离线训练的RQ码本将连续上下文映射为多层级上的离散质心分配，并通过shadow机制动态设定。基于此构建一系列加性bandit算法，在严格有界内存下实现非线性表达力。

Result: 在13个数据集上，RQ变体在其中11个数据集上优于其非RQ对应方法，且往往优势明显；同时以最多少1000倍的内存匹配需要倍增重训练的XGBoost和神经基线。

Conclusion: RQ表示层可作为通用桥梁，使高效加性bandit算法在极低内存开销下获得非线性建模能力，兼顾在线效率与表达力。

Abstract: Contextual bandits require balancing nonlinear reward modeling with online efficiency. Tree ensembles and neural methods capture nonlinearities but require periodic retraining and large replay buffers. Linear models update efficiently per observation with O(1) memory, but are fundamentally restricted to linear reward structures. We propose Residual Quantization (RQ) as a representation layer to bridge this gap. An offline-trained RQ codebook maps continuous contexts into discrete centroid assignments across multiple levels, set dynamically through a shadow mechanism. This enables a spectrum of additive bandit algorithms that achieve nonlinear expressivity with strictly bounded memory. Across 13 datasets, RQ variants beat their non-RQ counterparts on 11 of 13 datasets, often by wide margins, while matching doubling-retrain XGBoost and neural baselines using up to 1000 times less memory.

</details>

*Yixing Li,Mark Fenton,Matthew Kaufeler,Ka Ming Leung,Xin Ai,Zhiyu Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI驱动的数据中心热感知容量规划框架，用嵌入的AI模型从机架功率、服务器功率、服务器布局、HVAC设置等关键参数学习，可在毫秒级预测温度，并在未见过设计上以万倍加速达到高精度，从而秒级完成热感知容量规划。


<details>

Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推动数据中心热管理面临新挑战：GPU密集计算产生局部热点，训练与推理突发导致热负荷尖峰，使实时冷却响应更难以预测和控制。而热感知容量规划依赖大量昂贵的高保真CFD仿真；现有AI方法要么预测误差大，要么对数据中心运行做了过度简化假设。

Method: 提出一个AI驱动的框架用于真实数据中心的热感知容量规划。其内嵌AI模型从大量关键参数（机架功率、服务器功率、服务器摆放、HVAC设置等）中学习，实现毫秒级温度预测；并基于该模型构建热感知容量规划框架。

Result: 该AI模型与高保真CFD仿真对比测试，对未出现过的数据中心设计仍能取得高精度，并实现10000倍加速。

Conclusion: 该框架可帮助数据中心设计者和运维者即时优化工作负载分布与HVAC冷却效率，实现秒级的热感知容量规划。

Abstract: The emerging of large language models (LLMs) has posed significant challenges to the thermal management of data center. Intense GPU computation for LLMs results in localized hotspots. Moreover, spiking thermal loads during training and inference bursts make real-time cooling response more difficult to predict and control. Thermal-aware capacity planning of data center requires massive expensive high-fidelity CFD simulations. AI models can perform real-time prediction for unseen designs. However, existing works either have large prediction error, or have over-simplified assumptions for data center operations. This work presents an AI-driven framework that can perform thermal-aware capacity planning for a real-world data center in seconds. The embedded AI model learns from numerous key parameters (rack power, server power, server placement, HVAC settings etc.), and provides temperature prediction within milliseconds. This AI model is tested against high-fidelity CFD simulations, and results show that for unseen data center designs, model can achieve high accuracy with 10000X speedup. Driven by the AI model, the authors design the thermal-aware capacity planning framework. This framework can help data center designers and operators instantaneously optimize both workload distribution and HVAC cooling efficiency.

</details>


### [44] [A Composable AI-Accelerated Iterative Solver for 3D-IC Thermal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02461)
*Yixing Li,Jiahang Zhou,Zhiyu Zeng,Xin Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAIST，将全局封装热仿真分解为块级子域问题并用神经算子求解，通过界面温度/热流迭代耦合，实现可组合、跨拓扑复用的热求解器，最高178倍加速，平均温度误差0.068%–0.323%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构2.5D/3D-IC封装的热分析至关重要，但计算代价高昂：单次全封装FEM仿真耗时可长达数小时；而现有AI代理模型把整个堆叠当作单一预测目标，一旦芯片数量或拓扑改变就必须重新训练，存在“拓扑锁定”问题。

Method: DAIST采用域分解的AI加速迭代求解框架：将全局封装仿真拆解为块级子域问题，用神经算子替代各子域求解器，并通过界面温度与热流的迭代交换实现耦合。该“局部到全局”架构使块级神经算子可直接复用于未见过的封装组合而无需重训练，同时迭代次数可调节，提供可控的精度—运行时间权衡。

Result: 在多芯片系统与先进封装系统上评估，DAIST相对传统FEM求解器最高实现178倍加速，平均温度误差分别为0.068%和0.323%；并展示了块级模型在结构不同的封装组合间的跨拓扑复用能力。

Conclusion: DAIST通过域分解与神经算子结合，打破了单体式AI代理的拓扑锁定限制，兼顾高精度与高速度，为异构2.5D/3D-IC封装热分析提供了可组合、可复用且精度—效率可调的求解方案。

Abstract: Accurate thermal analysis of heterogeneous 2.5D/3D-IC packages is essential yet computationally prohibitive. A single full-package FEM simulation can take hours, while AI-based surrogates treat the entire stack as a monolithic prediction target and must be retrained whenever the die count or topology changes. To address this limitation, this work proposes Domain-Decomposed AI-Accelerated Iterative Solver for Thermal Analysis (DAIST), a composable thermal solver that decomposes the global package simulation into block-level subdomain problems, replaces subdomain solvers with neural operators, and couples them through iterative exchanges of interfacial temperature and heat flux. This local-to-global architecture eliminates the topology lock-in of monolithic models: block-level neural operators can be directly reused in unseen package assemblies without retraining. The iterative coupling strategy further provides a controllable accuracy-runtime tradeoff, where the iteration budget can be adjusted to trade accuracy for runtime. Evaluated on a multi-chiplet system and an advanced packaging system, DAIST achieves up to $178\times$ speedup over traditional FEM solvers with mean temperature errors of 0.068% and 0.323%, respectively, while demonstrating cross-topology reuse of block-level models across structurally distinct package assemblies.

</details>


### [45] [Capability Scaling-Down Laws for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02462)
*Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Liangjie Hong,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏）的能力下降缩放律，建立了将能力损失与模型规模、训练阶段、压缩设置等联系起来的预测关系，并评估了其预测精度、测量效率与在压缩方法选择中的决策价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM压缩虽能降低推理成本与内存需求，但方法及配置的选择主要依赖经验，因为相似的资源削减可能造成不同程度的能力损失，缺乏可预测的规律来指导选择。

Method: 构建统一框架，在数学、代码生成、问答任务上测量能力损失，并将其与模型规模、训练阶段、压缩设置、数据可用性和训练曝光度关联；开发简洁的预测关系，评估其准确性、测量效率及对未见配置与模型状态的泛化能力；通过对照实验研究蒸馏中的数据重用成本；并以数值选择与配置中位数、固定方法优先级对比来评估预测的决策价值。

Result: 跨剪枝级别共享密度响应可将拟合剪枝预测器所需的配置测量量减半：在Pythia新状态、预注册的OLMo-2测试状态及Wanda剪枝下，紧凑关系在数学与代码上以每token 0.020 nats以内的误差匹配全测量的回归；受控蒸馏实验显示重度数据重用的成本在问答分布上反复出现，净收益取决于评估分布；跨两个模型族的独立评估表明，选择能捕获问答上大部分跨方法收益，固定方法优先级达到相同遗憾，数学与代码机会较小。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了能力下降缩放律的预测适用范围及其在压缩方法选择中的应用价值，代码已公开（https://github.com/LabRAI/scaling_down_law）。

Abstract: LLM compression reduces inference costs and memory requirements, but selecting a method and configuration remains largely empirical because comparable resource reductions can produce different capability losses. We systematically investigate capability scaling-down laws for LLM compression across pruning, quantization, and distillation. Our framework measures capability loss in mathematics, code generation, and question answering, and relates these measurements to model size, training stage, compression settings, data availability, and training exposure. We develop simple predictive relations and evaluate their accuracy, measurement efficiency, and generalization to unseen configurations and model states. Sharing the density response across pruning levels halves the configuration measurements needed to fit a pruning predictor: on new Pythia states, on pre-registered OLMo-2 test states and under Wanda pruning, the compact relation matches a regression fitted with all measurements on math and code to within 0.020 nats per token, with coefficients refitted for each setting. Controlled distillation experiments show that the cost of heavy data reuse recurs across question-answering distributions, while the net benefit depends on the evaluation distribution. We further evaluate the decision value of these predictions by comparing numerical selection with configuration medians and fixed method priorities. Independent evaluations across two model families show that selection captures most of the available cross-method benefit for question answering within the tested candidate sets, where a fixed method priority attains the same regret, with smaller opportunities for mathematics and code. These results clarify the predictive scope of capability scaling-down laws and their use in compression method selection. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/scaling_down_law.

</details>


### [46] [Threshold-Aware Conformal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02487)
*Shiwei Tan,Huzefa Rangwala,Danielle C. Maddix*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出阈值感知共形路由（TACR），学习输入相关的区间尺度，使区间紧密性集中在决策边界附近，在保持共形覆盖保证的同时减少模拟器调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真模拟重复运行成本高，学习型代理模型虽快但误差可能改变下游决策，需判断何时可用代理、何时必须用完整模拟器。现有共形预测构建区间时不考虑下游决策阈值，导致阈值附近的窄区间也可能跨越阈值而触发模拟，而远离阈值的宽区间反而无需模拟，造成不必要的模拟调用。

Method: 针对"标量关注量是否高于或低于固定阈值"的决策问题：当代理的共形区间完全位于阈值一侧时采用代理，当区间与阈值相交时路由到模拟。提出TACR，用一个阈值感知目标学习输入相关的尺度，将区间紧致性集中在决策边界附近。在留出数据上做精确的分裂共形校准，以保持无分布假设的边际覆盖，并据此上界未路由情况下错误阈值决策的概率。

Result: 在多个科学与工程数据集上，相同覆盖目标下，TACR相比标准共形预测将模拟器延迟减少14-75%；与不使用阈值局部加权但预测精度相近的变体相比，TACR在四个数据集上进一步减少延迟10-24%。

Conclusion: 为路由而优化区间分配可在不削弱标准共形保证的前提下减少模拟器调用，说明阈值感知的区间分配是提升代理-模拟器混合决策效率的有效途径。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulations are essential to scientific and engineering design, but can be expensive to run repeatedly. Learned surrogates offer a faster alternative, yet their higher errors may alter downstream decisions. This accuracy-speed tradeoff creates a need to determine whether a surrogate can be used or the full simulator remains necessary. We study decisions determined by whether a scalar quantity of interest lies above or below a fixed threshold. For each input, we use the surrogate when its conformal interval lies entirely on one side of the threshold and route the input to simulation when the interval intersects it. Standard conformal prediction constructs intervals without reference to the downstream decision threshold: even a narrow interval near the threshold can cross it and trigger simulation, whereas a wider interval farther away can remain entirely on one side and require no simulation. We introduce Threshold-Aware Conformal Routing (TACR), which learns an input-dependent scale using a threshold-aware objective that concentrates interval tightness near the decision boundary. Exact split-conformal calibration on held-out data preserves distribution-free marginal coverage, which also upper-bounds the probability of an incorrect threshold decision that is not routed. Across various scientific and engineering datasets, TACR reduces simulator deferrals by 14-75% relative to standard conformal prediction at the same coverage target. Against a variant without threshold-local weighting but with similar predictor accuracy, TACR further reduces deferrals by 10-24% on four datasets. These results show that optimizing interval allocation for routing can reduce simulator calls without weakening the standard conformal guarantee.

</details>


### [47] [Harnessing LLMs as Agents: What Does It Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02488)
*Zelin Zhao,Xinyu Guo,Jingyuan Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出语言模型智能体机器（LAM），一个资源受限的抽象框架，在固定底层语义模型的同时显式计量智能体"外壳"（上下文、记忆、工具、验证、重复执行）所消耗的计算资源，并给出通信、访问、重计算与可靠性四类理论结果及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体日益依赖管理有限上下文、持久记忆、工具调用、验证和重复执行的外壳机制，但现有模型能力概念并未量化这些机制所消耗的计算资源，缺乏刻画智能体系统真实开销的资源理论。

Method: 引入语言模型智能体机器（LAM）：一种资源受限抽象，固定底层语义模型，同时对壳层资源显式计费。据此建立四类结果——通信（执行与红蓝卵石博弈等价，迁移经典I/O下界到上下文—内存流量）、访问（内存接口导致渐近分离，指针追逐上随机与非投机顺序访问存在Θ(n)差距）、重计算（比特反转DAG在上下文容量C与持久记忆容量S下需Θ(n²/(C+S)+n)次模型调用）、可靠性（紧的阶段性采样界、精确的不完美验证代价、Young–Daly型检查点定律及闭环最优验证间隔）。并在GPT-6 Astra上进行受控与留出实验。

Result: 实验验证了通信与可靠性预测，包括检查点最优值、程序化检查下的策略选择，以及链式MATH任务中调用粒度、逻辑输入流量与可靠性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 这些结果为语言模型智能体外壳的计算成本提供了一套资源理论。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on harnesses that manage bounded context, persistent memory, tools, verification, and repeated execution, yet existing notions of model capability do not quantify the computational resources these mechanisms consume. We introduce the Language Model Agent Machine (LAM), a resource-bounded abstraction that fixes the underlying semantic model while explicitly charging harness-level resources. We establish four classes of results. Communication: LAM execution is instancewise equivalent to red--blue pebbling under simultaneous call--transfer budgets, transferring classical I/O lower bounds to context--memory traffic. Access: memory interfaces induce asymptotic separations, including a $Θ(n)$ gap between random and non-speculative sequential access on pointer chasing. Recomputation: bit-reversal DAGs require $Θ(n^2/(C+S)+n)$ model calls with context capacity $C$ and persistent-memory capacity $S$, quantifying when stored intermediate state avoids repeated semantic computation. Reliability: we derive tight stage-local sampling bounds, exact imperfect-verification costs, and a Young--Daly-type checkpoint law with a closed-form optimal verification interval. Controlled and held-out experiments on GPT-6 Astra test communication and reliability predictions, including checkpoint optima, policy selection under programmatic checking, and tradeoffs among call granularity, logical input traffic, and reliability on chained MATH tasks. Together, these results provide a resource theory for the computational cost of language-model agent harnesses.

</details>


### [48] [LiteEMG-FM: An Efficient and Deployable Foundation Model for Robust EMG Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02497)
*Tianhao Wu,Xu Wu,Amirmohammad Radmehr,Jiawei Yu,Yi Wu,Phuc Nguyen,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LiteEMG-FM，一种高效混合CNN-Transformer肌电信号基础模型，支持实时可穿戴部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMG信号在个体、部位、采集会话和硬件间差异大，限制模型泛化；现有时间序列基础模型计算开销大，难以实时可穿戴部署，且难以捕捉EMG特有的时频特征。

Method: 构建混合CNN-Transformer基础模型，在16个上下肢EMG数据集上预训练；采用分层唤醒架构，由常开轻量1D-CNN过滤静息与非目标活动，仅对有效手势激活主模型；评估全推理卸载、拆分推理与全设备端处理三种模式。

Result: LiteEMG-FM在多种评估设置下优于最先进的时间序列基础模型和有监督基线，尤其在零校准跨受试者和数据稀缺条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: LiteEMG-FM是一款有效、高效且可部署的EMG基础模型，适用于辅助设备和人机交互应用。

Abstract: Electromyography (EMG) signals vary substantially across individuals, body regions, recording sessions, and sensing hardware, limiting the generalization of models for assistive devices and human-computer interaction. Existing time-series foundation models are also computationally expensive for real-time wearable deployment and often fail to capture EMG-specific time-frequency characteristics. We present LiteEMG-FM, an efficient hybrid CNN-Transformer foundation model for practical EMG sensing. Pretrained on 16 diverse upper- and lower-limb EMG datasets, LiteEMG-FM learns representations that generalize across users and datasets. For resource-constrained deployment, we implement a hierarchical wake-up architecture in which a lightweight, always-on 1D-CNN filters rest and non-target activity and activates LiteEMG-FM only for valid gestures. We evaluate full inference offloading, split inference, and full on-device processing, characterizing their trade-offs in latency, power consumption, and memory footprint. Across diverse evaluation settings, LiteEMG-FM outperforms state-of-the-art time-series foundation models and supervised baselines, particularly under zero-calibration cross-participant and data-scarce conditions. These results demonstrate that LiteEMG-FM is an effective, efficient, and deployable foundation model for EMG applications.

</details>


### [49] [Multi-Fidelity Policy Gradients Stabilize Data-Scarce Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02505)
*Xinjie Liu,Ruihan Zhao,Anirban Chaudhuri,Cyrus Neary,Ufuk Topcu,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MFPG-PPO，将多保真策略梯度框架扩展到现代actor-critic学习，通过重新设计采样、优势估计与控制变量构造来保持跨保真相关性并防止方差膨胀，在仿真机器人运动任务与真实Franka机械臂上验证了其数据效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略强化学习中，目标域的高保真数据昂贵且稀缺，导致梯度估计噪声大、训练不稳定。低保真数据虽然廉价丰富但存在偏差。现有方法多直接优化带偏的目标，而MFPG框架仅用低保真数据构造控制变量以降低方差、提升高保真数据效率，但已有工作局限于REINFORCE和小规模仿真，尚未适配现代actor-critic学习与真实机器人场景。

Method: 作者将MFPG扩展到GPU并行仿真与真实机器人上的现代actor-critic学习。分析表明朴素地将MFPG用于PPO会丢失跨保真相关性或放大方差。为此提出MFPG-PPO，重新设计采样、优势估计和控制变量构造以保持跨保真相关性，并通过监测估计器不确定性来防止方差膨胀；进一步提出预算感知的MFPG-PPO，在固定采样预算下分配高、低保真数据源的比例。

Result: 在具有不同低保真到高保真迁移难度和高保真数据预算的仿真机器人运动任务中，MFPG-PPO在几乎所有设置下优于仅用高保真数据训练的PPO；在最难任务的最小高保真预算下，其性能持续匹配使用16倍高保真数据训练的PPO。而多数使用低保真数据的基线仅能在直接低保真到高保真迁移成功时表现良好。此外，MFPG-PPO在真实Franka机械臂上仅用每次更新4个真实机器人回合、无人类示范即可实现稳定学习。

Conclusion: MFPG-PPO成功将多保真策略梯度推广到现代actor-critic框架与真实机器人场景，证明了在保持无偏策略梯度的同时有效利用低保真数据可将方差降至可控、显著提升高保真数据效率，为昂贵目标域数据下的稳定强化学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Policy gradient methods for on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) can become unstable when expensive, scarce target-domain data yield noisy gradient estimates. We address this challenge by complementing limited high-fidelity (HF) target-domain data with abundant, cheap, but biased low-fidelity (LF) data, e.g., from a simplified simulator. Most existing methods directly optimize biased objectives based on LF data. In contrast, the recently introduced multi-fidelity policy gradient (MFPG) framework uses LF data solely to construct a control variate that reduces variance and improves HF data efficiency without biasing the policy gradient estimator. However, published work on MFPG is limited to REINFORCE on small-scale simulation tasks. We develop MFPG for modern actor-critic learning in GPU-parallel simulation and on a physical robot. Our analysis and experiments show that naive extensions to proximal policy optimization (PPO) can lose cross-fidelity correlation or inflate variance. Our MFPG-PPO addresses these failures by redesigning the sampling, advantage estimation, and control variate construction to preserve cross-fidelity correlation, and by monitoring estimator uncertainty to prevent variance inflation. We also introduce a budget-aware MFPG-PPO to divide a fixed sampling budget among high- and low-fidelity data sources. Across simulated robot locomotion tasks of varying LF-to-HF transfer difficulty and HF data budgets, MFPG-PPO improves upon PPO trained on HF data alone in nearly all settings, and consistently matches the performance of PPO trained with 16x more HF data on the hardest task at the smallest HF budgets. In contrast, most baselines that use LF data perform well only where direct LF-to-HF transfer succeeds. MFPG-PPO enables stable learning on a physical Franka arm using only 4 real-robot episodes per update and no human demonstrations.

</details>


### [50] [Post-Training Quantization of Autoregressive Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02511)
*Ananyo Bhattacharya,Swastik Bhattacharya,Christiane Jablonowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统评估了训练后量化（PTQ）对全球尺度天气预测AI模拟器（DLWP和FourCastNet）的影响，发现PTQ配置在短时预报范围内仍能给出定性有意义的预报，为自回归天气模拟器的量化优化提供了首个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率数值天气预报（NWP）和数据同化的发展推动了用于模拟大气动力学的深度学习架构进步。这类天气预测模拟器预报质量可与物理模型相当，但依赖GPU硬件加速的矩阵乘法进行自回归推理。通过训练后量化（PTQ）可加速推理、提升单位时间计算量并降低功耗，从而支持向高分辨率域扩展及边缘硬件部署。然而，PTQ对预训练天气预测AI模拟器的具体影响尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 将PTQ算法应用于预训练的深度学习天气预测（DLWP）和FourCastNet（FCN）模型，作为地球物理流体动力学应用的验证性概念。系统研究PTQ对模拟器在短程预报时域内推理的影响，并使用模拟量化评估不同PTQ配置。

Result: 使用模拟量化评估PTQ配置表明，在短时预报时域内可获得定性上有意义的预报结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为自回归天气模拟器的PTQ提供了首个基准，并为动态系统深度学习模型基于量化的优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Advancements in high-resolution numerical weather prediction (NWP) and data assimilation (DA) have shaped the developments in deep learning (DL) architectures emulating atmospheric dynamics. Emulators for weather forecasting exhibit forecast quality comparable to physics based models at forecast horizon scaling from few days to subseasonal time scales. The emulators are driven by hardware-accelerated matrix multiplication in autoregressive inferences, significantly reducing the computation time and resources required for NWP. Optimization of the matrix multiplication processes in GPU architectures provides opportunities to scale towards high-resolution domain, and offers implementation of out of the box solutions. Post-training quantization (PTQ) has been demonstrated across multiple DL architectures to accelerate and increase the number of computations in unit time while consuming less power, enabling applications on edge hardware. In this study, we investigate the effect of PTQ on pre-trained AI emulators for global-scale weather forecasting. We implement PTQ algorithms in Deep Learning Weather Prediction (DLWP) and FourCastNet (FCN) models as a proof of concept for geophysical fluid dynamics applications. We systematically investigate the effect of PTQ on emulator inferences over short-range forecast horizons. Evaluation of PTQ configurations using simulated quantization hints at qualitatively meaningful forecasts over short-time horizons. These results provide a first benchmark of PTQ for autoregressive weather emulators and a basis for quantization-based optimization of DL models for dynamical systems.

</details>


### [51] [Student-Guided Teacher Distillation for Efficient LLM Task Routing: Positioning Against Jev-Style System-1 Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02516)
*Haifeng Wu,Srinivasan Manoharan,Jian Wan,Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种"学生引导教师"蒸馏流水线：用紧凑的 ModernBERT 学生模型一次前向预测全部 60 类分布并召回 top-k 候选，再由较大的 DeBERTa-v3 零样本 NLI 教师仅对候选重排，迭代互促，从而把随分类体系线性增长的打分成本降为常数级候选评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本 NLI 分类器用于将用户请求路由到特定 LLM 任务时，需对每个标签评估一次前提-假设对，成本随分类体系规模线性增长，无法对全部候选集逐一打分。作者希望在不损失决策相关信息的前提下，将大规模标签打分压缩为小规模候选重排。

Method: 面向固定 60 类 LLM 任务体系构建学生引导教师蒸馏流程：学生为紧凑的 ModernBERT 分类器，单次前向输出完整类别分布并检索 top-k 候选；教师为更大的 DeBERTa-v3 零样本 NLI 分类器，仅对 top-k 候选重排而非全部 60 个标签；教师产生的标签反过来迭代改进学生，学生再生成更精准的候选进入下一轮。与通用嵌入检索、极端多标签分类中的聚类短名单不同，候选生成器在目标体系上端到端训练，且即生产环境所用模型。同时分析了截断 top-k 教师分数用于 KL 蒸馏的偏差问题。

Result: 最佳学生检查点在 200 例评估集上达到 77.5% 的教师一致率；初步覆盖率测量显示 Coverage@16 为 91–100%，表明 top-k 集合保留了教师大部分与决策相关的信息。此外发现将截断的 top-k 教师分数当作完整 60 类软目标用于 KL 蒸馏会破坏软标签蒸馏所依赖的"暗知识"：把未截断类别置零引入系统性偏差，而非无害的稀疏近似。

Conclusion: 学生引导的教师蒸馏可将零样本路由成本从线性降为小规模候选重排，且兼顾精度与效率。但完整评估仍在进行中，包括多 k 下的留出集覆盖率、嵌入检索基线，以及更大规模的人工复核测试集，因此现有结论应视为初步结果。

Abstract: Zero-shot classifiers are useful for routing user requests to specialized LLM tasks, but scoring every request against a large candidate set is expensive: a zero-shot NLI classifier must evaluate one premise-hypothesis pair per label, so cost scales linearly with taxonomy size. We study a student-guided teacher distillation pipeline for a fixed taxonomy of 60 LLM task categories: a compact ModernBERT classifier predicts the full category distribution in one forward pass and retrieves a small top-k candidate set, and a larger DeBERTa-v3 zero-shot NLI classifier reranks only those candidates rather than all 60 labels; the resulting teacher labels iteratively improve the student, which produces sharper candidates for the next round. Unlike generic embedding retrieval or clustering-derived shortlists used in extreme multi-label classification, our candidate generator is trained end-to-end on the target taxonomy and is the same model serving production traffic, distinguishing it from LLM-routing work that routes between candidate models, and from concurrent System-1 encoder-classifier proposals (e.g. TypeSafe AI's Jev and the open-source Laya project) whose training methodology is undocumented or RL-based. Our best student checkpoint reaches 77.5% teacher agreement on a 200-example evaluation set, and preliminary coverage measurements show Coverage@16 of 91-100%, suggesting top-k sets retain most of the teacher's decision-relevant information. We further show truncated top-k teacher scores should not be treated as full 60-class soft targets for KL distillation: zeroing untruncated classes destroys the dark knowledge soft-label distillation depends on, introducing systematic bias rather than a harmless sparse approximation. A complete evaluation, including coverage at multiple k on a held-out set, an embedding-retrieval baseline, and a larger human-reviewed test set, remains in progress.

</details>


### [52] [Learning the Latent Structure: A Feature-Centric Approach to Graph Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02517)
*Yu Song,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Bingheng Li,Jingzhe Liu,Bo Long,Jiliang Tang,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以特征为中心的自监督图数据增强框架SelfAug，直接在嵌入空间操作而无需显式结构建模，通过逆掩码过程恢复未观测的结构信号，在归纳与冷启动场景下兼具准确性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界图常因数据采集与观测限制而不完整，严重制约图学习效果。现有图数据增强（GDA）方法多依赖标签、计算开销大且本质上是传导式（transductive），难以适用于实际场景。

Method: 提出以特征为中心的图数据增强框架，绕过显式结构建模，直接在嵌入空间中操作；通过自监督逆掩码（inverse masking）过程捕捉观测图与完整图之间的潜在关联，借助精化后的节点表示恢复未观测的结构信号；并引入消息正则化器与自举（bootstrap）策略以提升在噪声与稀疏监督下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Result: 在涵盖多个领域的十个图数据集上评估，SelfAug 在归纳（inductive）与冷启动（cold-start）设置下的准确性和效率均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SelfAug 可作为面向真实世界图学习场景的可扩展、可泛化的解决方案，具备良好应用潜力。

Abstract: Graph-structured data plays a pivotal role in modeling complex relationships. However, real-world graphs are often incomplete due to data collection and observational constraints, severely limiting the effectiveness of modern graph learning pipelines. While existing Graph Data Augmentation (GDA) methods attempt to refine graph structures for improved downstream performance, they are typically label-dependent, computationally expensive, and inherently transductive, limiting their applicability in practical scenarios. In this work, we present a novel feature-centric graph data augmentation framework that bypasses explicit structure modeling by operating directly in the embedding space. Through a self-supervised inverse masking process, our method captures latent ties between observed and complete graphs, enabling recovery of unobserved structural signals through refined node representations. To enhance robustness under noisy and sparse supervision, we introduce a message regularizer and a bootstrap strategy for effective training and generalization. Evaluated on ten graph datasets spanning multiple domains, our approach, SelfAug, consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and efficiency across inductive and cold-start settings, highlighting its potential as a scalable and generalizable solution for real-world graph learning scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [Instance-Dependent Regret for CMDPs with Step-Wise Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02520)
*Qian Zuo,Francesco Emanuele Stradi,Leyang Xue,Sattar Vakili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带逐步安全约束的episodic表格型约束MDP在线学习，提出SVAE算法，先学习候选安全子图，再在其内做方差自适应乐观规划，实现近乎最优的累积遗憾与约束违反界，并给出下界证明其依赖不可避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带逐步安全约束的约束MDP中，约束会诱导出一个安全子图，该结构决定了可行策略下累积回报的方差，从而影响学习难度。然而利用这一结构需要同时学习哪些动作安全并控制约束违反，现有方法未能充分刻画这一方差自适应结构。

Method: 提出Safe Variance-Adaptive Exploration (SVAE)算法：学习候选安全子图，并在其中进行方差自适应的乐观规划，兼顾安全性探索与效率。

Result: SVAE以高概率达到累积遗憾 \widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{SAH\min{\mathbb{V}_\Sigma,K\mathrm{Var}^{\star}}}+S\sqrt{AH^3\min{K,\mathcal{C}}}+S^2AH^2)（K个episode），并达到 \widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H\sqrt{SAK}+S^2AH^2) 的逐步约束违反界及gap依赖性、对K多对数级的违反界；同时给出下界，表明对这些实例相关量的依赖不可避免。

Conclusion: SVAE在带逐步安全约束的约束MDP中实现了方差自适应的近优学习，同时在遗憾与约束违反上均有理论保证，且下界证明所依赖的实例相关量是不可或缺的。

Abstract: We study online learning in episodic tabular constrained Markov decision processes with step-wise safety constraints. In such a setting, the constraints induce a safe subgraph that shapes the variance of cumulative rewards under feasible policies and, consequently, the difficulty of learning. Exploiting this structure, however, requires learning which actions are safe while controlling constraint violations. We propose Safe Variance-Adaptive Exploration (SVAE), an efficient algorithm that learns candidate safe subgraphs and performs variance-adaptive optimistic planning within them. With high probability, SVAE achieves cumulative regret of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{SAH\min\{\mathbb{V}_Σ,K\mathrm{Var}^{\star}\}}+S\sqrt{AH^3\min\{K,\mathcal{C}\}}+S^2AH^2)$ over $K$ episodes, where $H$ is the horizon of a single episode, while $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, respectively. Here, $\mathrm{Var}^{\star}$ is the maximum return variance among safe policies, $\mathbb{V}_Σ$ is the variance accumulated before the first unsafe action is encountered, and $\mathcal{C}$ captures the statistical complexity of eliminating actions incorrectly considered potentially safe. SVAE additionally attains $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H\sqrt{SAK}+S^2AH^2)$ step-wise constraint violation and a gap-dependent violation bound that is polylogarithmic in $K$. Finally, we establish a lower bound showing that dependence on these instance-specific quantities is unavoidable.

</details>


### [54] [BaCP: Backbone Contrastive Pruning for Preserving Representations in Extremely Sparse Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02524)
*Mohammad Haroon Khawaja,Muhammad Haseeb,Mohammad Fatim Shoaib,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究骨干对比剪枝（BaCP），通过在极端稀疏下对齐稀疏网络与预训练、微调及历史快照模型的嵌入空间，缓解表征崩塌并显著提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端稀疏下的非结构化剪枝常导致表征崩塌，使精度急剧下降。作者希望通过对稀疏网络嵌入空间进行正则化来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出 Backbone Contrastive Pruning（BaCP），利用与预训练模型、微调模型和历史快照模型的对齐来正则化稀疏网络嵌入空间；基于 CAP 框架（Xu 等，2022）的对比分解，在多种剪枝准则下给出严格的匹配预算刻画。

Result: 在 90 种设置下评估，BaCP 在标准剪枝失效的极端稀疏区间大幅提升精度，而在表征未受损的情况下接近基线。

Conclusion: BaCP 是一种有效缓解极端稀疏剪枝下表征崩塌的方法，在极端稀疏时收益显著，在低稀疏时保持与基线相当的表现。

Abstract: Unstructured pruning at extreme sparsity often suffers from representational collapse, causing sharp drops in accuracy. To address this, we study Backbone Contrastive Pruning (BaCP), which regularizes the sparse network's embedding space by aligning it with pretrained, fine-tuned, and historical snapshot models. Building on the contrastive decomposition of the CAP framework (Xu et al., 2022), we provide a rigorous matched-budget characterization of this approach across multiple pruning criteria. Evaluated across 90 settings, BaCP improves accuracy substantially in extreme sparsity regimes where standard pruning fails, and is close to baseline where representations remain intact.

</details>


### [55] [Autoregressive Differentiable Method for Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02528)
*Ouns El Harzli,Yudong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自回归可微分方法求解0-1整数规划，通过Transformer在可行域内逐位预测二进制变量，并结合拉格朗日惩罚与Gumbel-softmax在松弛目标上继续训练，在密集二次背包问题上优于开源求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0-1整数规划（尤其是非凸问题）求解困难，现有求解器在密集、大规模问题上表现受限。作者希望利用深度生成模型的可微性，在保持可行性的前提下更高效地探索可行域，并借助变量变换跨越松弛目标的局部障碍。

Method: 1) 固定二进制变量的顺序，训练Transformer逐位预测下一位并始终保持在可行域内；2) 先用任意求解器提供的可行解（incumbent）进行训练，使Transformer初始化解落在可行域中；3) 引入拉格朗日惩罚项惩罚不可行解，并通过Gumbel-softmax激活在松弛目标上进一步训练，使模型探索可行域。

Result: 在非凸二次背包问题（QKP）实例上测试，对密度较高、最多含10,000个二进制变量的问题，相较最先进的开源求解器取得了一致的改进。实验还观察到类似隧穿效应的现象：从二进制变量到Transformer连续权重的有效变量替换，使方法能够跨越松弛目标景观中的势垒。

Conclusion: 该方法将自回归生成建模与可微分优化、拉格朗日惩罚相结合，为0-1整数规划提供了一种可行的连续化求解范式，并展现出在大规模密集非凸问题上的潜力与跨越局部障碍的能力。

Abstract: We introduce an autoregressive differentiable method to solve 0-1 integer programs. We fix an arbitrary order of the binary variables and we train a transformer to predict the next bit while remaining in the feasible set. Our method is first trained on feasible incumbents provided by any solver, thus allowing us to initialize the transformer in the feasible set. Our procedure then implements a Lagrangian penalty to penalize infeasible solutions, and the transformer is further trained to explore the feasible set using Gumbel-softmax activations on the relaxed objective. We have tested our method on non-convex instances of quadratic knapsack problem and demonstrated consistent improvement upon state-of-the-art open-source solvers for dense problems up to 10,000 binary variables. In particular, we empirically demonstrate a phenomenon akin to a tunneling effect where the effective change of variables from binary variable to the continuous weights of the transformer that the method implements enables crossing barriers in the relaxed objective landscape.

</details>


### [56] [Reward Inflation: A Healthy Stimulus for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02545)
*Ganghun Lee,Minji Kim,Minsu Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"奖励膨胀"（reward inflation），即在训练过程中逐步放大奖励幅度，证明它可作为强化学习的正向刺激，理论上带来隐式近期加权、抑制休眠神经元并保持可塑性，实验在ALE和MuJoCo上验证有效，并提出自适应变体Fed。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中奖励通常作为主要学习信号，但其幅度在训练全程被固定，奖励的时间调制（随时间改变奖励规模）尚未被充分探索。作者希望探究对奖励进行时间上的缩放是否能成为有益的训练刺激。

Method: 提出reward inflation：在训练过程中逐步缩放（放大）奖励幅度。理论上分析其作用机制，并进一步提出自适应变体Fed，可根据训练情况动态调整膨胀水平。

Result: 理论分析表明：奖励膨胀产生隐式近期加权，使策略更新时更重视近期经验，从而加快适应；同时通过维持梯度信号（在策略饱和时）抑制休眠神经元的出现，帮助保持可塑性。在ALE游戏和MuJoCo任务上的实验验证了这些发现，适当的奖励膨胀对广泛任务有益，且Fed往往优于固定膨胀。

Conclusion: 奖励的时间调制（如奖励膨胀）是被忽视但有效的训练手段：适度膨胀能加快适应并保持可塑性，自适应方案Fed通常表现更佳。

Abstract: Reward serves as the primary learning signal in reinforcement learning (RL). However, while reward magnitudes are typically held fixed throughout training, their temporal modulation remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose reward inflation, a gradual scaling of rewards over the course of training, and show that it can act as a healthy stimulus for RL. Theoretically, reward inflation induces an implicit recency weighting that upweights recent transitions during policy updates, enabling faster adaptation. We further show that, by sustaining gradient signals as the policy saturates, reward inflation suppresses the emergence of dormant neurons and helps preserve plasticity. Empirical results on ALE games and MuJoCo tasks corroborate these findings, showing that an appropriate level of reward inflation benefits a broad range of tasks. Finally, we introduce Fed, an adaptive variant that adjusts the inflation level on the fly, and find that it often improves upon fixed inflation.

</details>


### [57] [Test-time Multi-agent Coordination by Decomposed Value Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02554)
*Dongsu Lee,Haoran Xu,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOUT是首个将生成式基础模型与学习价值函数通过测试时动作精炼结合的离线多智能体强化学习框架，在多个基准上取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MARL存在持续的权衡：表达性生成策略能表示数据中的多模态协调，但无法区分高价值区域；而价值优化策略能利用学到的Q函数，却会将多模态坍缩为单一主导模式。单智能体的模式坍缩会破坏联合协调，多智能体同时漂移会将联合策略推向未见过的动作空间区域。

Method: 提出SCOUT，训练两个解耦组件：流匹配行为先验和分解价值函数。测试时通过Stein变分梯度下降将行为样本向高价值区域传输，传输步数控制自适应测试时缩放，替代固定正则化系数。

Result: 在IGM原则下证明了联合软价值差距的单一项KL界，该界随传输收敛而消失，并存在与IGM违反程度成正比且不可约的加性残差。实验上，SCOUT在离散和连续离线MARL基准上取得最佳平均性能，并在所有离线到在线配置中均有性能提升。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过测试时动作精炼有效结合生成先验与价值优化，缓解了离线MARL中表达性与价值优化的权衡问题，理论与实验均验证其有效性。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces a persistent trade-off. Expressive generative policies can represent multi-modal coordination in the data, but cannot distinguish high-value regions, while value-optimized policies exploit the learned Q-function but collapse the multi-modal into a single dominant mode. A single agent's mode collapse can break joint coordination, and simultaneous drift across agents can push the joint policy into unseen regions of the action space. We propose scalable coordination via optimal unified transport (SCOUT), the first offline MARL framework to combine a generative foundation model with a learned value function through test-time action refinement. SCOUT trains two decoupled components: a flow-matching behavioral prior and a decomposed value function. At test-time, it transports behavioral samples toward high-value regions via Stein variational gradient descent. The number of transport steps controls adaptive test-time scaling, replacing a fixed regularization coefficient. Under the individual-global-max (IGM) principle, we prove a single-term KL bound on the joint soft-value gap that vanishes as transport converges, with an irreducible additive residual proportional to the IGM violation. Empirically, SCOUT achieves the best average performance across discrete and continuous offline MARL benchmarks and yields performance improvements in all offline-to-online configurations.

</details>


### [58] [Neuron merging via inverse-activation regression for post-training compression of sigmoid neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02559)
*Ao Kuniya,Jun Ohkubo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于聚类的神经元合并方法，用于训练后神经网络的压缩，通过将神经元响应映射回预激活空间，并用最小二乘法估计代表神经元的权重和偏置，实现了结构化剪枝式的合并压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着神经网络规模不断扩大，在有限计算资源下进行高效推理变得愈发重要。结构化剪枝方法会移除被认为不重要的神经元或通道，但被移除的单元可能仍包含有用信息。从对已训练网络进行粗粒化的视角出发，当多个神经元的自由度被合并时，哪些信息应当被保留是一个有价值的问题。

Method: 本文讨论了用于压缩已训练神经网络的基于聚类的合并方法。除了数据无关的贡献加权平均法外，作者提出了神经元合并方法：通过逆激活函数将神经元响应映射回预激活空间，并使用最小二乘法估计每个代表神经元的权重和偏置。同时考察了两种策略：使用真实训练输入的数据辅助策略，以及使用随机生成输入的数据无关策略。

Result: 在所测试的 sigmoid 网络上，比较结果提供了经验证据：权重信息对聚类特别有用，而激活信息对合并过程中代表神经元的重建有用。

Conclusion: 聚类式神经元合并是一种有效的网络压缩思路。在合并过程中，应区分利用不同信息：聚类阶段侧重权重信息，代表神经元重建阶段侧重激活信息。

Abstract: As neural networks continue to grow in scale, model compression is becoming increasingly important for efficient inference under limited computational resources. Structured pruning methods remove neurons or channels that are estimated to be less important, but the removed units may still contain useful information. From the viewpoint of coarse-graining a trained network, it is valuable to ask which information should be retained when multiple neuronal degrees of freedom are consolidated. In this paper, we discuss cluster-based merging methods for compression of trained neural networks. In addition to a data-free contribution-weighted averaging method, we propose neuron-merging methods in which neuron responses are mapped back to the pre-activation space via the inverse activation function, and the weights and biases of each representative neuron are estimated using the least-squares method. We also examine both a data-assisted strategy with actual training inputs and a data-free strategy using randomly generated inputs. The comparisons provide empirical evidence, in the tested sigmoid networks, that weight information is particularly useful for clustering whereas activation information is useful for representative-neuron reconstruction in the merging process.

</details>


### [59] [OpenGameEval: Benchmarking Agentic Programming and Exploration in a Stateful Game Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02563)
*Eray Turkel,Mengsha Sun,Kartik Ayyar,Sean Dunigan,Jack Lu,Vlad Shcherban,Hsiang-Shun Shih,Xin Wang,Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenGameEval，一个在 Roblox Studio 内评估智能体游戏开发的基准与评测框架，通过可复现的有状态游戏引擎会话和可执行检查打分，并发现探索行为能显著预测任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数智能体编程基准需要探索，却只对最终任务成功进行评分，无法直接衡量探索行为本身。因此需要一个能区分观察工具与编辑工具、从而直接度量探索的评测框架。

Method: OpenGameEval 让语言模型作为智能体在可复现、有状态的游戏引擎会话中运行，用可执行检查对编辑后场景和模拟游玩会话进行评分；其八工具动作空间将观察工具与编辑工具分离，从而可直接测量探索行为。研究在 84 个人工筛选的核心任务上，以每任务 16 次尝试，测量 13 个前沿模型的通过率与探索行为。

Result: 任务对当前模型较难：最佳模型单次尝试解决 51.7% 的任务，五次全过率 39.4%，且有六个任务无任何模型解决。按任务类型拆分后，前五名模型在脚本编写任务上相差 5.0pp，在场景修改任务上相差 12.5pp。探索行为可预测成功：在固定任务与模型时，行动前检查参考解所触及全部对象的运行，在纯场景任务上通过率高 13.4pp，在纯脚本任务上高 9.8pp。

Conclusion: OpenGameEval 提供了一个可复现、可执行验证的智能体游戏开发评测方案，揭示探索行为与任务成功之间的显著关联。作者以 MIT 许可证发布了任务集、place 文件、逐任务标注、Roblox Studio 插件及更新的排行榜。

Abstract: We present OpenGameEval, a benchmark and evaluation framework for agentic game development inside Roblox Studio. It runs language models as agents in reproducible, stateful game-engine sessions and scores each run with executable checks, both on the edited scene and in a simulated play session. Most agentic coding benchmarks require exploration but score only final task success. OpenGameEval separates observation tools from editing tools in its eight-tool action space, so exploration can be measured directly. We measure the pass rates and exploration behavior of 13 frontier models on 84 human-curated core tasks, with 16 attempts per task.
  The tasks are hard for current models. The best model solves 51.7% of tasks on a single attempt and 39.4% five times out of five, and no tested model solves six of the tasks. Models at the frontier reach similar pass rates by solving different tasks: splitting tasks by the kind of work they require spreads the top five by 5.0pp on script-authoring tasks and 12.5pp on scene-change tasks.
  Exploration behavior predicts whether a run succeeds. Holding task and model fixed, a run that inspects every object a reference solution touches before acting on it passes 13.4pp more often than a run that inspects none of them on scene-only tasks, and 9.8pp more often on script-only tasks.
  We release the task suite, its place files, the per-task annotations, a plugin that runs the tasks inside Roblox Studio, and an updated leaderboard under the MIT license at https://github.com/Roblox/open-game-eval.

</details>


### [60] [DISSOLVR: An Interpretable and Fast Framework for Aqueous and Organic Solubility Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02574)
*Vansh Ramani,Har Ashish Arora,Dhairya Kuchhal,Sayan Ranu,Tarak Karmakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出DISSOLVR，一个透明且可解释的分子溶解度预测框架，旨在证明无需依赖不透明的深度学习架构也能达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真溶解度预测对药物开发和环境分配至关重要，需将分子结构与热力学行为耦合。然而当前进展多由深度学习主导，常以牺牲物理可解释性换取预测能力，作者希望挑战这一趋势。

Method: 引入透明框架DISSOLVR，通过将分子映射到物理基础描述符实现结构不变性；开展全面文献综述与基准测试；并设计LLM辅助的事后解释流程，将符号化模型产物与化学叙事相衔接。

Result: DISSOLVR接近实验不确定性的随机极限，并通过结构不变性实现分布外泛化；在涉及22位专家化学家的调研基准中，专家评估者提供了深入见解。

Conclusion: 实现最先进性能并不需要不透明的深层架构，透明、物理可解释的框架同样能兼顾精度与泛化，并可借助LLM提升可解释性。

Abstract: High-fidelity solubility prediction is fundamental to pharmaceutical development and environmental partitioning, where accurate modeling must couple molecular structure with thermodynamic behavior across diverse chemical environments. However, recent advancements have been dominated by deep learning architectures that often sacrifice physical interpretability for predictive power. We challenge this trend by showing that state-of-the-art performance does not require such non-transparent architectures. To address this, we introduce DISSOLVR, a transparent framework for molecular solubility prediction. In addition, we perform a comprehensive literature review and a benchmarking study against various methods. We show that DISSOLVR approaches the aleatoric limit of experimental uncertainty and achieves OOD generalization through structural invariance, derived by mapping molecules to physically-grounded descriptors. Then, we present an LLM-assisted post-hoc explanation pipeline that bridges the gap between symbolic model artifacts and chemically grounded narratives. Finally, a comparative benchmark of a survey involving 22 expert chemists reveals that expert evaluators provide deep insights.

</details>


### [61] [Dense Mixture-of-Experts as a Reparameterized Wide FFN: A Granularity Sweep at Fixed Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02584)
*Vu Quang Hoang,Nghia Hieu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定总FFN宽度下，用密集MoE类比（K个全激活SwiGLU专家+softmax门控）研究动态专家组合的贡献，发现验证损失随K非单调变化，K=2最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sparse MoE模型将学习路由与从大专家池中选择相结合，但动态专家组合本身（而非更大专家池或离散选择）的相对贡献尚不清晰，需要隔离研究。

Method: 构建密集类比模型：K个SwiGLU专家，每个token全部激活，由softmax门控组合，总FFN宽度固定；K=1即密集基线。通过改变K（1,2,4,6）测试验证损失，并分析路由软硬程度与专家使用均衡性。进一步证明该结构等价于带token依赖、单纯形约束组缩放的密集SwiGLU，且其函数类包含密集基线。

Result: 验证损失随K非单调：K=2比基线改善0.0048；K=4和K=6分别恶化0.0053和0.0197。路由总体保持软且专家使用均衡，唯K=4首层路由接近one-hot；强制该门控均匀会在诊断子集上增加2.4 nats损失，说明其集中路由具有功能重要性。

Conclusion: 在固定宽度、全激活软门控FFN中存在粒度最优区间，所测配置中K=2表现最佳（基于单次运行结果）。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models combine learned routing with selection from a large expert pool. We isolate the contribution of dynamic expert combination using a dense analogue: $K$ SwiGLU experts, all active for every token and combined by a softmax gate, at fixed total FFN width. With no larger pool or discrete selection, the dense baseline is the $K=1$ case. Validation loss varies non-monotonically with $K$: $K=2$ improves over the baseline by $0.0048$, whereas $K=4$ and $K=6$ worsen it by $0.0053$ and $0.0197$, respectively. Routing generally remains soft and expert usage balanced, except in the first layer of the $K=4$ model, where routing is nearly one-hot. Forcing this gate to uniform increases loss by $2.4$ nats on a diagnostic subset, indicating that its concentrated routing is functionally important. We further show that the architecture is exactly a dense SwiGLU with token-dependent, simplex-constrained group scaling and contains the dense baseline in its function class. These single-run results suggest a granularity sweet spot for always-active, softly gated FFNs at fixed width, with $K=2$ performing best among the configurations tested.

</details>


### [62] [Fisher-Guided Submodular Data Selection for Continual Pre-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02593)
*Zhenghao Zhao,Gaowen Liu,Zhiling Lan,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种Fisher感知的持续预训练数据选择器，通过将候选样本梯度分解为锚定分量与前沿分量，并用对数行列式次模目标优化，从而在提升目标域质量的同时抑制对预训练能力的遗忘，且具有10倍token效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型训练中，网络语料噪声大、token预算有限，数据选择已成为核心瓶颈。在持续预训练（CPT）中，该问题更变成遗忘控制问题：选择不当的目标域语料会覆盖预训练检查点中已编码的能力。现有CPT实践要么用困惑度等与参数无关的标量对候选打分，要么通过消耗大量额外通用域回放token来缓解遗忘，二者都未直接考察对候选样本训练将如何移动模型参数。

Method: 作者发现基于损失的选择会使CPT后的Fisher对角线恰好在预训练模型已承诺的高Fisher坐标上向下漂移，而低Fisher坐标基本不受影响，这一不对称性揭示了灾难性遗忘的参数空间机制。基于此，提出Fisher感知的CPT选择器，将每个候选样本的梯度分解为：锚定分量（衡量沿已承诺参数方向的扰动）和前沿分量（衡量在未约束的低Fisher子空间中的更新容量）。利用对数行列式次模目标聚合这些信号，并通过可扩展的流式数据选择流水线在单遍中优化。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3.1-8B上针对医学数据进行CPT，该选择器提升了目标域质量，同时将留出预训练基准上的遗忘限制在可控范围内。最重要的是，它比遗忘感知回放显著更省token：仅用1B个选定token就在适应性和遗忘两方面均优于用10B token训练的�回放策略，带来10倍token效率优势。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示并利用了CPT中遗忘的参数空间不对称机制，提出Fisher感知的数据选择方法，在提升目标域适应性的同时有效控制遗忘，并实现了显著的token效率提升。

Abstract: Data selection is already a central bottleneck in large-language-model training, where web-scale corpora are noisy and token budgets are finite. In continual pre-training (CPT), it becomes a forgetting-control problem: a poorly chosen target-domain corpus can overwrite capabilities encoded in the pretrained checkpoint. Existing CPT practice either scores candidates with parameter-agnostic scalars such as perplexity, or mitigates forgetting by spending many extra general-domain replay tokens. Neither strategy directly asks how training on a candidate will move the model parameters. We show that loss-based selection causes the post-CPT Fisher diagonal to drift downward on exactly the high-Fisher coordinates the pretrained model had committed to, while leaving low-Fisher coordinates largely untouched. This asymmetry exposes a parameter-space mechanism for catastrophic forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we propose a Fisher-aware CPT selector that decomposes each candidate's gradient into an anchor component, which measures perturbation along committed parameter directions, and a frontier component, which measures update capacity in unconstrained low-Fisher subspaces. We aggregate these signals with a log-determinant submodular objective and optimize it in a single pass using a scalable streaming data selection pipeline. On TinyLlama-1.1B and Llama-3.1-8B CPT over medical data, our selector improves target-domain quality while bounding forgetting on held-out pretraining benchmarks. Most importantly, it is substantially more token-efficient than forgetting-aware replay. 1B selected tokens already outperform the replay strategy trained with 10B tokens on both adaptation and forgetting, giving a 10x token-efficiency advantage.

</details>


### [63] [How Causality Bridges the Semantic Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02594)
*Shuhao Zhang,Xuran Zhou,Han Guo,Pengtao Xie,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CausalBridge 框架，利用因果结构而非人类通用知识，为未命名变量（含观测与潜变量）赋予语义。它从测量数据中发现因果图，在图的依赖关系约束下求解变量嵌入，并用语言模型转成名称。在五个问卷和三个机器人场景（变量名掩蔽 20–90%）上，其语义恢复比基于关联的方法更准，且文档越少优势越大，命名成本与耗时远低于采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值测量刻画了系统的行为，却常未说明变量含义：一些变量被测量却无标签，另一些从未被测量。现有方法借助通用人类知识赋予语义，既继承了人类知识本身的偏见，在知识缺失处又无能为力。作者希望用因果结构弥合"测量"与"含义"之间的鸿沟，从变量对其他变量的作用方式读出其语义，因为因果结构刻画了生成数据的机制、仅由测量本身恢复，可能是唯一不受人类知识偏见影响的信息来源。

Method: 将任务形式化为"结构约束的语义对齐"：在因果图所暗示的依赖关系约束下求解每个未命名变量的嵌入，并以少量已知名称的嵌入作为锚点。据此构建 CausalBridge 框架：先从测量数据中发现因果图（含潜变量），再在这些关系约束下求解嵌入，最后通过语言模型将其表达为名称。

Result: 在五个问卷和三个机器人场景上评估，掩蔽 20–90% 的变量名：对观测变量与潜变量的语义恢复比依赖关联的现有方法更准确，且系统文档越少、领先幅度越大；其发现的因果图命名变量的准确率与人工记录的图相当；命名一个新系统只需数分钟，成本仅为采样方法的很小一部分。

Conclusion: 一旦语义鸿沟被忠实弥合，机器便能理解世界并以因果方式采取行动。因果结构可作为不受人类知识偏见影响的语义来源，为未命名变量（含潜变量）赋予含义。

Abstract: Numerical measurements capture how a system behaves, but often leave the meanings of its variables unspecified. Some variables are measured but never labeled, and others are never measured at all. Existing methods assign semantics to such variables by consulting general human knowledge, but this inherits its biases where that knowledge exists and offers nothing where it does not. We bridge this gap between measurements and their meanings with causal structure instead, reading a variable's semantics from how it acts on other variables. We formalize this as structure-constrained semantic alignment, in which the embedding of each unnamed variable is solved under the dependence relations implied by the causal graph, with the embeddings of a few known names as anchors. Accordingly, we build CausalBridge, a framework that discovers the causal graph from the measurements, latent variables included, solves for the embeddings under those relations, and expresses them as names through a language model. The causal structure reflects the mechanism that generated the measurements and is recovered from the measurements alone, which may make it the one source of information free of bias from human knowledge. We evaluate CausalBridge on five questionnaires and three robotics scenarios, with 20 to 90% of the variable names masked. It recovers the semantics of observed and latent variables more accurately than existing methods that rely on association, and its lead widens as less of the system is documented. The graph it discovers names variables as accurately as the documented one, and a new system is named in minutes and at a fraction of the cost of sampling methods. Once the semantic gap is bridged faithfully, machines can understand the world and take actions causally.

</details>


### [64] [Activation Sparsity with Weight Approximation for Faster LLM Decoding on Offloaded Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02598)
*JuneHyung Kim,Sankeerth Durvasula,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpAx通过将激活稀疏中权重读取的二元选择改为“保留、压缩近似、省略”三种选项，改善显存不足时大模型推理的质量与速度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级GPU显存不足会导致LLM推理极慢，因为解码需反复以低带宽从系统RAM或闪存向GPU传输卸载的权重。激活稀疏可减少传输，但过度省略激活贡献会使模型质量急剧下降，说明小幅度激活对应的权重对质量影响敏感。因此需要改善利用激活稀疏时质量与解码性能的权衡。

Method: 核心思想是把“是否读取某个权重”的二元选择替换为三种选项：完全保留、用压缩权重表示近似、或完全省略。SpAx对最接近零的激活跳过对应权重，对较小幅度激活读取近似权重，对最大幅度激活读取原始权重。较小激活会衰减近似权重引入的误差，而压缩权重表示所需传输字节更少。

Result: 权重卸载到CPU内存时，16位权重平均加速3.86倍（最高5.57倍），4位权重平均加速2.06倍（最高2.74倍），WikiText-2困惑度增加至多10%。权重卸载到闪存时，加速分别为平均3.31倍（最高4.81倍）和1.54倍（最高2.03倍）。

Conclusion: SpAx通过三级权重读取策略，在控制模型质量下降的前提下显著提升显存不足场景下的解码速度，适用于CPU内存和闪存卸载。

Abstract: Deploying LLMs on consumer-grade GPUs with insufficient memory to hold their weights can result in prohibitively slow inference, because decoding repeatedly transfers offloaded weights from system RAM or flash storage into GPU at much lower bandwidth than local GPU-memory access. Activation sparsity reduces these transfers by skipping weights associated with zero or near-zero activations. However, as more activation contributions are omitted, model quality eventually degrades rapidly, indicating that weights associated with small-magnitude activations collectively influence model quality sharply. In this work, we improve the trade-off between model quality and decoding performance when exploiting activation sparsity. Our key idea is to replace the binary choice of whether or not to read a weight with three options: fully retain it, approximate it using a compressed weight representation, or omit it entirely. SpAx skips weights associated with activations closest to zero, reads approximate weights for smaller-magnitude activations, and reads original weights for the largest-magnitude activations. Smaller-magnitude activations attenuate the errors introduced by approximate weights, while compressed weight representations require fewer bytes to be transferred. With weights offloaded to CPU memory, SpAx speeds up decoding by 3.86X on average (up to 5.57X) with 16-bit weights and 2.06X (up to 2.74X) with 4-bit weights, at a WikiText-2 perplexity increase of at most 10%. With weights offloaded to flash storage, the speedups are 3.31X on average (up to 4.81X) and 1.54X (up to 2.03X).

</details>


### [65] [Seer: Maximum Likelihood Regression for Learning-Speed Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02610)
*Carl Myers Kadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Seer 系统，通过实证观测分类学习性能并建立统计模型，用于预测达到目标精度所需训练样本数以及无限样本下的最大精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习性能建模需要兼顾理论约束与实用性，作者希望构建能刻画分类学习性能并可用于预测的统计模型。

Method: 先界定最大似然分类学习性能模型的要求，探索合理设计选择；将非线性规划问题利用约束转化为非线性回归问题，并用高效迭代算法求解；在大豆病害、心脏病和听力学三个领域开展近 100 个实验。

Result: 实验表明 Seer 能很好地刻画学习性能，并且在预测学习性能方面表现优异，接近最佳水平。

Conclusion: 论文给出了针对具体情境选择回归模型的建议，并指出了后续研究方向。

Abstract: The research presented here focuses on modeling machine-learning performance. The thesis introduces Seer, a system that generates empirical observations of classification-learning performance and then uses those observations to create statistical models. The models can be used to predict the number of training examples needed to achieve a desired level and the maximum accuracy possible given an unlimited number of training examples. Seer advances the state of the art with 1) models that embody the best constraints for classification learning and most useful parameters, 2) algorithms that efficiently find maximum-likelihood models, and 3) a demonstration on real-world data from three domains of a practicable application of such modeling.
  The first part of the thesis gives an overview of the requirements for a good maximum-likelihood model of classification-learning performance. Next, reasonable design choices for such models are explored. Selection among such models is a task of nonlinear programming, but by exploiting appropriate problem constraints, the task is reduced to a nonlinear regression task that can be solved with an efficient iterative algorithm. The latter part of the thesis describes almost 100 experiments in the domains of soybean disease, heart disease, and audiological problems. The tests show that Seer is excellent at characterizing learning-performance and that it seems to be as good as possible at predicting learning performance. Finally, recommendations for choosing a regression model for a particular situation are made and directions for further research are identified.

</details>


### [66] [Scale-Recursive Rectified Flows for Few-Step Precipitation Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02611)
*Shunya Nagashima,Takumi Bannai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种尺度递归整流流模型，用多步采样生成高分辨率降水集合，并通过按空间尺度分配采样步数提升集合变异性与概率精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗分辨率卫星降水产品无法刻画网格内的降雨细节，而生成式模型虽可产生高分辨率降雨场的集合以表达这种不确定性，但整流流等模型在减少采样步数加速生成时，集合成员易趋同，导致不确定性被低估。

Method: 提出尺度递归整流流（scale-recursive rectified flow）：先生成广域大尺度格局，再生成局部细节；通过比较各空间尺度上的集合变异度与预测误差来指导采样步数分配，并用验证分数和降雨功率谱约束该分配，避免过度放大。

Result: 在美国本土的卫星到雷达降尺度任务中，分析发现大尺度降雨格局是采样预算缩减下集合变异不足的主要来源；在固定架构与计算成本下，将更多步数分配给粗尺度流，跨多个训练随机种子提升了概率精度和降雨检测能力；相比使用更多步数的非递归流，所提模型以更短采样时间取得了更好的概率精度。

Conclusion: 按空间尺度递归生成并差异化分配采样步数，可在不增加计算成本的前提下缓解整流流集合变异不足问题，提升高分辨率降水降尺度的概率精度与采样效率。

Abstract: Fine-resolution precipitation estimates support flood risk assessment and water management, but coarse satellite products cannot resolve rainfall within each grid cell. Generative models address this ambiguity by producing ensembles of plausible high-resolution rainfall fields. Among these models, rectified flows generate samples by iteratively transforming random noise into rainfall fields. Reducing the number of sampling steps accelerates generation but can make ensemble members too similar, understating uncertainty. We propose a scale-recursive rectified flow that generates broad patterns before local details and guides sampling-step allocation by comparing ensemble variability with prediction error across spatial scales. Validation scores and rainfall power spectra constrain the allocation to avoid excessive amplification. In satellite-to-radar downscaling over the contiguous United States, our analysis identified broad rainfall patterns as the main source of insufficient ensemble variability under reduced sampling budgets. Allocating more steps to the coarse flow improved probabilistic accuracy and rain detection across training seeds at fixed architecture and computational cost. The proposed model also achieved better probabilistic accuracy with shorter sampling time than a nonrecursive flow using more steps.

</details>


### [67] [What Is Lost in Post-Training? Default Collapse and the Loss of In-Context Steerability Across Diverse Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02614)
*Jessica Dierking,Itai Shapira,Niclas Boehmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现后训练在塑造模型默认价值观的同时，会削弱其根据上下文被引导至其他视角的能力，并提出"立场分布匹配"这一替代目标以在满足指定视角分布的前提下最大化奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型需要服务异质人群，应能根据用户和语境采取恰当的立场。既有研究多关注模型的默认行为，而忽视了其依据上下文信息进行适应（in-context steerability）的能力。

Method: 在受控实验中，将模型针对文化价值分歧的某一方进行微调，并沿训练过程评估各检查点；同时提出并分析一种替代目标——在预设的表达视角分布约束下最大化奖励，并以"立场分布匹配"作为实际实现方式。

Result: 被训练的立场在常规使用中日益占据主导，而模型识别并忠实表达对立视角的能力随之下降，即后训练损害了上下文引导能力。

Conclusion: 优先固化单一价值体系与保留服务多元利益相关者所需的技术能力之间存在张力；作者提出的立场分布匹配方法为缓解这一张力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI models serving a heterogeneous population must act on the principles appropriate to each user and context. While post-training has been shown to narrow the views large language models express, prior work has focused on default behavior rather than the ability to adapt to in-context information. We show that post-training also degrades a model's ability to be steered in-context toward perspectives it was not trained to favor. In controlled experiments, we fine-tune models toward one side of cultural-value disagreements and evaluate checkpoints throughout training. The trained side becomes increasingly dominant in ordinary use, while the ability to recognize and faithfully enact the opposing view declines. These findings point to a tension between prioritizing a single set of values and preserving the technical capacity needed to serve diverse stakeholders. Finally, we propose and analyze an alternative objective that maximizes reward subject to a prescribed distribution over expressed perspectives, and present stance-distribution matching as a practical implementation.

</details>


### [68] [Quantifying the Value of Constructive Induction, Knowledge, and Noise Filtering on Inductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02615)
*Carl M. Kadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“有效维度”（effective dimension）这一新的学习度量，用于刻画问题属性与平均学习性能之间的联系，并在多个学习系统（含反向传播）及FRINGE特征构造系统上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习研究的一个核心目标是度量、建模并理解学习问题属性如何影响平均情况下的学习性能，例如量化构造性归纳、噪声过滤和背景知识的价值。现有度量（如VC维度）多为最坏情况分析，难以经验估计，应用范围受限。

Method: 提出“有效维度”这一新学习度量。它与VC维度类似，常与问题属性呈简单线性关系；但不同的是，它可经验估计，并能做出平均情况预测，因而更广泛适用于机器学习和人类学习研究。作者在包括反向传播在内的多个学习系统上对该度量进行了演示，并将其用于预测FRINGE特征构造系统的收益。

Result: 该度量在多个学习系统（包括Backpropagation）上得到成功演示；使用该度量精确预测了FRINGE特征构造系统的收益，并发现该收益随目标概念复杂度的增加而减小。

Conclusion: 有效维度是一种可经验估计、能进行平均情况预测的学习度量，比VC维度更具广泛适用性，可将学习问题属性与学习性能有效关联，并能精确预测具体技术（如FRINGE）的收益随问题复杂度的变化趋势。

Abstract: Learning research, as one of its central goals, tries to measure, model, and understand how learning-problem properties affect average-case learning performance. For example, we would like to quantify the value of constructive induction, noise filtering, and background knowledge. This paper describes the effective dimension, a new learning measure that helps link problem properties to learning performance. Like the Vapnik-Chervonenkis (VC) dimension, the effective dimension is often in a simple linear relation with problem properties. Unlike the VC dimension, the effective dimension can be estimated empirically and makes average-case predictions. It is therefore more widely applicable to machine and human learning research. The measure is demonstrated on several learning systems including Backpropagation. Finally, the measure is used to precisely predict the benefit of using FRINGE, a feature construction system. The benefit is found to decrease as the complexity of the target concept increases.

</details>


### [69] [Online Verification of Language Model Responses Under Cost Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02632)
*Erfan Hajihashemi,Yanning Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OMVV算法，通过在线维护多个弱验证器池并自适应路由，在保证有限时间错误率的同时，以更低成本实现更高验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多步推理中，验证输出正确性至关重要，但调用强验证器（真值预言机）成本过高，无法在线每步调用。已有工作用单一弱验证器决定是否调用强验证器，但固定弱验证器难以适应查询主题与难度的动态变化，预先选择易导致成本过高或验证不准。

Method: 提出OMVV（Online Multi-Verifier Verification）算法，维护一个包含K个不同成本与性能的候选弱验证器池，通过在线分数组合器与指数权重路由策略，自适应地为每轮决策选择验证器。

Result: 在推理数据集基准上，OMVV在多种操作预算下，均比任何单一固定验证器实现更高准确率且验证成本更低。

Conclusion: OMVV在全验证器池上提供无分布假设的有限时间误接受率和误拒绝率保证，并在成本与一致性联合目标下，相对事后最优固定验证器实现次线性遗憾。实验验证其优于单一固定验证器。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed for multi-step reasoning, verifying the correctness of their outputs has become essential for maintaining reliability at scale. Verifying the correctness of large language model outputs is often done by querying a costly ground-truth oracle, which is impractical to invoke at every step in an online setting. Prior work addresses this by querying a single weak verifier on every step, and using its score to decide whether the costly strong verifier needs to be queried as well, reserving strong verification for only a small fraction of the steps. However, a single fixed weak verifier may not perform consistently well as the subject matter or difficulty of incoming queries changes over time, and committing to one in advance risks either overly costly or inaccurate verification. We introduce OMVV (Online Multi-Verifier Verification), an algorithm that maintains a pool of $K$ candidate weak verifiers with differing cost and verification performance, and adaptively routes each round's decision to a verifier selected via an online score combiner and an exponential-weights routing policy. OMVV provides a distribution-free, finite-time guarantee on false-accept and false-reject rates across the full pool of verifiers, and further achieves sublinear regret against the best fixed verifier in hindsight under a combined cost and consistency objective. Experiments on reasoning dataset benchmarks show that OMVV achieves higher accuracy at lower verification cost than any single fixed verifier, across a range of operating budgets.

</details>


### [70] [Context-Tower Conversion Preserves Generation While Freezing Retains Knowledge: Low-Budget AR-to-Diffusion Conversion of MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02657)
*Wentao Lu,Jesse Clark,Tianyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定训练协议下比较将30B MoE自回归模型转换为扩散语言模型的两种方案，发现冻结塔（cross-attention）方案远优于原地更新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有将预训练自回归模型转换为扩散语言模型的方法在训练数据量上相差约三个数量级，且从未在统一协议下进行过比较，因此难以公平评判各方案优劣。

Method: 对同一30B MoE父模型进行两种转换对比，固定语料、监督token预算、可训练参数集与评测框架：原地模型用去噪与表示对齐损失更新部分父权重；冻结塔模型通过交叉注意力条件化于父模型的冻结因果副本。另做稠密父模型实验，并进行理论分析。

Result: 10亿训练token下，冻结塔模型HumanEval pass@10为71.60，原地模型为6.19，提升11.6倍；同预算下冻结塔保留父模型95%的GSM8K与99%的MMLU-Pro分数。稠密父模型实验复现了HumanEval差距。约5亿token下，冻结上下文塔比训练它更能保留MMLU-Pro表现。评测协议会显著影响已发表的500B-token转换结果，而父模型分数波动不足三点。

Conclusion: 在测试的低预算区间，冻结塔配置比原地转换更能保留父模型的生成性能，且dLLM比较需要统一评测协议。理论分析表明两类转换在硬注意力掩码与每轮左到右提交一个位置的条件下都包含AR父模型的精确采样器，冻结会去除经由上下文状态的梯度贡献。

Abstract: Converting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model to a diffusion language model (dLLM) enables parallel generation without pretraining a new model. Published conversion methods differ by roughly three orders of magnitude in training data and have not been compared under a common protocol. We compare two conversions of the same 30B Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) parent, holding the corpus, supervised-token budget, trainable parameter set and evaluation harness fixed, each under its own training recipe. The in-place model updates a subset of the parent's weights using denoising and representation-alignment losses; the frozen-tower model instead conditions through cross-attention on a frozen causal copy of the parent. With 1B training tokens, the frozen-tower model scores 71.60 on HumanEval pass@10 against 6.19 for the in-place model, an 11.6x improvement. At the same budget it also keeps 95% of the parent's GSM8K score and 99% of its MMLU-Pro score. A dense-parent experiment reproduces the HumanEval separation. Within the two-tower design at about 500M tokens, freezing the context tower retains substantially more MMLU-Pro performance than training it, while both give similar observed HumanEval scores. Our theoretical analysis establishes that both conversion classes contain an exact sampler for the AR parent under a hard attention mask and left-to-right commitment of one position per round. Under a shared loss, freezing removes the gradient contribution through the context states. Furthermore, evaluation protocol substantially affects a published 500B-token conversion's scores in both directions across tasks, while its AR parent's scores vary by less than three points, so comparing dLLMs needs a common protocol.
