Motivation: 现有GNN解释器评测普遍采用"合理性"（plausibility）指标，即解释是否恢复预设的真值（如植入的motif）。该协议隐含假设：在该数据上训练的GNN确实依赖预期的motif。尽管已有工作质疑此假设，该做法仍被广泛使用。

Method: 首先验证该假设在若干常用基准上不成立（例如仅靠度统计即可求解任务）。为消除该混杂因素，用"编译"替代训练：提出Gracr，首个将分级模态逻辑公式翻译为GNN权重的编译器，使模型行为可由构造确定。据此可形式化定义其真值解释并精确计算，进而构建GracrBench基准，用于在该精确真值下评测解释器。

Result: 在六个任务上对十一种解释器进行实验，显示出细粒度诊断评测的有效性；尤其发现多数解释器对间接影响或同一公式的替代实现不具备鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GracrBench为图事后可解释性提供了一种新颖且严谨的评测框架。

Abstract: Explainers for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are commonly evaluated by their plausibility, i.e., how well their explanations recover a predefined ground truth, such as a motif planted in the data. This protocol implicitly assumes that a GNN trained on such data relies on the intended motif. Although prior work has questioned this assumption, plausibility remains widespread. First, we show that the assumption is violated on several widely used benchmarks, where, e.g., degree statistics alone suffice to solve the task. Then, we remove this confounder by replacing training with compilation. We achieve this by introducing $\mathsf{Gracr}$, the first compiler translating graded modal logic formulas into GNN weights, yielding models that replicate the behaviour of the corresponding formulas. Since the behaviour of the model is now known by construction, we can define its ground truth explanation formally and compute it exactly. Building on this, we introduce $\mathsf{Gracr}\mathsf{Bench}$, a benchmark of compiled GNNs for the evaluation of explainers against this exact ground truth. Experiments on eleven explainers across six tasks show its effectiveness for fine-grained diagnostic evaluation: notably, we discover that most explainers are not robust to indirect influences or alternative implementations of the same formula. These results position $\mathsf{Gracr}\mathsf{Bench}$ as a novel, rigorous evaluation setting for graph post-hoc explainability.

</details>


### [178] [An Automated and Reproducible Workflow for Crack Identification and Damage Assessment of Fusion Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03505)
*Rinkle Juneja,Viktor Reshniak,Richard K. Archibald,John W. Duggan,Gregory R. Watson,Cory D. Hauck,Gary M. Staebler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Galaxy科学工作流环境的可复现自动化工作流，用于从SEM图像中识别裂纹并定量评估核聚变材料损伤，并在钨材料数据集上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代核聚变材料研究中，辐照后显微分析是材料合格性评估的核心，但人工分析无法应对现代实验数据的体量、异质性和多分辨率特征，因此需要自动化、可复现的分析手段。

Method: 在Galaxy科学工作流环境中实现端到端工作流：处理SEM图像与实验元数据，自动识别裂纹、量化损伤，并保留中间产物与处理历史以保证可复现性；方法设计上无需针对具体图像调参，可跨钨牌号、微观组织、放大倍数和损伤状态通用。输出包括裂纹掩膜、骨架化裂纹网络、质控可视化及标量损伤描述符。

Result: 在包含114个实验、418张图像、覆盖五种钨牌号和三种微观组织状态的稀疏电子束热冲击数据集上验证了该工作流，并定义了裂纹密度描述符，可为下游机器学习预测和物理裂纹模拟提供标准化输入。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提供一个端到端、可共享、计算可移植的工作流，将实验表征、自动化图像分析、初步损伤预测与模拟引导的数据采集连接起来，用于核聚变材料研究。

Abstract: Post-exposure microscopy is central to qualification of fusion materials. However, manual analysis does not scale to the volume, heterogeneity, and multiresolution character of modern fusion-materials campaigns. To address this challenge, we present a reproducible workflow, implemented in the Galaxy scientific workflow environment, for automated crack identification and quantitative damage assessment from scanning electron microscopy images. The workflow processes SEM images and experimental metadata to identify cracks, quantify damage, and retain the intermediate products and processing history needed for reproducibility. Outputs include crack masks, skeletonized crack networks, quality-control visualizations, and scalar damage descriptors. The method is designed to operate without image-specific parameter tuning across tungsten grades, microstructures, magnifications, and damage states. We demonstrate the workflow on a sparse electron-beam thermal-shock dataset containing 418 images from 114 experiments spanning five tungsten grades and three microstructural states. We define a crack-density descriptor, which provides standardized inputs for downstream machine-learning prediction and physics-based crack simulation. These predictive components are exposed in the same Galaxy environment and are intentionally treated here as extensible workflow modules. The principal contribution is therefore an end-to-end, shareable, and computationally portable workflow that links experimental characterization, automated image analysis, preliminary damage prediction, and simulation-guided data acquisition for fusion-materials research.

</details>


### [179] [Cephalonauts One: A deep fMRI dataset for decoding naturalistic speech in the human brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03558)
*Antoine Collas,Louis Jalouzot,Géraud Ilinca,Corentin Caris,Romain Valabrègue,Ahmed Hassayoune,David Goncalves,Madeleine Hueber,Thaddée Delebarre,Julien Savatovsky,Clara Fonteneau,Charles Maussion,Bertrand Thirion,Alexis Thual*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cephalonauts One 是一个全脑 3T fMRI 数据集，记录被试聆听母语播客时的脑活动，每位被试 30 小时数据，是当前基于自然语音刺激的最深 fMRI 数据集，并提供脑解码基准与基线解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 fMRI 数据集在自然语音刺激下的数据深度不足，难以支撑深层脑解码研究，因此需要更长时长、配对齐音频与文本标注的数据资源。

Method: 3 名健康被试多次扫描，每次含 5 个 15 分钟 run，聆听母语播客；数据配对脑活动、播客音频、转录标注与派生刺激嵌入；并构建音频片段检索式脑解码基准，提供标准划分、评估指标与基线解码器。

Result: 每位被试 30 小时 fMRI 数据；解码基准可依据留出 session 的 fMRI 活动在候选片段中识别时间对齐的播客音频；缩放分析显示解码性能随训练数据量增加而持续提升。

Conclusion: 该数据集为自然语音条件下的脑解码研究提供了深度资源与可复现基准，并表明数据规模对解码性能具有持续增益。

Abstract: Cephalonauts One is a whole-brain 3 Tesla (3T) functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) dataset recorded while subjects listened to audio podcasts. Three healthy subjects underwent multiple scanning sessions, each consisting of five 15-minute runs, while listening to podcasts in their native language. With 30 hours of fMRI data per subject, the current release is the deepest available fMRI dataset using naturalistic speech stimuli. The dataset pairs brain activity with the corresponding podcast audio, transcript annotations, and derived stimulus embeddings. Furthermore, we introduce a brain decoding benchmark formulated as audio segment retrieval: given fMRI activity from a held-out session, the decoder must identify the corresponding time-aligned podcast audio segment among candidate segments. We provide standardized splits, evaluation metrics, and baseline decoders for this task. Finally, a scaling analysis shows that decoding performance improves continuously with the amount of training data per subject.

</details>


### [180] [What Should World Models Forget? Stratified Retention for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03713)
*Nishit Anand,Ramani Duraiswami,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型不满足持续学习中"稳定预测目标"的前提，主张按不变性时间尺度分层保留知识，并提出 differential retention 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习通常把对旧数据性能下降视为失败，但这一惯例源于预测目标稳定的设定。世界模型的预测目标是会变化的环境，因此过时知识应当被修正而非保留。然而概念漂移文献与语言模型时序事实性研究尚未针对世界模型提出表述，且世界模型还包含绝不可修正的知识，需要新的框架与评估方式。

Method: 作者论证持续世界模型需要按不变性时间尺度分层的保留机制：将物理规律、客体永久性等"不变量"（永不修正）与实例级事实（环境变化即修正）区分开来。并提出 differential retention 评估方法，在适应流上同时报告不变量回归测试与修正延迟，且不做聚合。

Result: 作者指出标准遗忘指标无法区分"正确修正过时知识"与"灾难性遗忘"，甚至会错误地把冻结模型排为最优；而现有物理推理基准也只评估冻结检查点，无法反映上述能力。

Conclusion: 世界模型的持续学习需重新定义"遗忘"与"保留"，采用分层保留与 differential retention 指标，分别衡量不变量稳定性与过时知识的修正速度，避免单一聚合掩盖二者的冲突。

Abstract: Continual learning treats degradation on previously seen data as evidence of failure, a convention inherited from settings with a stationary prediction target, where a correct label remains correct indefinitely. World models do not satisfy this condition. Their prediction target is the environment, which changes, so knowledge that was accurate when acquired may later become false, and discarding it is required behavior rather than a defect. Non-stationary ground truth is well studied in the concept drift literature and in the temporal factuality of language models, but has not been formulated for world models, which are distinctive in that they also encode knowledge that must never be revised. We argue that continual world models require retention stratified by invariance timescale, separating invariants such as physics and object permanence, which must never be revised, from instance-level facts that should be revised as soon as the environment changes. Standard forgetting metrics cannot distinguish a world model that has correctly revised outdated knowledge from one that has suffered catastrophic forgetting, and consequently rank a frozen model highest, while existing physical-reasoning benchmarks evaluate only frozen checkpoints. We propose differential retention, which reports invariant regression testing across the adaptation stream jointly with revision latency, without aggregation.

</details>


### [181] [Divergence controls entropy in distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03529)
*Nicolas Zucchet,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从熵的视角研究大语言模型蒸馏，证明前向KL会使学生模型熵高于教师，反向KL则降低熵，散度充当隐式熵正则化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏已成为大语言模型训练的核心手段，但其性质尚未被充分理解，尤其是学生模型熵与数据、散度之间的关系缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 从熵的角度出发，分析学生模型熵如何依赖于数据和定义蒸馏目标的散度；通过理论证明前向KL、反向KL及二者插值的熵行为，并在预训练和监督微调中做定量验证。

Result: 证明前向KL会膨胀学生熵使其高于教师，交叉熵训练作为特例给出可验证的恒等式；反向KL则降低熵直至师生差距过大；二者插值在训练早期平滑改变熵，但在收敛时突变。on-policy蒸馏的低熵来源于token级反向KL而非on-policy采样。

Conclusion: 散度充当隐式熵正则化器，在自蒸馏中作用最明显：当特权信息条件化降低熵时，最优散度超参数正是补偿这种熵降低的参数。

Abstract: Distillation has become a core primitive of large language model training, but its properties are not yet well understood. We take an entropic perspective, studying how the entropy of the student depends on the data and the divergence that define the distillation objective. We prove that forward KL inflates the entropy of the student above that of the teacher. Since cross-entropy training is a special case, this yields an identity that we verify quantitatively in pretraining and supervised finetuning. Other divergences come with no such guarantee: reverse KL deflates entropy until the gap between student and teacher gets too large, and interpolating between the two changes entropy smoothly early in training but abruptly at convergence. The lower entropy of on-policy distillation comes from token-level reverse KL, not from on-policy sampling. The divergence therefore acts as an implicit entropy regularizer, whose role is clearest in self-distillation: as conditioning on privileged information deflates entropy, the divergence hyperparameters that work best are those that compensate for it.

</details>


### [182] [ZeroMAG: Zero-Shot Multimodal Adapter Generation for Plug-and-Play EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03546)
*Yubo Wang,Jingying Ma,Xinliang Zhou,Yangxuan Zhou,Jiquan Wang,Sha Zhao,Yiyuan Yang,Yi Ding,Ziyu Jia,Chenyu Liu,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeroMAG是一种零样本多模态适配器生成框架，可在无目标标签、无目标端优化的情况下，将冻结的EEG基础模型扩展到多模态记录，平均比纯EEG推理提升7.22个百分点平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型虽能捕获大规模EEG数据中的可复用知识，但许多记录还包含能提供互补信息的伴随生理信号。核心挑战在于：如何在保留预训练知识的同时，通过从无标签目标数据中推断出的适配，将EFM扩展到异构多模态记录。

Method: ZeroMAG围绕配置不变的适配器组织伴随模态；从未标注记录和任务上下文中构建模态-被试-任务条件；并在从源适配器学习到的函数约束潜空间中生成适配器权重。整个流程使用冻结的EEG编码器和预测头，无需目标标签或目标端优化，且目标数据集在训练与模型选择中被完全排除。

Result: 在六个留出目标数据集和三种EFM骨干网络上，ZeroMAG相比纯EEG推理提升7.22个百分点平衡准确率，相比直接权重回归提升4.89个百分点，平均仅比有监督多模态适配低0.50个百分点。消融实验表明，从表示学习或条件生成中移除函数监督都会降低生成适配器性能。

Conclusion: ZeroMAG能够有效将冻结的EEG基础模型零样本扩展到多模态记录，接近有监督多模态适配的水平，且表示学习与条件生成中的函数监督均为关键组件。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (EFMs) capture reusable knowledge from large-scale EEG data, while many EEG recordings also include companion physiological signals that provide complementary information beyond the EEG-only interface. The challenge is to preserve this pretrained knowledge while extending the EFM to heterogeneous multimodal recordings through an adaptation inferred from unlabeled target data. We introduce ZeroMAG, a zero-shot multimodal adapter generation framework that extends a frozen EEG encoder and prediction head using unlabeled target recordings, without target labels or target-side optimization. The target datasets are held out from all model training and selection in the ZeroMAG pipeline. ZeroMAG organizes companion modalities around a configuration-invariant adapter, constructs a modality-subject-task condition from unlabeled recordings and task context, and generates adapter weights in a function-constrained latent space learned from source adapters. Across six held-out target datasets and three EFM backbones, ZeroMAG improves balanced accuracy by 7.22 percentage points over EEG-only inference and 4.89 points over direct weight regression, while coming within 0.50 points of supervised multimodal adaptation on average. Ablations further show that removing functional supervision from either representation learning or conditional generation degrades generated-adapter performance, confirming the contribution of both components.

</details>


### [183] [Objects Without Morphisms: What LLMs for Mathematics Do Not Represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03551)
*Yanli Wang,Suijin Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM在无外部评判标准时进行数学子领域间方言翻译的能力，发现模型倾向于在翻译中系统性地改变断言的量化范围（泛化框架下扩大、具体框架下缩小），且这种偏差与模型能力无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在竞赛数学上的表现主要依赖大量搜索和外部标准筛选正确答案，但这种方式只改进了最终存活的结果，并未改变模型内部所表征的内容。作者希望在不存在外部标准的情形下考察模型的真实表征能力——即在不同数学子领域方言之间进行翻译，其中忠实性取决于断言内容所处的泛化层级，而该层级在源文本词汇中是隐含的，需要从理论间的关系中恢复。

Method: 作者设计了一种工具，将真值、内容和范围编码在独立的盲队列中，并采用无评判者（judge-free）的度量来判断改写是否陈述了源文本中隐含的假设，同时用植入阳性对照验证其灵敏度。测试覆盖四个家族的七个模型。

Result: 在向泛化框架翻译时，60.6%的改写扩大了量化域，没有任何一个缩小；在向具体框架翻译时，28.3%缩小了量化域，仅0.3%扩大。能够防止该问题的假设在模型改写中被陈述的比例为21.6%，在人类陈述中仅为4.2%。这种不对称性在所有测试模型中均出现，且能力最强的模型扩大最少。结果在预注册规则留出的一半基准上以及数学家撰写的陈述上均得到复现。指示模型陈述所有所需假设可提高该比例，但不改变其对方向的敏感性。

Conclusion: 这些系统已经获得了子领域词汇之间的对象级对应关系，但缺乏使理论间翻译将假设映射为假设的约束条件。该不对称偏差是系统性的、与能力无关的，且在保留基准和人类陈述上均可复现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have reached expert-level performance on competition mathematics largely through the volume of search placed around them: candidate solutions are sampled in quantity and retained only when an external criterion accepts them. Such a procedure improves the outcome that survives it while leaving untouched what the model represents. We examine that question where no external criterion exists: translating statements between the dialects of neighbouring subfields, where fidelity turns on the level of generality at which content is asserted. The source leaves that level implicit in its vocabulary, so a faithful translation must recover it from the relation between the theories. We introduce an instrument that codes truth, content and scope in separate blind queues, with a judge-free measure of whether a rewrite states the hypothesis implicit in its source, and establish its sensitivity with a planted-positive control. Across seven models from four families, translating towards the general framing widens the domain of quantification in 60.6% of rewrites and narrows it in none; translating towards the concrete framing narrows it in 28.3% and widens it in 0.3%. The hypothesis that would prevent it is stated in 21.6% of model rewrites and 4.2% of human statements. Capability does not govern the asymmetry: it appears in every model tested, and the most capable widens least. It replicates on the half of the benchmark held out by a pre-registered rule, and on statements written by mathematicians. Instructing a model to state every hypothesis it requires raises that rate but not its sensitivity to direction. We argue that these systems have acquired an object-level correspondence between subfield vocabularies without the constraint under which a translation between theories carries hypotheses to hypotheses.

</details>


### [184] [Get a GRIP, this will be a long TRIP: A Quantifiable Long-Range Framework for Verifying Over-squashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03556)
*Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Simon Heilig,Adrián Bazaga,Asja Fischer,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出长程图基准缺乏严格定义，提出四条可验证公理与TRIP/GRIP构造方法，将任意图转化为可证的长程任务，并给出闭式逐范围误差下界及对现有基准的审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于GNN过挤压与长程交互的经验结论，只有在基准真正需要长程交互时才可信。然而事实标准Long Range Graph Benchmark已被反复证明可被调优的短程模型饱和，现有合成替代方案又依赖特定拓扑，任意图上缺乏长程性的原则性证明，根源在于缺少对长程基准的精确刻画。

Method: 提出四条可验证公理：Predictability、Tightness、Strictly k-Range、Topology-Invariance，任何声称测试k跳交互的任务须满足，并形式化证明违反任一条会引入削弱结论的失效模式。基于公理提出TRIP及泛化GRIP，通过从稳定分布抽取特征，将任意图构造为可证长程任务；GRIP依构造具备闭式逐范围最大似然oracle，给出首个任意基准上可用的逐范围先验测试误差下界。

Result: (i) 审计4个常见长程基准并识别其相对公理的失效模式；(ii) 在TRIP实例化拓扑上，发现一种流行的曲率概念与GNN性能不相关，支持拓扑瓶颈与计算瓶颈的区分；(iii) 展示新基准验证的过挤压度量还受纯粹长程性之外的因素影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为长程图基准提供了公理化刻画与可构造验证框架，弥补了长程性缺乏原则性证明的空白，为评估GNN长程交互能力及区分过挤压来源提供了理论与工具支持，代码已开源。

Abstract: Empirical claims about the connection between over-squashing and long-range interactions in GNNs, can only be trusted if the benchmarks used to validate them genuinely require long-range interactions. The de-facto standard, the Long Range Graph Benchmark, has been repeatedly shown to be saturated by tuned short-range models, with existing synthetic alternatives being tied to specific topologies. As such, there is a lack of principled certificate of long-rangedness on arbitrary graphs. This state reflects the absence of a precise characterization of long-ranged benchmarks. We address this fundamental gap by introducing four verifiable axioms: Predictability, Tightness, Strictly $k$-Range, and Topology-Invariance, that any task claiming to test $k$-hop interactions must satisfy. We formally prove that violating any one of them admits failure modes that undermine conclusions drawn from the task. Based on these axioms, we introduce TRIP (Truly Ranged Interactions Problem) and its generalisation GRIP (Generally Ranged Interactions Problem), constructive procedures that turn any graph into a provably long-ranged task by drawing features from stable distributions. Moreover, by construction, GRIP admits a closed-form, per-range Maximum-Likelihood oracle that yields the first a priori per-range lower bound on test error available on any benchmark. Using our framework, we: (i) audit 4 common long-range benchmarks and identify their failures modes with respect to our axioms; (ii) on TRIP-instantiated topologies, we find a popular notion of curvature is uncorrelated with GNN performance, supporting topological-vs-computational bottleneck distinction; and (iii) we show that a novel benchmark's over-squashing measures factors beyond pure long-rangedness. Code to use the framework and reproduce experiments is released https://github.com/ferranhernandezc/graph-grip.

</details>


### [185] [A Path Integral Surrogate for Multi-Step Gradient Inversion in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03597)
*Agnivo Ghosh,Saumik Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-SME，将联邦学习中的累积更新视为梯度场的路径积分，用Gauss-Legendre求积在可学习Bézier路径上多节点逼近，从而更准确地从单次共享更新中重建客户端私有图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习承诺客户端只共享模型更新而非原始数据，但梯度反转攻击可直接从单次更新重建私有输入图像，对隐私构成挑战。在FedAvg下，更新累积了多个本地训练步，服务器只能看到隐藏权重轨迹的两个端点。现有反转攻击虽沿两端点之间的路径拟合代理模型，却仅在单一点读取梯度，信息利用不足。

Method: 提出Path-Integral Surrogate Model Extension（PI-SME）。该方法将累积更新建模为梯度场的路径积分，并通过Gauss-Legendre求积在一个可学习的Bézier路径上的多个节点进行逼近，从而比单点梯度读取更充分地刻画整条权重轨迹。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和FEMNIST图像上，涵盖多种轨迹长度和类别受限批次的设定下，PI-SME在多项反转指标及匹配损失上均比最强的代理基线更忠实地重建私有输入。

Conclusion: 将累积更新视为路径积分并采用多节点求积的可学习Bézier路径逼近，能显著提升从单次联邦更新中反转图像的保真度，表明这一路径积分视角对梯度反转攻击有效。

Abstract: Federated learning lets many clients train a shared model together without ever sending their private data to a central server. Each client shares only a model update, and this update should reveal far less about the client than its raw training examples would. This premise is what protects the privacy of the clients. Gradient inversion attacks challenge it directly by trying to reconstruct a client's private input images from the single update it shared. Under FedAvg, a client's update accumulates several local training steps, so the server sees only the two endpoints of a hidden weight trajectory. Recent gradient inversion attacks fit a surrogate model along the path between these two endpoints but they still read its gradient at a single point. We propose the Path-Integral Surrogate Model Extension (PI-SME) which treats the accumulated update as a path integral of the gradient field and approximates it by Gauss--Legendre quadrature over several nodes along a learnable Bézier path. On CIFAR-100 and FEMNIST images across a range of trajectory lengths and class-restricted batches PI-SME reconstructs the private inputs more faithfully than the strongest surrogate baseline on several inversion metrics and the matching loss.

</details>


### [186] [Mastering Atari 2600 Games with Discovered Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03604)
*Erik M. Lintunen,Marlos C. Machado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Wayfarer是一种通用、与领域无关的在线深度强化学习智能体，通过从高维观测中学习拉普拉斯表示来发现选项（options），并利用这些选项进行控制，在最具挑战性的Atari游戏中取得单流智能体的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间抽象（如选项）长期被视为加速信用分配、促进探索和提升泛化的机制，但现有选项发现方法难以适用于大规模高维领域：它们或局限于简单领域，或依赖手工设计/准符号化表示，或相比无选项学习提升甚微。因此，开发有效的通用选项发现方法仍是根本性挑战。

Method: 提出Wayfarer，一个通用、与领域无关的在线深度强化学习智能体。它从高维观测中通过拉普拉斯表示学习（Laplacian representation learning）发现选项，并将这些选项用于控制。

Result: 所得到的选项同时改善了探索、加速了信用分配，并能有效泛化到未见场景，从而显著加快复杂策略的学习。Wayfarer在最具挑战性的Atari 2600游戏中达到单流智能体的最优性能，在需要长时程探索和策略行为的游戏（如Montezuma's Revenge和Private Eye）中提升最大。

Conclusion: Wayfarer表明，通过拉普拉斯表示学习在高维观测上进行通用、领域无关的选项发现是可行的，并能在具有挑战性的任务中带来实质性的学习加速与性能提升。

Abstract: Temporal abstractions, often instantiated as options, have long been regarded as a mechanism for accelerating credit assignment, facilitating exploration, and enabling generalisation in reinforcement learning (RL). However, developing general option discovery methods that are effective in large-scale, high-dimensional domains remains a fundamental challenge. Existing option discovery methods are either confined to relatively simple domains, depend on handcrafted or quasi-symbolic representations, or offer little improvement over learning without options. We present Wayfarer, a general, domain-agnostic, online deep RL agent that discovers options through Laplacian representation learning from high-dimensional observations and leverages them for control. We show that the resulting options simultaneously improve exploration, accelerate credit assignment, and generalise effectively to unseen settings, enabling substantially faster learning of complex policies. Wayfarer achieves state-of-the-art performance among single-stream agents on the most challenging Atari 2600 games, with the largest gains in games that require long-horizon exploration and strategic behaviour, such as Montezuma's Revenge and Private Eye.

</details>


### [187] [UniIntervene++: An Adaptive Intervention Agent for Efficient Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03620)
*Yudong Lin,Haoyuan Deng,Zhuoxuan Yuan,Zaijia Yang,Yuanjiang Xue,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniIntervene++ 是一种自适应干预智能体，通过在线学习动态分配自主执行与多种辅助行为之间的控制权，以提升真实世界机器人强化学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习让机器人策略能通过物理交互不断改进，但其所需的辅助程度会随能力变化而改变。现有的干预策略基于离线估计或固定规则，容易与当前策略不匹配，因此需要一种能随能力演化而自适应调整干预的方法。

Method: 将演化中的 RL 策略、轨迹纠正以及任务结构化的 CodePolicy 建模为统一半马尔可夫决策过程中的 Options，并在线学习其相对价值；提出能力自适应干预，通过无辅助执行周期性探测 RL 策略，使控制分配随其能力变化保持响应；采用耦合经验学习，使辅助行为改进 RL 策略，而策略演化结果反过来重塑未来的干预决策。由此联合决定何时干预、如何干预以及何时交还控制权。

Result: 在五个真实世界操作任务中，UniIntervene++ 平均成功率达到 89.67%，至少超越所有基线 6 个百分点，同时将人工干预降至 0.77%，相比最佳基线相对减少至少 94.6%。

Conclusion: UniIntervene++ 通过能力自适应与耦合经验学习，实现了对干预时机、干预方式和交还时机的联合优化，在真实操作任务中显著提升成功率并大幅降低人工干预。代码已开源。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) enables robot policies to improve through physical interaction, but the assistance they require changes as their competence evolves. Existing intervention strategies based on offline estimates or fixed decision rules can therefore become mismatched to the current policy. To address this, we propose UniIntervene++, an adaptive intervention agent that learns to allocate control between autonomous execution and heterogeneous assisted behaviors during online RL. Specifically, UniIntervene++ first formulates the evolving RL policy, trajectory correction, and a task-structured CodePolicy as Options in a unified semi-Markov decision process and learns their relative values online. Building on this, competence-adaptive intervention periodically probes the RL policy through unassisted execution, keeping control allocation responsive to its evolving capability. Finally, coupled experience learning allows assisted behaviors to improve the RL policy, whose evolving outcomes in turn reshape future intervention decisions. In this way, UniIntervene++ jointly determines when to intervene, how to intervene, and when to return control as the RL policy improves. Across five real-world manipulation tasks, UniIntervene++ achieves an average success rate of 89.67%, outperforming all baselines by at least 6 percentage points, while reducing human intervention to 0.77%, a relative reduction of at least 94.6% from the best baseline. Code is available in our \href{https://github.com/dannyyudong/An-Adaptive-Intervention-Agent-for-Efficient-Real-World-Reinforcement-Learning}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [188] [Broken scale symmetries in undercomplete linear autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03640)
*Farhad Pashakhanloo,Jacob A. Zavatone-Veth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究欠完备线性自编码器中SGD对尺度对称性的破缺，发现SGD在PCA解流形上倾向于增大解码器权重，这种定向尺度漂移发生在慢时间尺度上并具有可解析的有效描述，但最终受限于有限步长稳定性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络损失景观存在多种对称性，梯度流保持这些对称性，而有限步长SGD会将其破坏。一个典型例子是齐次网络的尺度对称性：逐层缩放参数而保持网络输出不变。已有工作记录了SGD通过平衡梯度噪声或最小化波动来破坏该对称性的情形，但损失几何如何将残余梯度噪声转化为沿功能等价解流形的定向运动尚不清楚。

Method: 以欠完备线性自编码器为对象，分析其在PCA解流形上的SGD动力学，推导尺度漂移的慢时间尺度有效描述，并考察其与有限步长稳定性边界的关系，同时结合损失Hessian最大特征值等锐度度量进行分析。

Result: 在PCA解流形上，SGD偏好较大的解码器权重，即对尺度漂移选择了偏好的符号。该定向漂移发生在慢时间尺度上，其动力学具有解析可处理的有效描述；但漂移不能无限持续，尺度增大会将动力学推向有限步长稳定性边界。所得解在损失Hessian最大特征值意义上比平衡基线更尖锐，而不同锐度度量可能朝相反方向变化。

Conclusion: 欠完备自编码器提供了一个具体例证，说明损失几何如何将残余梯度噪声转化为沿功能等价解流形的定向运动。

Abstract: Neural network loss landscapes have many symmetries, which are preserved by gradient flow but broken by finite-stepsize stochastic gradient descent (SGD). A canonical example of such a symmetry is scale in homogeneous networks: one can scale up the parameters in one layer and down in the next without changing the network output. Previous work has documented cases in which SGD breaks this symmetry in favor of balancing gradient noise or minimizing fluctuations. Here, we show that the solution geometry of undercomplete linear autoencoders instead selects a preferred sign for scale drift: on the PCA solution manifold, SGD favors large decoder weights. This directed scale drift occurs on a slow timescale, and its dynamics admit an analytically-tractable effective description. However, it cannot continue indefinitely: increasing scale eventually drives the dynamics towards a finite-stepsize stability boundary. The resulting solutions are sharper than a balanced baseline in the sense of the maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian, but different sharpness measures can move in opposing directions. Thus, undercomplete autoencoders give a concrete illustration of how loss geometry can convert residual gradient noise into directed motion along a manifold of functionally-equivalent solutions.

</details>


### [189] [IDRF: Inverse-Distilled Reward Fine-tuning of Masked Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03641)
*Vladislav Gromadskii,David Li,Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Eric Moulines,Maxim Panov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IDRF是一个针对少步掩码离散扩散生成器的奖励微调框架，用逆蒸馏正则替代难解序列级KL惩罚，可在减少去噪步数的同时获得高奖励并保持样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码离散扩散模型是自回归生成的有前景替代方案，但迭代采样成本高，且序列似然难以计算，导致奖励微调困难。因此需要一种能在少步采样场景下有效进行奖励微调的方法。

Method: 从标准反向KL正则目标出发，用逆蒸馏正则替换难解的序列级KL惩罚；在最优辅助去噪器下证明总体逆蒸馏损失上界序列级KL散度。优化该损失的轨迹代理，无需参考模型采样，保留学生自身少步采样器。将少步生成视为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，用裁剪策略梯度目标优化奖励。

Result: 在DNA、图像和文本生成任务上，IDRF取得高奖励，去噪步数比参考模型最多减少32倍，同时缓解奖励攻击并保持样本质量。

Conclusion: IDRF为少步掩码离散扩散生成器提供了有效的奖励微调方案，兼顾高效采样、高奖励与样本质量。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, but iterative sampling can be costly, and intractable sequence likelihoods complicate reward fine-tuning. We introduce IDRF, a framework for reward fine-tuning of few-step masked discrete diffusion generators. Starting from a standard reverse-KL-regularized objective, IDRF replaces the intractable sequence-level KL penalty with inverse-distillation regularization. With an optimal auxiliary denoiser, we prove that the population inverse-distillation loss upper-bounds the sequence-level KL divergence to the reference distribution. IDRF optimizes a trajectory-based surrogate of this loss without reference-model rollouts, so the student keeps its own few-step sampler. We view few-step generation as a finite-horizon Markov decision process and optimize reward with a clipped policy-gradient objective over the student's trajectories. Across DNA, image, and text generation, IDRF achieves high reward with up to $32\times$ fewer denoising steps than the reference while mitigating reward hacking and preserving sample quality.

</details>


### [190] [On the Convergence of Success Conditioning for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03642)
*Matthew Brun,Xu Andy Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了成功条件化（success conditioning）在广泛的马尔可夫决策过程中收敛到最优策略，并推导了常见情形下的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功条件化是随机环境中改进决策策略的常用方法，广泛应用于强化学习，但其极限行为与收敛速率尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究成功条件化在一般MDP上的收敛性质，并在折扣MDP与单周期MDP等常见设定下推导收敛速率。

Result: 在折扣MDP中，算法在 O(1/ε^p) 次迭代内收敛到 ε-最优策略，其中指数 p 取决于问题数据；在单周期MDP中，可在 O(log(1/ε)) 次迭代内获得该策略。

Conclusion: 成功条件化具有理论上的收敛保证，能够收敛到最优策略，且在不同MDP设定下展现出不同的收敛速率。

Abstract: Success conditioning is a strategy for improving decision-making policies in stochastic environments; it updates a policy by increasing the probability of taking actions that yield successful outcomes. Success conditioning is common to many reinforcement learning applications, yet its limiting behavior and convergence rates are not well understood. In this work, we demonstrate that success conditioning converges to an optimal policy on a broad class of Markov decision processes (MDPs). We also derive convergence rates in some common settings. For discounted MDPs, we prove convergence within $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^p)$ iterations to an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy, where the exponent $p$ depends on problem data. For single-period MDPs, such a policy is obtained within $\mathcal{O}(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ iterations.

</details>


### [191] [When May a Bandit Leave Its Anchor? E-Process-Authorized Thompson Sampling under Non-stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03646)
*Mayand Gulati,Kerong Wang,WeiChen Au*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 e-process-authorized Thompson sampling (e-ATS)，通过 anytime-valid e-process 授权折扣状态的使用，并用可逆相关性分数控制其影响，从而决定何时允许"遗忘"（适应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平稳环境下记忆是有益的，但环境一旦发生变化，相同的历史可能产生误导。核心问题是：应该在何时允许遗忘/适应？需要一个有原则的机制来判定适应何时开始，而非依赖拟合阈值。

Method: e-ATS 为每个臂同时维护全历史 Beta 状态和折扣 Beta 状态。先由 anytime-valid e-process 授权折扣状态，再由可逆相关性分数控制其影响。授权前，e-ATS 与乐观 Thompson sampling (OTS) 完全一致。在 Beta-Bernoulli 先验预测平稳模型下，方法无需拟合阈值即可给出偏离概率上界。

Result: 在 Beta-Bernoulli 先验预测平稳模型下，e-ATS 偏离 OTS 的概率至多等于所选 α_E，且无需拟合阈值。相比 e-ATS，去掉授权机制后在注册套件上平均归一化动态伪遗憾增加 38.4%，但在文献导出的回放套件上反而降低 7.5%。

Conclusion: 证据决定适应何时开始，而非决定适应是否总是有益。授权机制提供了有原则的控制，但其收益依赖于具体环境。

Abstract: Stationarity rewards memory, but after a change the same history can mislead. We ask when forgetting should be permitted. E-process-authorized Thompson sampling (e-ATS) gives each arm full-history and discounted Beta states. An anytime-valid e-process first authorizes the discounted state, then a reversible relevance score controls its influence. Before authorization, e-ATS exactly follows optimistic Thompson sampling (OTS). Under a Beta-Bernoulli prior-predictive stationary model, e-ATS's probability of ever departing from OTS is at most the chosen $α_E$, without fitted thresholds. Relative to e-ATS, removing authorization increased mean normalized dynamic pseudo-regret by $38.4\%$ on the registered suite but reduced it by $7.5\%$ on the literature-derived replay suite. Therefore, evidence controls when adaptation begins, not whether it always helps.

</details>


### [192] [Forecasting from Counterfactual Simulator Rollouts: A Sim2Real Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03662)
*Angel Wang,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alvaro Maggiar,Dean Foster,Carson Eisenach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过两个真实库存控制部署案例，验证了用模拟器生成的反事实轨迹训练预测模型可解决新策略的冷启动问题，其精度优于历史数据训练，并可通过轻量校准进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署新决策策略时，预测模型面临冷启动问题：历史观测反映旧策略，而新策略下的真实观测尚不可得。模拟器可针对反事实场景滚动展开目标策略来弥补这一空缺。

Method: 通过模拟器展开目标策略生成反事实轨迹，训练系统对控制措施的响应预测模型，并进行Sim2Real迁移；在两个真实库存控制部署中，从模拟器保真度、零样本迁移到真实行为、以及随真实观测累积的适应三方面进行回测评估。

Result: 模拟器训练的预测器MAPE低于用历史真实数据训练的相同架构：研究1降低1.2–3.1个百分点，研究2降低12.5–18.7个百分点；部署后用早期真实观测进行轻量校准可再降低最高2.5个百分点的误差。

Conclusion: 模拟器生成的反事实数据可支持新策略下的冷启动预测，且随真实部署数据积累模型可被进一步优化。

Abstract: Deploying a new decision policy creates a cold-start problem for prediction models whose targets depend on the policy's actions: historical observations reflect earlier policies, while real observations under the new policy are not yet available. Simulation offers a way to address this gap by rolling out the target policy across counterfactual scenarios and using the resulting trajectories to learn how the system responds to those controls. The simulation-to-reality (Sim2Real) transfer of this simulator-trained model can then be backtested by evaluating it against real observations from past deployments. Using two real-world inventory-control deployments, we evaluate this process from three angles: simulator fidelity, zero-shot transfer to real behavior, and adaptation as real target-policy observations accumulate. The simulator-trained forecaster achieves lower point-estimate mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) than the same architecture trained on historical real data, reducing MAPE by 1.2-3.1 percentage points in Study 1 and 12.5-18.7 points in Study 2. After deployment, lightweight calibration using early real observations further reduces error by up to 2.5 percentage points. These results provide empirical evidence that simulator-generated counterfactual data can support cold-start forecasting under a new policy, and the resulting model can be further refined as real deployment data become available.

</details>


### [193] [Pivot-SD: Efficient Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03665)
*Seo Hyun Kim,Sunwoo Hong,Younwoo Choi,Chen-Hao Chao,Se-Young Yun,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pivot-SD是一种离线自蒸馏框架，通过信息增益度量挑选掩码扩散语言模型中高影响力的"关键承诺"（pivots），仅对这些位置进行监督训练，用少量数据即提升模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（dLMs）在复杂推理上是有前景的并行替代方案，但存在信用分配难题：去噪过程中少数承诺会大幅降低剩余掩码位置的不确定性并塑造大部分响应。现有的后训练方法未利用这一信号，通常对最终文本训练或对整个去噪步骤分配奖励，而非选择决定响应的个别承诺。

Method: 提出Pivot-SD离线自蒸馏框架，仅监督高影响力承诺（pivots）。使用信息增益度量（衡量对剩余掩码位置的不确定性降低程度）来选择pivots。成功轨迹的pivots用交叉熵训练，失败轨迹的pivots用定向unlikelihood训练，失败轨迹的其余部分保持不变。

Result: 仅用200个问题和每个4次rollout，Pivot-SD在数学和代码基准上使LLaDA-8B-Instruct超过全序列SFT和预算匹配的扩散RL基线。

Conclusion: 聚焦于高影响力承诺的Credit-assignment策略为dLMs后训练提供了一种高效路径，以极小数据量实现了优于SFT和RL的推理性能提升。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLMs) offer a promising parallel alternative to autoregressive models for complex reasoning. However, they face a distinct credit-assignment challenge, since a few commitments during denoising sharply reduce the uncertainty over the remaining masked positions and shape much of the response. Most post-training recipes for dLMs do not use this signal to decide which tokens to train on: they typically train on the final text or assign rewards to whole denoising steps, rather than selecting the individual commitments that shape the response. We introduce Pivot-SD, an efficient offline self-distillation framework that supervises only these high-impact commitments (pivots). Pivot-SD selects pivots using an information-gain metric measuring uncertainty reduction over the remaining masked positions. Pivots from successful trajectories are trained with cross-entropy, and pivots from failed trajectories with targeted unlikelihood, leaving the rest of the failed trajectory untouched. Using only 200 questions and four rollouts each, Pivot-SD improves LLaDA-8B-Instruct over full-sequence SFT and budget-matched diffusion RL baselines across math and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [194] [Planning to Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03667)
*Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出精确策略梯度因"短视"（仅评估当前收益，忽略其对后续学习起点的影响）而劣于交叉熵；通过将交叉熵解释为"耐心准确率"（对数几率永续增长时的总误差），并按剩余学习量截断，提出单行改动的 horizon loss，在 MNIST 与 ImageNet（ResNet-50/101、ViT-S/16）上以恒定学习率提升 top-1 准确率，且标签噪声越大增益越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类任务中不存在探索、信用分配与动作采样噪声等问题，策略梯度精确且平滑，但精确策略梯度在期望准确率上仍败给交叉熵。作者试图解释并弥合这一反直觉的差距。

Method: 将分类器视为策略，其期望奖励即期望准确率（正确标签概率）。指出精确策略梯度是短视的：更新价值取决于剩余学习量。由此把交叉熵重新解释为"耐心准确率"，即对数几率以单位速度永续上升时的总误差，而精确策略梯度是其零视界极限。按剩余学习量截断该总误差，得到 horizon loss——相对交叉熵的一行改动，随训练推进从交叉熵逐步过渡到精确策略梯度。

Result: 在一个简单分配模型中可证明 horizon loss 能跳出两个端点各自陷入的陷阱。在 MNIST 以及使用 ResNet-50、ResNet-101、ViT-S/16 的 ImageNet 上，恒定学习率下 horizon loss 的 top-1 准确率优于交叉熵，且标签噪声越大，增益越显著。

Conclusion: 交叉熵可理解为具有"耐心视界"的准确率目标，而精确策略梯度只是其零视界极限。用 horizon loss 按剩余学习量截断该视界，能在理论与实验上同时规避两者的缺陷，是一种简单而有效的损失改进。

Abstract: Policy-gradient methods are central to modern reinforcement learning, including LLM post-training. When they struggle, the usual suspects are exploration, credit assignment and action-sampling noise. Classification has none of them. A classifier is a policy whose expected reward, its \emph{expected accuracy}, is the probability it assigns to the correct label, and because that label is known, the policy gradient is exact and smooth. Yet exact policy gradient loses to cross-entropy, even on expected accuracy. The exact gradient is myopic: it values an update only by what it buys now, but each update also sets where the next one starts, so an update's value depends on how much learning remains. Viewed this way, cross-entropy is patient accuracy, the total error an example would pay if its log-odds rose at unit speed forever, while exact policy gradient is the zero-horizon limit. Truncating this total at the learning that remains yields the horizon loss, a one-line change that moves from cross-entropy toward exact policy gradient as training runs out. In a simple allocation model, it provably escapes the trap that catches each endpoint. On MNIST and on ImageNet with ResNet-50, ResNet-101 and ViT-S/16, the horizon loss improves top-1 accuracy over cross-entropy at a flat learning rate, and the gain grows with label noise.

</details>


### [195] [Simulation-Free Learning of Population Dynamics with Wasserstein Lagrangian Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03679)
*Fedor Sergeev,Markus Heinonen,Daniel Waxman,Tim Cooijmans,Ricardo Baptista,Dmitry Batenkov,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Double-Stitch，一种无需仿真的方法，通过学习种群路径上的运动方程残差来学习Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学，可处理保守和周期动态，训练速度比基于仿真的WLM快4-14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞、生物体和流体的动态常被建模为随时间演化的概率分布。从非配对快照重建并外推该演化需要关于潜在过程的假设。Wasserstein梯度流是常见选择，但无法描述保守或周期动态。Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学可覆盖两者，但现有学习方法基于仿真，每个训练步骤都需运行数值求解器，训练代价高昂。

Method: 提出Double-Stitch，一种无需仿真的方法：通过惩罚沿学习到的种群路径上的运动方程残差来学习这些力学。该方程由不需要梯度速度的Clebsch变分原理推导得出，并证明当方程成立时残差恰好为零。

Result: 在合成、单细胞和海洋涡旋数据集上测试Double-Stitch，发现其在多数任务上与梯度流方法及基于仿真的WLM相当或更优，同时训练速度比WLM快4-14倍。提供了JAX实现。

Conclusion: Double-Stitch通过免仿真的残差惩罚方式有效学习Wasserstein拉格朗日力学，兼顾保守与周期动态建模能力，并在精度与效率上优于现有方法。

Abstract: The dynamics of cells, organisms, and fluids are often modeled as probability distributions evolving over time. Reconstructing and extrapolating this evolution from unpaired snapshots requires assumptions about the underlying process. Wasserstein gradient flows are a common choice, but they cannot describe conservative or periodic dynamics. Lagrangian mechanics in Wasserstein space covers both, but existing methods for learning it are simulation-based: they run a numerical solver at every training step, which makes training expensive. We propose Double-Stitch, a simulation-free method that learns these mechanics by penalizing the residual of the equation of motion along a learned population path. We derive this equation from a Clebsch variational principle that does not require gradient velocities, and show that the residual vanishes exactly when the equation holds. We test Double-Stitch on synthetic, single-cell and ocean vortex datasets and find that it matches or outperforms gradient-flow methods and simulation-based WLM on most tasks, while training $4$-$14$ times faster than WLM. We provide a JAX implementation of Double-Stitch at https://github.com/BasisResearch/stitching.

</details>


### [196] [LESSER: Post-Training Data Selection with Output-Layer Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03702)
*Lyuxin David Zhang,Eric Wong,Surbhi Goel,Anton Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LESSER，用输出层梯度替代全参数梯度进行数据选择，大幅降低计算成本并保持下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的训练数据选择对下游性能影响大，但全参数梯度排名需对每个样本反向传播，计算量巨大，难以处理大规模候选池。因此需要探索能否以更低成本近似全梯度特征。

Method: 发现输出层梯度足以进行有效数据选择，且只需更便宜的前向传播。将其实现为 LESSER，作为选择方法的即插即用封装。

Result: LESSER 将特征提取的 FLOP 成本在 SFT 上降低 9.7 倍，在 RL 基准上降低 3.0 倍，同时在下游任务上保持与全梯度相当的性能。即使输出层与全梯度对单个样本排名不同，它们选出的批次梯度仍然对齐。

Conclusion: 输出层梯度是高效且有效的全梯度近似方案，LESSER 可作为通用封装显著降低数据选择成本。

Abstract: The choice of post-training data for large language models substantially affects downstream performance. Gradient-based data selection is a popular approach that ranks training data by how well their gradients align with those of a small validation set. However, ranking with full-parameter gradients requires an expensive backward pass on every sample, making computation intractable for large candidate pools. This raises a natural question: can we approximate full-gradient features at a fraction of the cost? Conveniently, we find that output-layer gradients suffice for effective data selection, yet require only the cheaper forward pass. We implement this as LESSER, a drop-in wrapper for selection methods that reduces the feature-extraction FLOP cost by $9.7\times$ for SFT and $3.0\times$ for RL benchmarks, while tracking full-gradient performance on downstream tasks. Empirically, we find that even when output-layer and full gradients rank individual samples differently, they select batches with aligned gradients.

</details>


### [197] [RNADyn: A Benchmark for Generating and Understanding RNA Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03712)
*Yiming Huang,Lennart Bastian,Hanqun Cao,Luis Vollmers,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出RNADynBench（标准化RNA分子动力学基准，含2585条质控100纳秒全原子轨迹）与RNADynNet（统一模型，用共享骨干同时实现轨迹生成与动力学指纹提取），在测试集上取得RMSF相关性0.875和0.766。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA功能依赖构象变化，静态结构无法完全刻画；而大规模标准化RNA动力学数据有限，且现有方法通常将轨迹生成与动力学理解视为彼此分离的目标。

Method: 构建RNADynBench基准，包含2585条经过质量控制的100纳秒全原子轨迹及防泄漏数据划分；基于此开发RNADynNet，采用共享骨干网络，结合坐标去噪、单帧到轨迹对齐与物理约束，实现从单一构象同时进行轨迹生成和动力学指纹提取。

Result: 在两个测试集（含高柔性挑战集）上，生成轨迹的RMSF相关性分别达0.875与0.766；单构象预测与MD推导的动力学结果具有可比一致性；物理约束同时改善了生成动力学及指纹中可恢复的物理信息。

Conclusion: RNADynBench与RNADynNet共同为RNA动力学的生成与理解建立了基准与统一建模框架。

Abstract: Ribonucleic acid (RNA) functions through conformational changes that are not fully captured by static structures. However, large-scale standardized RNA dynamics data remain limited, and existing approaches typically treat trajectory generation and dynamics understanding as separate objectives. Here, we introduce RNADynBench, a standardized RNA molecular dynamics (MD) benchmark with 2585 quality-controlled 100-ns all-atom trajectories and leakage-controlled splits. Building on RNADynBench, we develop RNADynNet, a unified model for RNA dynamics learning that uses a shared backbone for both trajectory generation and dynamics fingerprint extraction from a single conformer. It combines coordinate denoising, single-frame-to-trajectory alignment, and physical grounding to connect all-atom trajectory generation with dynamics representation learning. Physical grounding improves both generated dynamics and the physical information recoverable from these fingerprints. Across both test sets, including the high-flexibility challenge set, the generated trajectories achieve RMSF correlations of 0.875 and 0.766, while single-conformer predictions show comparable agreement with MD-derived dynamics. RNADynBench and RNADynNet together establish a benchmark and unified modeling framework for generating and understanding RNA dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [MintFlow: Minimal Trajectory Intervention for Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02260)
*Yesom Park,Kelvin Kan,Qifan Chen,Thomas Flynn,Hayden Schaeffer. Xihaier Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MintFlow 是一种无需训练的约束采样框架，将约束满足表述为对预训练流轨迹的最小干预，在满足约束的同时显著更好地保持预训练生成分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型擅长生成建模，但许多下游应用要求样本满足预设约束（如观测测量和物理定律）。现有约束采样器常面临权衡：强制约束会使样本大幅偏离预训练数据分布。

Method: MintFlow 将约束满足表述为对预训练流轨迹的最小干预。它寻找对中间流状态的最小扰动，使得该状态在预训练流场下后续演化能满足目标约束。通过仅最小扰动流状态而保持预训练流场不变，在满足约束的同时最小化对预训练分布的偏离。伴随（adjoint）表述给出了该扰动的闭式解，避免昂贵的迭代优化。此外，MintFlow 自适应选择干预时刻，以平衡所需扰动幅度与其被剩余流放大的程度。

Result: 在生成视觉和物理系统建模的一系列任务上，MintFlow 在实现有竞争力的约束满足的同时，比最先进的约束方法显著更好地保持了预训练生成分布。

Conclusion: MintFlow 通过最小干预预训练流轨迹、闭式扰动解和自适应干预时刻选择，有效缓解了约束满足与分布保持之间的权衡，且无需训练。

Abstract: Flow matching models excel at generative modeling, and many downstream applications require their samples to satisfy prescribed constraints, such as observed measurements and physical laws. However, existing constrained samplers often face a trade-off: \textit{enforcing constraints can substantially displace samples from the pretrained data distribution}. To address this trade-off, we introduce \textbf{MintFlow}, a training-free constrained sampling framework that formulates constraint enforcement as a minimal intervention on the pretrained flow trajectory. MintFlow seeks the minimal perturbation of an intermediate flow state such that its subsequent evolution under the pretrained flow field satisfies the target constraint. By minimally perturbing the flow state while keeping the pretrained flow field unchanged, MintFlow enforces the constraint while minimizing unnecessary deviation from the pretrained distribution. An adjoint formulation yields a closed-form expression for this perturbation, eliminating expensive iterative optimization. Furthermore, MintFlow adaptively selects the intervention time to balance the required perturbation magnitude with its amplification by the remaining flow. Across a range of tasks in generative vision and physical system modeling, MintFlow achieves competitive constraint satisfaction while preserving the pretrained generative distribution substantially better than state-of-the-art constrained methods.

</details>


### [199] [Fast Models, Slow Evidence: A Paired and Self-Audited Evaluation of System-1 Decision Models for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02267)
*Jiawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对两个System-1决策模型（开源Laya与托管Jev）在11个智能体决策点上进行配对评估，发现Jev在9/11决策点上显著更准，同时作者审计并修正了自身流程中的四处偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架每任务需做大量小型类型化决策（选模型、选工具、判断检索相关性、检测注入等）。System-1决策模型用单次前向传播输出类别概率，有望大幅节省成本与延迟，但其可靠性与评估严谨性尚不明确，因此需要严格的配对评估与自我审计。

Method: 基于18个公开来源构建11个决策点，包含7,283个基础样本和6,640个鲁棒性变体；采用字节级相同输入、配对检验、跨硬件与跨天复现性检查，对开源模型Laya与托管模型Jev进行比较；并对评估流程本身进行审计。

Result: Jev在9/11决策点上显著更准（+10.8至+46.0个百分点）。两模型在零样本模型路由上均未超过随机水平，在RAG相关性门控上打平。Laya在选项顺序反转时改变30%的答案，且候选多或相似时性能骤降（50个最近邻工具时仅31%，而Jev在唯一正确工具项上达98%）。流程审计发现三处分析错误和一处设计混淆：遗漏预筛成本（报告23.9%节省，实际4.3%）、将门控准确率当作端到端质量（58%对98%）、样本内阈值（目标5%，实际留出集最高17%漏检）、以及随通道原生内容消失的通道效应；另两个疑似混淆未改变结论。

Conclusion: System-1决策模型在智能体决策中尚不可靠，尤其在模型路由、顺序敏感性和大量相似候选项场景下表现差；严格的配对评估与自我审计对避免夸大部署收益至关重要。所有案例、原始输出与分析代码已开源。

Abstract: Agent harnesses make many small, typed decisions per task: which model to call, which tool to use, whether retrieved text is relevant, whether an input carries an injection. System-1 decision models answer such questions in a single forward pass with class probabilities, promising large cost and latency savings over LLM calls. We present a paired evaluation of an open-weight (Laya) and a hosted (Jev) System-1 model on 11 agent decision points built from 18 public sources: 7,283 base cases plus 6,640 robustness variants, with byte-identical inputs, paired tests, and cross-hardware and cross-day reproducibility checks. Jev is significantly more accurate on 9 of 11 decision points (+10.8 to +46.0 pp). Neither model beats chance on zero-shot model routing, and they tie on RAG relevance gating. Laya changes 30% of its answers when the option order is reversed and degrades sharply with many or similar candidates (31% at 50 nearest-neighbour tools, vs. 98% for Jev on items with a unique correct tool). We also audit our own pipeline. Three analysis errors and one design confound distorted headline deployment claims: an omitted pre-screen cost (reported 23.9% saving, actual 4.3%), gate accuracy reported as end-to-end quality (58% vs. 98%), in-sample thresholds (5% target, up to 17% held-out misses), and a "channel effect" on injection false positives that vanishes with channel-native content. Two other suspected confounds did not change the conclusions. All cases, raw outputs and analysis code are available at https://github.com/David-DL-Space/sys1-eval.

</details>


### [200] [The AI Risk Observatory: What Can We Learn from AI Disclosures in Annual Reports About Societal Resilience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02281)
*Bart Jaworski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究用可复现的两阶段LLM分类流程分析了1,362家英国上市公司2020–2025年的9,821份年报，发现AI风险披露比例从2.8%升至41.2%，但其中仅4.3%属于实质性披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会韧性研究需要有用且可操作的数据，因此作者提出核心问题：能否用LLM大规模处理年报，从而获取企业如何披露其应对AI的信号。

Method: 采用可复现的两阶段分类流程，处理1,362家英国上市公司2020–2025年（含部分2026年数据）的9,821份年报；先用474段人工标注文本验证方法，并构建"实质性"（substantiveness）分类以评估披露质量。

Result: 方法验证显示召回率高、标签层面一致性中等。三项经验发现：(i) 提及AI风险的年报占比从2020年2.8%升至2025年41.2%，AI采用披露从13.8%升至45.2%，提及的供应商集中于少数主要厂商（以微软为首）；(ii) 披露因关键国家基础设施部门和市场板块而异，AIM市场AI风险披露率远低于主板市场，能源与数据基础设施等部门落后；(iii) 危害披露几乎缺失（全语料仅7份）。2025年41.2%的报告提及AI风险，但仅4.3%为实质性披露。

Conclusion: LLM规模化处理年报可提供关于企业AI披露的有用信号，但多数AI风险披露缺乏实质内容；披露水平在行业与市场板块间差异显著，且危害披露几乎空白。

Abstract: Societal resilience research relies on access to useful and actionable data, which motivates our main research question: Can annual reports, processed at scale with LLMs, provide a useful signal about how companies disclose their response to AI? We test this by applying a reproducible two-stage classification pipeline to 9,821 annual reports from 1,362 UK listed companies (2020-2025, with partial 2026 data). We first validate the method against 474 human-annotated passages, finding high recall and moderate label-level agreement. We then report three empirical patterns: (i) between 2020 and 2025, the share of reports mentioning AI risk rose from 2.8% to 41.2%, while AI adoption disclosure also rose, from 13.8% to 45.2%, and named vendor mentions cluster around a small set of major providers led by Microsoft; (ii) disclosure varies substantially by Critical National Infrastructure sector and market segment: AIM reports disclose AI risk at far lower rates than Main Market reports, and sectors such as Energy and Data Infrastructure lag behind the rest in AI risk disclosure; and (iii) harm disclosures are near-absent (seven reports across the entire corpus). We develop a substantiveness classification to assess the quality of the disclosure and find that most AI risk disclosure is not substantive: in 2025, 41.2% of all reports mention AI as a risk, but only 4.3% contain AI risk disclosure we classify as substantive.

</details>


### [201] [Keep It CALM: Analyzing the Limits of Global Unsafety in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02300)
*NaHyeon Park,Minhyun Lee,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CALM，一种无需训练的文生图安全防护方法，用提示词局部的反事实修正替代全局不安全信号移除，在更好抑制不安全内容的同时保留良性生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图安全防护多依赖可复用的全局安全信号（如不安全方向或全局有害子空间），并统一应用于所有提示词。作者通过受控的几何分析检验该"全局不安全"假设，发现存在一致的覆盖度—选择性权衡：紧凑的不安全子空间无法覆盖异质的不安全语义，而扩大聚合范围又会扭曲与安全相邻的良性提示。

Method: 提出CALM（反事实自适应局部调制），一种免训练防护方法。它利用配对的不安全—良性锚点，为每个提示词路由到其实际激活的不安全类别，仅对违规的token表示做最小化的安全侧编辑，并抑制正向对齐的不安全残差成分，从而以提示词局部的反事实修正取代统一的全局移除。

Result: 在广泛评估中，CALM显著提升了不安全内容的抑制效果，同时保持了良性提示的实用性与生成质量。

Conclusion: 局部反事实修正比全局不安全信号移除更具选择性，是文生图安全防护的可行替代方案。

Abstract: Training-free safeguards for text-to-image generation often rely on a reusable safety signal, such as an unsafe direction or global toxic subspace, applied broadly across prompts. We provide a controlled geometric analysis of this global-unsafety assumption and reveal a consistent coverage-selectivity trade-off: compact unsafe subspaces fail to cover heterogeneous unsafe semantics, whereas broader aggregation increasingly distorts safety-adjacent benign prompts. Motivated by this finding, we propose CALM (Counterfactual Adaptive Local Modulation), a training-free safeguard that replaces uniform global removal with prompt-local counterfactual correction. Using matched unsafe-benign anchors, CALM routes each prompt to active unsafe categories, minimally edits only violating token representations toward the safe side, and suppresses positively aligned unsafe residual components. Across broad evaluation, CALM significantly improves unsafe content suppression while preserving benign utility, demonstrating that local counterfactual correction provides a more selective alternative to global unsafe signal removal.

</details>


### [202] [Choosing Before Acting: Comparative Value Estimation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02330)
*Yu Li,Zheng Zhang,Xin Liu,Shengtian Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CITA框架，训练比较推断模型（CIM）在长程工具调用中估计下一步调用的长期价值，通过配对信号监督提升工具调用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型进行复杂任务时依赖长程工具调用序列，每次调用会改变任务状态并影响后续决策。然而最终结果奖励难以对长交互轨迹进行有效信用分配，而步级奖励又需人工/LLM判断或额外探索来估计中间决策的下游影响。

Method: 提出CITA（Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents）。核心是训练比较推断模型（CIM），利用配对信号：观测到的工具行为、来自贝叶斯工具图模拟器的可扩展监督，以及基于LLM比较的语义判断。CIM学习估计在当前上下文下某个可能的下一步工具调用对最终任务成功的贡献概率，从而在执行前评估其长期价值。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准和多个骨干LLM上，CITA持续提升Tool F1和任务成功率。额外分析表明CIM能为比较性工具选择学习到准确的步级价值估计。

Conclusion: 工具使用智能体应在执行前估计下一步工具调用的长期价值；CITA通过结合观测行为、模拟器监督与LLM语义判断，有效解决了长程信用分配问题，并在多基准多模型上验证有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on long-horizon tool invocation sequences for complex tasks, where each invocation can alter the task state and condition subsequent decisions. In long-horizon tool use, final-outcome rewards provide weak credit assignment over long interaction traces. Step-level rewards can offer more targeted feedback, but obtaining reliable step supervision often requires human or LLM judgment, or additional rollouts to estimate the downstream effect of an intermediate decision. In this paper, we argue that effective tool-use agents should estimate the long-horizon value of a possible next tool invocation before executing it. This objective requires comparative supervision over alternative invocations under the same context, while logged trajectories only contain the invocation that was actually taken. Therefore, we propose Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents (CITA). CITA trains a Comparative Inference Model (CIM) from paired signals that combine observed tool behavior, scalable supervision from a Bayesian tool-graph simulator, and semantic judgments from LLM-based comparison. The resulting CIM learns to estimate how likely a possible next tool invocation is to support final task success under the current context. Across three tool-use benchmarks and multiple backbone LLMs, CITA consistently improves Tool F1 and task success. Additional analysis shows that CIM learns accurate step-level value estimates for comparative tool choices.

</details>


### [203] [World Editing: Intervening on Executable Worlds at Increasing Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02331)
*Max Ku,Nok-Kan Law,Yu-Chien Tang,Shih-Ying Yeh,Ping Nie,Andy Zheng,Tat Hei Lai,Fei-Yueh Chen,Nikko Yu,Wei-Chieh Sun,Suzy Huang,Chiao-Wei Hsu,Chih-Chuan Huang,Chak-Wing Mak,Ho Yin Sam Ng,Edisy Kin Wai Chan,Min-Hung Chen,Ho Kei Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"世界编辑"这一新能力并构建IGMWorld与IGMBench基准，评估前沿编码智能体在可执行世界中实施干预的能力，发现其表现良好但可靠性随干预深度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型已能生成环境并在其中行动，但对已有可执行世界进行刻意编辑的研究仍很缺乏。世界编辑与生成、交互不同，需要在实施干预的同时保持不应改变的性质，因此需要专门的形式化定义、任务体系和评测方法。

Method: 将世界编辑形式化为对已有世界的干预，并要求保留应保持不变的性质；提出"干预深度"作为刻画编辑对世界实体、动力学与系统耦合强度的维度。通过工业级游戏模组（modding）实例化该能力，构建IGMWorld，并推出IGMBench：涵盖Minecraft与Terraria的110个任务、1100余条可执行状态与行为准则，任务分为属性、实体、动力学与系统四类干预，采用确定性可执行性、行为、保持性与视觉四类检查进行评估。

Result: 前沿编码智能体已具备显著的世界编辑能力：最强配置在严格任务级标准下解决78.2%的任务，准则级表现达94.8%。可靠性总体随干预深度增加而下降，且在评测准则数量相近的任务中依然存在这一规律。多数失败编辑仍能成功构建和加载，说明主要难点在于让编辑后的世界按要求运行。视觉一致性是独立短板，所有评估配置的联合视觉通过率均低于50%。

Conclusion: 结果表明世界编辑是与世界生成、世界交互不同的一项独立能力，可执行游戏为研究该能力提供了实用测试平台。

Abstract: Interactive world models are increasingly capable of generating environments and acting within them, yet deliberately editing an existing executable world remains underexplored. We formulate world editing as intervening on an existing world while preserving properties that should remain unchanged, and introduce intervention depth as an axis describing how strongly an edit couples world entities, dynamics, and systems. We instantiate this capability through industry-grade game modding and introduce IGMWorld, together with IGMBench, a benchmark of 110 tasks and over 1.1K executable state and behavioral criteria across Minecraft and Terraria. The tasks span property, entity, dynamics, and system interventions and are evaluated through deterministic executability, behavioral, preservation, and visual checks. Frontier coding agents already exhibit substantial world-editing capability: the strongest configuration solves 78.2% of tasks under a strict task-level criterion, while criterion-level performance reaches 94.8%. Reliability generally decreases with intervention depth, and this pattern persists even among tasks with similar numbers of evaluation criteria. Most failed edits still build and load successfully, suggesting that the main difficulty is making the edited world behave as requested. Visual consistency remains a separate weakness, with all evaluated configurations below 50% joint visual pass rate. These results show that world editing is a distinct capability from world generation and interaction, and that executable games provide a practical testbed for studying it.

</details>


### [204] [A Multi Method Importance and Performance Efficiency Analysis of Topological Metrics for Natural Visibility Graph Based Cyber Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02342)
*Ali Melih Kanca,Ilker Turker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估21个NVG拓扑指标，通过SHAP、分组排列重要性、Boruta与RFE的共识排序筛选紧凑特征子集；在CICIDS2018上用CNN与5折交叉验证，Top3配置取得97.148%准确率和0.9675 MCC，运行时间减少96.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然可见图（NVG）分析能通过网络拓扑描述符刻画流量结构，但并非所有描述符对网络攻击分类贡献相同，使用大量指标会增加计算成本。因此需要研究是否能用紧凑的指标子集在保持分类能力的同时提升效率。

Method: 评估21个NVG衍生拓扑指标；整合SHAP、分组排列重要性、Boruta和递归特征消除（RFE）四种重要性分析方法，形成共识排序；据此构建Full21、Top15、Top10、Top7、Top5、Top3六种配置，在CICIDS2018数据集上用CNN分类器和分层5折交叉验证进行评估。

Result: 排名最高的三个指标为avg_clustering_coeff_median、avg_clustering_coeff_std、avg_clustering_coeff_mean。Top3取得最高观测平均性能：准确率97.148%、加权F1 97.055%、MCC 0.9675；而Full21分别为95.999%、95.521%、0.9549。总运行时间从14,961.39秒降至589.22秒（减少96.06%）。

Conclusion: 在所评估的设置下，基于重要性引导的指标约简能够提供紧凑的NVG表示，同时获得更高的观测平均预测性能和显著更低的计算成本。

Abstract: Natural Visibility Graph (NVG) based analysis characterizes network traffic through topological descriptors reflecting different structural properties. However, not all descriptors contribute equally to cyber-attack classification, and extracting a large metric set can increase computational cost. This study evaluates 21 NVG derived topological metrics and investigates whether a compact subset can preserve classification capability while improving computational efficiency. Four importance analysis methods SHAP, grouped Permutation Importance, Boruta, and Recursive Feature Elimination (RFE) are integrated through a Consensus Ranking strategy. Based on this ranking, Full21, Top15, Top10, Top7, Top5, and Top3 configurations are evaluated using the CICIDS2018 dataset, a CNN classifier, and stratified 5 fold cross validation. The three highest ranked metrics are avg_clustering_coeff_median, avg_clustering_coeff_std, and avg_clustering_coeff_mean. Top3 achieved the highest observed mean performance, with 97.148% accuracy, 97.055% weighted F1 score, and an MCC of 0.9675, compared with 95.999%, 95.521%, and 0.9549 for Full21, respectively. It also reduced total runtime from 14,961.39 s to 589.22 s (96.06%). These results indicate that importance guided metric reduction can provide a compact NVG representation with higher observed mean predictive performance and substantially lower computational cost under the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [205] [DeReAct: Decomposed Reasoning and Acting for Reliable AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02351)
*Ajay Vohra,Tao Chen,Neeti Narayan,Caron Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeReAct是一种模块化智能体架构，将动作验证与任务完成判定外化为Critic和Context Manager两个门控策略，在GAIA和SWE-bench Verified上显著提升较弱模型的Pass@1，并在强模型上带来更强的证据支撑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct式智能体依赖单一LLM策略同时完成动作提出、环境交互与任务完成判定，导致动作授权与完成控制难以独立执行，错误会传播，且不受支持的任务完成声明会直接终止执行。

Method: 提出DeReAct模块化架构，外置两个门控策略：Critic在动作执行前进行验证；Context Manager重建由环境支持的State并认证任务完成。

Result: 在GAIA和SWE-bench Verified上，DeReAct对较弱Brain模型提升最大：Qwen3-Coder-480B提升6.5–7.0分，Claude Sonnet 4.5提升4.2–5.2分；随Brain能力增强增益递减。轨迹与消融分析表明，当目标失败足够普遍且门控策略本身充分时，外部门控才有效。使用Claude Opus 4.5时Pass@1与ReAct相当，但轨迹更具备证据完整性与约束满足性。

Conclusion: DeReAct能改善较弱智能体，并随模型增强保留 grounding 收益，说明完成控制可以用更早终止换取更强的依据支撑。

Abstract: ReAct-based agents typically rely on a single LLM policy to propose actions, interact with the environment, and decide when a task is complete. This coupling makes action authorization and completion control difficult to enforce independently, allowing errors to propagate and unsupported completion claims to terminate execution. We introduce DeReAct, a modular agent architecture that externalizes two gating policies: a Critic that validates proposed actions before execution, and a Context Manager that reconstructs an environment-supported \textsc{State} and certifies task completion.
  Across GAIA and SWE-bench Verified, DeReAct improves Pass@1 most for weaker Brain models, with gains of 6.5--7.0 points for Qwen3-Coder-480B and 4.2--5.2 points for Claude Sonnet~4.5; gains diminish as Brain capability increases. Trajectory and ablation analyses show that external gating is effective when targeted failures are sufficiently prevalent and the gating policy is itself sufficient. With Claude Opus~4.5, Pass@1 remains comparable to ReAct, while DeReAct produces more evidence-complete and constraint-satisfying trajectories, indicating that completion control can trade earlier termination for stronger grounding. Overall, DeReAct improves weaker agents while retaining grounding benefits as models strengthen.

</details>


### [206] [Traversing the Satisfaction-Diversity Frontier in Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02372)
*Kevin Zhai,Siva Rajesh Kasa,Soumya Roy,Sumit Negi,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将文本到图像生成建模为"满意度"问题：每张图须满足奖励下限，整批图须满足多样性下限；提出免训练推理时方法 SatisDive，通过在 Pick-a-Pic 上验证其可在匹配多样性下显著提升最差候选奖励，并帕累托优于 FK steering。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一提示词生成多张图像时，每张既要符合用户偏好（由奖励模型度量），又要彼此视觉上保持多样。现有方法要么将奖励与多样性分开处理，要么合成单一聚合分数，导致高多样性可补偿低奖励，无法保证每张图质量。

Method: 将生成形式化为满意度问题：每个候选须满足奖励下限，整批须满足多样性下限；奖励下限控制最差候选奖励与批多样性的权衡，并可刻画帕累托前沿。提出免训练的推理时方法 SatisDive，使用批相对奖励截断区分低奖励与高奖励候选，对低于截断的候选强调奖励提升，对高于截断的候选强调多样性。

Result: 在 Pick-a-Pic 上，匹配 DreamSim 时，以 FLUX.1-dev 为基座、HPSv3 为奖励，SatisDive 相比 FK steering 将最差候选奖励提升最多 0.43；以 SANA-1.6B 为基座、ImageReward 为奖励，提升最多 0.70。在重叠的 DreamSim 区间内，各设置下 SatisDive 的满意度—多样性曲线均帕累托优于 FK steering。

Conclusion: 将奖励与多样性以满意度约束方式解耦，可避免聚合分数下的相互补偿问题；SatisDive 作为免训练推理时方法，在不牺牲多样性的前提下有效提升最差候选奖励，并在多种基座与奖励模型组合上取得帕累托占优表现。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation enables users to explore several images generated from the same prompt. For these generated images to be useful, each one must reflect the user's preferences, measured by a learned reward, and differ visually from the others to maintain diversity. Existing methods are limited: they either address reward and diversity separately or combine them in one aggregate score, enabling high diversity to offset low rewards. In this paper, we address these limitations by formulating generation as satisficing: every image (candidate) must satisfy a reward floor and the batch of images must satisfy a diversity cutoff. The reward floor controls the balance between worst-candidate reward and batch diversity; we show that varying this floor defines a Pareto frontier. To traverse this frontier, we introduce SatisDive, a training-free inference-time method. SatisDive uses a batch-relative reward cutoff to distinguish lower- from higher-reward candidates, emphasizing reward improvement for candidates below the cutoff and diversity among candidates above it. On Pick-a-Pic, at matched DreamSim, SatisDive improves worst-candidate reward over FK steering by up to 0.43 with FLUX.1-dev as the base model and HPSv3 as the reward, and by up to 0.70 with SANA-1.6B as the base model and ImageReward as the reward. More broadly, across their overlapping DreamSim ranges, SatisDive's satisfaction-diversity curve Pareto-dominates FK steering's curve in each setting.

</details>


### [207] [THPL: A Vision-to-Language Decision Support Framework for Rainbow Trout Feeding Management in RAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02378)
*Meng Liang,Guanbo Feng,Haozhuang Chi,Shilong Zhao,Zhixin Xiong,Yuhang He,Wenfeng Han,Tianhao Zhao,Zhihong Ma,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出THPL生成式投喂决策框架，通过鱼类轨迹与LLM推理的融合，为循环水养殖中的虹鳟精准投喂提供可解释决策支持，决策准确率从33.33%提升至96.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环水养殖系统（RAS）中精准投喂对降低成本、提升鱼类福利至关重要，但现有方法缺乏鱼类行为与管理知识之间的认知对齐，难以转化为可执行、可解释的投喂决策。

Method: THPL框架分三步：1）Fishsort提取轨迹并构建活动系数（AC）以量化摄食强度；2）分层行为编码器（HBE）利用时序Transformer与集合Transformer建模个体时序演进与群体动态，将轨迹张量转化为显式物理与隐式软令牌的双证据表示；3）将令牌与环境参数、元数据和专家规则结合，通过LoRA微调LLM，并用反事实多模态直接偏好优化（mDPO）强化因果推理。

Result: 活动系数AC与专家标注摄食强度呈显著单调正相关（Spearman ρ=0.925，p<0.001）。消融实验显示决策准确率从纯文本基线的33.33%提升到双证据令牌的93.33%；相比标准LoRA，反事实mDPO将准确率从93.33%提升到96.67%，METEOR从58.10%升至85.30%，Self-BLEU-2从58.79%降至52.88%，Distinct-3从6.68%升至7.81%。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将连续运动学与LLM推理相结合，抑制了模板化与执行偏差，增强了因果一致性与操作安全性，为精准水产养殖提供了新的决策支持范式。

Abstract: In Recirculating Aquaculture Systems (RAS), precision feeding is critical for minimizing costs and improving fish welfare. However, existing methods lack cognitive alignment between fish behaviors and management knowledge, impeding translation into executable, interpretable feeding decisions. To address this, we propose THPL, a generative feeding decision framework tailored for rainbow trout (Oncorhynchus mykiss) in RAS. First, Fishsort extracts trajectories to establish an Activity Coefficient (AC) quantifying feeding intensity. Second, a Hierarchical Behavior Encoder (HBE) models individual temporal progression and collective dynamics using Temporal and Set Transformers, transforming trajectory tensors into dual-evidence representations of explicit physical and implicit soft tokens. Finally, these tokens are integrated with environmental parameters, metadata, and expert rules to fine-tune an LLM via LoRA, followed by counterfactual multimodal Direct Preference Optimization (mDPO) to reinforce causal reasoning. Results show that AC exhibits a statistically significant monotonic positive correlation with expert-annotated feeding intensity (Spearman $ρ= 0.925$, $p < 0.001$). Ablations indicate that decision accuracy improves from 33.33% (text-only baseline) to 93.33% with dual-evidence tokens, confirming that continuous spatiotemporal tokens provide necessary physical grounding for LLMs. Compared with standard LoRA, counterfactual mDPO elevates decision accuracy from 93.33% to 96.67%, advances METEOR from 58.10% to 85.30%, reduces Self-BLEU-2 from 58.79% to 52.88%, and increases Distinct-3 from 6.68% to 7.81%, suppressing templating and actuation biases while reinforcing causal consistency and operational safety. Overall, by integrating continuous kinematics with LLM reasoning, this study provides a novel decision support paradigm for precision aquaculture.

</details>


### [208] [FlashSinkhorn 2: Block-Sparse Entropic Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02395)
*Felix X. -F. Ye,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Naigang Wang,Davis Wertheimer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FlashSinkhorn 2（FS2），通过粗粒度质心求解与块稀疏细粒度阶段耦合，在单 GPU 上高效求解大规模离散熵正则最优传输问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式 GPU 求解器（如 FlashSinkhorn）虽避免存储稠密核，但每次 Sinkhorn 迭代仍需计算全部 n×m 个点对，代价高，难以扩展到超大规模问题。

Method: 针对低维点云上的平方欧氏代价，采用两阶段：粗阶段在单元质心上求解并将势函数提升到每个点，当采样边际检查拒绝该提升时继续在质心上迭代，替代大部分点级更新；细阶段采用块稀疏方法消除质心误差。Morton 排序块支持筛选与融合张量核执行，阈值由块质量设定，限定每个被省略瓦片对各行与各列的贡献。

Result: 在合成基准上，FS2 在全部 32 个问题达到目标残差，GeomLoss 多尺度达到 10 个。单张 A100 上，FS2 在 2.5 小时内求解两个 1.34×10^8 粒子宇宙 N 体模拟测度间的离散 EOT（熵模糊等于平均粒子间距），全粒子边际残差低于 0.01，为据所知数小时内达到该精度的最大离散 EOT 问题。

Conclusion: FS2 显著提升了大规模离散熵正则最优传输的可扩展性与可解精度，并开源实现以支持复现。

Abstract: Streaming GPU solvers for entropic optimal transport (EOT), such as FlashSinkhorn, avoid storing the dense kernel but still evaluate all $n\times m$ point pairs in every Sinkhorn iteration. We present \textbf{FlashSinkhorn~2} (FS2), a solver for squared-Euclidean cost on low-dimensional point clouds that solves large discrete EOT problems to a prescribed marginal residual on a single GPU by coupling two stages. A coarse stage solves on cell centroids, lifts the potentials to every point and, when a sampled marginal check rejects the lift, continues on the centroids, replacing most point-level updates. A block-sparse fine stage then removes the centroid error that coarse updates cannot. Its Morton-ordered blocks support screening and fused tensor-core execution, and a threshold set by the block masses bounds each omitted tile's contribution to every row and column. On synthetic benchmarks, FS2 reaches the target residual on all 32 problems and GeomLoss multiscale on 10. On one A100, FS2 solves discrete EOT between two $1.34\times10^8$-particle measures from a cosmological $N$-body simulation, at an entropic blur equal to the mean interparticle distance, to an all-particle marginal residual below 0.01 in under 2.5 hours. To our knowledge, it is the largest discrete EOT problem solved to this accuracy within hours. For reproducibility, we release an open-source implementation at https://github.com/ot-triton-lab/flash-sinkhorn

</details>


### [209] [DataWeave: Deploying Human-LLM Analytics for Exploratory Structured Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02679)
*Raquib Bin Yousuf,Harith Laxman,Vitaliy Shkremetko,Eunice Son,Shambhavi Verma,Brian O'Leary,Venketesh Subramony,Sylvain Nazef,Jacquelyn Elias,Ron Coddington,Chris Contakes,Michael Riley,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataWeave 是一个面向数据新闻的交互式分析系统，通过对话交互、模式接地、分析规划与可执行查询生成，将 LLM 定位为可检查、可纠正、可引导的分析伙伴，而非自主问答引擎；并以 IPEDS 数据集的记者案例研究提炼人机协作设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据新闻依赖记者在大规模结构化数据中发现趋势、差异与问责线索，但实际流程缓慢且脆弱：需跨多年、多数据集处理数百个变量，理解编码约定，并在假设不断演变时编写复杂分析代码。LLM 虽被宣传为“用英语提问即得 SQL/答案”，但在真实新闻编辑室中频繁出现模式不匹配与漂移、领域语义与单位误读、隐性假设等失败。

Method: 提出 DataWeave 系统，融合会话式交互、模式接地（schema grounding）、分析规划与可执行查询生成，支持结构化数据上的探索性分析。系统不把 LLM 当作自主答案引擎，而是作为可检查、可纠正、可随假设变化而引导的交互伙伴。采用专业记者使用该系统分析美国教育部 IPEDS（具有强领域语义且模式频繁更新）的案例研究，并报告部署经验与迭代优化如何塑造其架构与分析工作流。

Result: 通过专业记者在 IPEDS 高风险公共数据集上的案例研究，展示了系统在真实新闻编辑室探索性分析中的使用情况；同时总结了部署经验与迭代改进对 DataWeave 当前架构及分析工作流的影响。

Conclusion: 研究提炼出在结构化数据分析中实现可信人-LLM 协作的设计原则与部署经验，强调将 LLM 作为可监督、可引导的交互伙伴而非全自动答案生成器。

Abstract: Data journalism, the practice of using data analysis to surface newsworthy stories, depends increasingly on the ability of reporters and investigative journalists to uncover trends, disparities, and accountability narratives. In practice, exploring large structured datasets remains slow and brittle: journalists must navigate hundreds of variables across many datasets over years, understand data coding conventions, and write non-trivial analysis code while hypotheses evolve. Although LLMs are often touted as "ask in English, get SQL/answers," real newsroom workflows expose recurring failures, e.g., schema mismatches and drift, misread domain semantics and units, and silent assumptions. We present DataWeave, a system that addresses these needs by combining conversational interaction, schema grounding, analytical planning, and executable query generation to support exploratory analysis over structured data. Rather than treating LLMs as autonomous answer engines, DataWeave frames them as interactive partners whose outputs can be inspected, corrected, and steered as hypotheses shift. We present a case study with professional journalists using our system to analyze the U.S. Department of Education's Integrated Postsecondary Education Data System (IPEDS), a high-stakes public dataset with substantial domain semantics and frequent schema updates. We also report how deployment experience and iterative refinement shaped the current DataWeave architecture and its analytical workflow. Our findings distill design principles and deployment lessons for trustworthy human-LLM collaboration in structured data analysis.

</details>


### [210] [When Terminal-Agent Training Stalls: Demystifying Data Generation and Verification Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02405)
*Xi Qin,Isabel Kurth,Xin Cui,Elin Park,Alexander Schaefer,Yaad Oren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用前沿模型（如Claude Opus）作为元智能体为RL训练生成终端任务与验证器时，仅"能跑通"并不等于流水线可靠。本文提出一套元智能体流水线，诊断出三类失败并发现可解性区间是模型特定的，需将可解性校准、验证器审计与基础设施错误核算作为一级评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用前沿模型充当元智能体来生成终端任务及验证器用于强化学习训练已日益普遍，但"可运行的Docker镜像+可执行测试套件"并不保证端到端训练流水线是忠实可靠的，存在被忽视的失效风险。

Method: 提出一套由上述差距驱动的元智能体流水线，系统诊断并归类三类失败：基准无效性（benchmark invalidity）、评测框架脆弱性（harness brittleness）与奖励错配（reward misalignment）。通过提示重设计与上下文扩展提升基线可解性，并引入困难任务以测试可解性区间的模型依赖性。

Result: 提示重设计与上下文扩展使基线可解性提升约5.6倍；但9B模型在Claude Opus生成任务上、20步内即达到81.3%的平均pass@2饱和。在不改变训练配置的前提下加入困难任务后，平均pass@2降至20.6%，有力证明可解性区间是模型特定的。

Conclusion: 元智能体的可靠性要求把可解性区间校准、验证器审计与基础设施错误核算作为一级评估标准，而非事后的诊断手段。

Abstract: Using a frontier model like Claude Opus as a meta-agent to generate terminal tasks and verifiers for RL training is increasingly common. Yet a runnable Docker image and executable test suite do not guarantee a faithful end-to-end pipeline for terminal agent training. We present a meta-agent pipeline motivated by this gap, diagnosing three classes of failure: benchmark invalidity, harness brittleness, and reward misalignment. Prompt redesign and context extension raise baseline solvability 5.6 times, but a 9B model saturates at 81.3% mean pass@2 within 20 steps on Claude Opus-generated tasks. Adding hard tasks reduces mean pass@2 to 20.6% without changing the training configuration, a strong evidence that the solvability band is model-specific. These findings demonstrate that meta-agent reliability requires solvability-band calibration, verifier audits, and infrastructure error accounting as first-class evaluation criteria, not post-hoc diagnost.

</details>


### [211] [Personalized Automatic Speech Recognition for a Dysarthric and Tracheostomic Speaker using Artificial Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03017)
*David Nadrchal,Monorama Swain,Florian Schmid,Gerhard Widmer,Paul Primus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究为一位带有永久气管造口且患有严重构音障碍的捷克语使用者构建了个性化语音识别系统，公开33小时数据集并提出基于Whisper Base的多阶段训练流程，字符错误率相对降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该使用者的语音对未受训练的听者而言难以理解，现有通用ASR系统难以有效识别此类严重受损语音，因此需要构建个性化ASR以帮助其沟通。

Method: 公开包含33小时标注语音的数据集，采用新型"人工对话"协议采集以保证高参与度和对话真实性；基于Whisper Base提出多阶段训练流程：在标准捷克语语音、声学模拟的气管造口语音以及该使用者数据上依次微调。

Result: 在脚本对话、问答和自发对话三种近实时场景下评估，相比Whisper Base基线字符错误率相对降低50%，在孤立语句的声学识别上超过其助手们的平均识别准确率。

Conclusion: 即使对于严重受损语音，也可实现有用的ASR系统，定量结果与该使用者的反馈共同证明了这一点。

Abstract: This work presents an automatic speech recognition (ASR) system personalized for a Czech speaker with a permanent tracheal stoma and severe dysarthria rendering their speech unintelligible to untrained listeners. We release a public dataset containing 33 annotated hours of the speaker's speech, collected using a novel "artificial conversation" protocol designed for high engagement and dialogue realism. We propose a multi-stage training pipeline based on Whisper Base: fine-tuning on standard Czech speech, acoustically simulated tracheostomic speech, and the speaker's data. We evaluate the system across three near real-time scenarios: scripted conversations, question answering, and spontaneous dialogue, achieving a 50\% relative reduction in Character Error Rate compared to Whisper Base baseline and surpassing the average recognition accuracy of their assistants in acoustic recognition of isolated utterances. We demonstrate that even for severely impeded speech, a helpful ASR is achievable, as evidenced by the quantitative results and the feedback from the speaker.

</details>


### [212] [Reinforcement Learning Techniques for the Optimization of Target Polarization in Nuclear Physics Scattering Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02452)
*Armen Kasparian,Torri Jeske,Monibor Rahman,Chris Keith,James Maxwell,Thomas Britton,Malachi Schram,David Lawrence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结合代理模型与强化学习的数据驱动控制框架，用于动态极化靶的微波频率调优，基于APOLLO低温靶运行数据训练高斯过程与MLP模型，并将代理模型嵌入标准化仿真环境，采用下置信界奖励训练RL智能体，实现约2倍于人工操作的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态极化靶运行中需持续调节微波频率以补偿辐射损伤和材料特性变化，传统上依赖专家人工试错，效率低且成本高，因此需要自动化的数据驱动控制方法来优化靶极化。

Method: 利用APOLLO低温靶系统的运行数据，训练并评估多层感知机（MLP）和高斯过程回归模型，以微波频率、束流和累积辐射剂量为输入预测极化；引入高斯过程近似方法并嵌入标准化仿真环境，训练采用下置信界（LCB）奖励公式的强化学习智能体，在性能最大化与不确定性之间取得平衡。

Result: 高斯过程模型能提供校准的不确定性估计并可靠识别训练分布之外的区域，而MLP对分布偏移的敏感性有限；基于LCB奖励训练的强化学习智能体相对于操作员的动作实现了近2倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结合高斯过程代理模型与考虑不确定性的强化学习，可有效实现动态极化靶的自动化调优，显著优于传统人工试错，具备在核物理实验中进行实际部署的潜力。

Abstract: The operation of dynamically polarized targets in nuclear physics experiments relies on continuous tuning of the microwave frequency to compensate for radiation damage and evolving material properties, a task that is traditionally performed through manual trial-and-error by expert operators. This work presents a data-driven control framework that combines surrogate modeling with reinforcement learning to optimize the target polarization. Using operational data from the APOLLO cryogenic target system, we train and evaluate multilayer perceptron and Gaussian process regression models to predict polarization as a function of microwave frequency, beam current, and accumulated radiation dose. We show that Gaussian process-based models provide calibrated uncertainty estimates and reliably identify regions outside the training distribution, while MLPs exhibit limited sensitivity to distributional shift. To enable learning and control across multiple target samples, we introduce a Gaussian process approximation and embed the surrogate model within a standardized simulation environment. A reinforcement learning agent is trained using a lower-confidence-bound reward formulation that balances performance maximization against uncertainty. We are able to show an almost 2x improvement on the operators actions utilizing our RL agent.

</details>


### [213] [Tropical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02478)
*Arip Asadulaev,Aladin Djuhera,Karim Salta,Holger Boche,Fakhri Karray,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tropical Reinforcement Learning，用最大值运算替代概率求和的经典期望回报，将状态价值定义为最可能验证解的对数概率及其可重放路径，并据此设计训练算法TROPIC，在四个智能体任务上比最强同策略基线最高提升16个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
