Conclusion: 蛋白质序列信息与文本上下文结合可有效改善LLM基因集注释，但其作用具有领域依赖性。

Abstract: Gene set analysis is a cornerstone of functional genomics, yet it remains labor-intensive and heavily dependent on manual curation and expert biological interpretation. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for genomic reasoning and annotation, most existing approaches rely on symbolic gene names and fail to capture domain-specific biological structure, particularly protein sequence information that governs molecular activity, interactions, and downstream gene function. In this work, we propose SoftGene, a novel framework for LLM-based gene set annotation that leverages the hierarchical structure of gene sets. First, we use a hierarchical attention-based encoder built on ESM, a protein language model, to represent each gene set using protein-level amino acid sequence information. Second, we construct a hybrid prompting scheme that combines soft prompts derived from gene set embeddings with hard prompts containing auxiliary context generated by an LLM, and feed the resulting prompt into a local LLM for annotation. We evaluate our framework on two benchmark datasets: Gene Ontology (GO) and the Molecular Signatures Database (MSigDB). Our results show that integrating protein-sequence representations with textual context improves gene set annotation overall, while per-domain analyses reveal that the contribution of protein embeddings varies across biological domains.

</details>


### [280] [When Numbers Start Talking: Numerical Signalling and Strategic Behaviour Among LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03033)
*Alessio Buscemi,Daniele Proverbio,Alessandro Di Stefano,The Anh Han,German Castignani,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究不同消息类型（自然语言、数值信号、随机序列）如何影响LLM多智能体在策略博弈中的合作水平，发现结构化消息会改变最终收益但无规律，且智能体生成的数值消息偏离随机性，可为监测AI协调提供消息层面的指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体越来越多地在多智能体系统中进行策略互动，但人们对不同类型的消息是否以及多大程度影响策略博弈的结果知之甚少。作者希望探究消息类型与智能体人格对合作均衡的影响，并检验"具备额外能力后AI智能体仍能收敛到稳定均衡"这一假设是否成立。

Method: 让基于四种主流LLM的AI智能体参与四种具有不同合作均衡的博弈；向智能体注入不同种类的消息（自然语言、数值信号或随机序列），并结合智能体被赋予的人格设定，测量每种博弈中合作水平的变化；同时分析智能体生成的数值消息的符号分布特征，检验其是否偏离随机性。

Result: 结构化消息在大多数博弈和LLM中都改变了最终收益，但没有可预测的模式，这挑战了AI智能体不依赖额外能力即可收敛到稳定均衡的假设。智能体生成的数值消息偏离随机性，在明确指示其进行交流时这种偏离最强且最一致；但其符号分布主要与收益结构相关，并随重复趋于集中，总体难以被人类解读，带来了额外的可解释性挑战。

Conclusion: 监测通过受限信道进行的AI智能体协调，应优先关注消息层面的指纹（其在模型间具有泛化性），而非行为决策层面的特征（后者不具备泛化性）。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly operate in multi-agent systems (MAS) characterised by strategic interaction. However, little is known about whether, and to what extent, different types of messages affect the outcomes of strategic games. By investigating AI agents based on four popular LLMs, playing four games with different cooperation equilibria, we study whether messages of different kinds (natural language, numerical signals, or random sequences) significantly modify the levels of cooperation in each game, also depending on the agents' assigned personalities. We observe that structured messages alter the final payoffs for most games and LLMs, but without a predictable pattern; this challenges the assumption that AI agents can converge to stable equilibria regardless of additional capabilities. Moreover, we observe that agent-generated numerical messages depart from randomness, most strongly and consistently when agents are explicitly instructed to communicate; however, they introduce an additional interpretability challenge, as their symbol distributions are mostly associated with the payoff structure and typically become more concentrated with repetition, but are overall difficult for humans to interpret. Monitoring for coordination of AI agents through restricted channels should thus prioritise message-level fingerprints, which generalise across models, over behavioural decisions, which do not.

</details>


### [281] [hacktrace: behavior-supervised detection of reward hacking during code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03055)
*Hao Jiang,Xin Li,Annan Wang,Yichi Zhang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发布173,561条多轮编码轨迹，提出HACKTRACE行为监督监控器，通过复用智能体生成代码时的内部状态，结合最终文件静态特征，实现在8毫秒开销下AUC达0.997的捷径行为检测，并可作为强化学习的高效监控信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可通过修复代码或删除暴露缺陷的测试来获得及格成绩，这属于奖励黑客行为（reward hacking）。检测此类捷径（包括失败尝试）需要识别故意抄近路的意图性行为。现有方法需要重新运行模型进行"诚实性"问答，效率低且准确率不足，因此需要一种既准确又低开销的监控方式，同时探索这种检测能否转化为有用的训练信号。

Method: 1) 发布来自Qwen3-8B的173,561条带标注多轮编码轨迹，将捷径行为监督与利用是否成功解耦监督；2) 提出HACKTRACE，一种行为监督监控器，读取智能体生成代码时已计算的内部状态，无需额外语言模型token或前向传递即可在回合完成前监控；3) 将内部状态证据与最终文件的静态特征结合；4) 在强化学习中用该信号施加GRPO惩罚。

Result: 结合内部状态与静态特征后，平均每题AUC达0.997，监控开销仅8毫秒，在准确率和延迟上均优于需重新运行模型回答诚实性问题的监控器。在强GRPO惩罚下，HACKTRACE将及格解中的作弊比例从82-91%降至1-5%，同时保留诚实正确的解，并在策略演化过程中保持高检测准确率。

Conclusion: 监督目标（独立于利用成功的行为监督）与监控证据来源（智能体已计算的生成状态）二者对将准确检测转化为有用的训练信号都至关重要。

Abstract: A coding agent can earn a passing grade by fixing its code, or by deleting the test that exposes the bug. Detecting such reward hacking requires recognizing attempted shortcuts, including those that fail. We release 173,561 annotated multi-turn coding trajectories from Qwen3-8B and show that supervising shortcut behavior independently of exploit success substantially improves detection. We introduce HACKTRACE, a behavior-supervised monitor that reads the internal states the agent already computes while generating code. Reusing these states enables monitoring before a turn is complete, without additional language-model tokens or passes. Combining this evidence with static features of the final files achieves a mean per-problem AUC of 0.997 with 8 ms of monitoring overhead, improving both accuracy and latency over monitors that run the model again on an honesty question and answer. The same generation states also provide an inexpensive monitoring signal for reinforcement learning. With strong GRPO penalties, HACKTRACE reduces the cheating share of passing solutions from 82-91% to 1-5%, while retaining honest, correct solutions and maintaining high detection accuracy as the policy evolves. Our results show that both the supervision target and the source of monitoring evidence matter for turning accurate detection into a useful training signal.

</details>


### [282] [MOF-VERIFY: A Failure-Aware Agentic Harness for MOF Hypothesis Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03056)
*Donghyun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Geonhee Ahn,Jieun Kim,Jihyun Park,Suyeon Cho,Yoona Kim,Chaerim Shin,Hoi Ri Moon,Jonggeol Na,Sukho Hong,Jihwan Oh,Soo Kyung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOF-Verify诊断基准与失败感知智能体框架，用于评估和提升LLM在MOF假设验证中的可靠性，显著优于直接推理和检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地作为AI材料Co-Scientist的推理组件，但其验证流程的可靠性尚不明确。MOF结构标识多样、合成结果依赖实验条件、证据来源异构、部分假设需计算验证，构成极具挑战的场景，亟需诊断验证pipeline的失败模式。

Method: 提出含四类任务族的诊断基准：结构锚定、合成条件验证、证据充分性验证、基于MLIP的计算验证。T-MOF-1-3在闭卷、启用检索、oracle-evidence三种设置下评估，以定位知识获取、证据获取与推理的失败；T-MOF-4单独评估计算验证。基于诊断出的失败模式，开发MOF-Verify失败感知智能体框架，在给出最终判定前针对结构、文献、证据充分性与计算瓶颈逐一处理。

Result: 在多个骨干LLM上，MOF-Verify在假设验证性能上大幅优于直接推理与检索基线。基准数据集已开源于指定GitHub仓库。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可诊断的MOF验证基准与失败感知的智能体框架，提升了LLM材料假设验证的可靠性，并公开数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as reasoning components in AI-driven materials Co-Scientists, yet the reliability of the resulting verification pipeline remains unclear. Metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) provide a particularly challenging setting because structures may appear under different identifiers, synthesis outcomes depend strongly on experimental conditions, evidence is distributed across heterogeneous sources, and some hypotheses require computation rather than literature alone. We introduce a diagnostic benchmark with four task families covering structural grounding, synthesis-condition verification, evidence-sufficiency verification, and MLIP-based computational verification. T-MOF-1-3 are evaluated under closed-book, retrieval-enabled, and oracle-evidence settings to localize failures in knowledge access, evidence acquisition, and reasoning, while T-MOF-4 separately evaluates computational verification. Guided by these diagnosed failure modes, we develop MOF-Verify, a failure-aware agentic harness that targets structural, literature, evidence-sufficiency, and computational bottlenecks before producing a final verdict. Across multiple backbone LLMs, MOF-Verify substantially improves hypothesis-verification performance over direct inference and retrieval-based baselines. Benchmark datasets are released at https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MOF-Verify-Benchmark.

</details>


### [283] [RIFAR: Reliability and Forgetting-Aware Replay for Continual Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03079)
*Zirong Song,Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIFAR：面向世界-动作模型的可靠性筛选与漂移感知重放选择，实现持续学习中的抗遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需将连续真实经验转化为可迁移技能，但持续学习中新任务会侵蚀旧知识；完整示教回放存储成本高，世界-动作模型生成式回放虽省存储，却存在动作无法实现预测转移、以及新任务适应破坏旧行为的可靠性问题。

Method: RIFAR结合可靠性筛选与漂移感知的重放选择：从紧凑示教前缀重建轨迹，用冻结的逆动力学模型评估动作-视觉一致性；训练先用当前示教与最高质量筛选轨迹结合；再在相同历史输入上比较适应前后的动作预测，从筛选池中重选归一化漂移更大的轨迹继续训练。

Result: 在三个LIBERO套件与真实世界实验中，RIFAR超越此前基于WAM生成式回放的最优方法；LIBERO-Goal上取得90.97 AUC，每任务仅保留约320个历史时间步，约为50示教回放所保留步数的4.9%。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性筛选与漂移感知重放选择，RIFAR在极低存储开销下有效缓解持续学习中的遗忘，优于现有WAM生成式回放方法。

Abstract: Genuine embodied agency requires robots to turn continuous real-world experience into lasting, transferable skills. This demands continual learning that integrates new capabilities without eroding prior knowledge as tasks and environments evolve. Experience replay mitigates forgetting, but storing complete demonstrations becomes costly as tasks accumulate. World-action models offer a generative alternative, reconstructing past experience through joint predictions of actions and future observations. However, visually coherent rollouts may contain actions that cannot realize the predicted transitions, while new-task adaptation can disrupt previously learned behavior. RIFAR therefore combines reliability screening with drift-aware replay selection. It reconstructs trajectories from compact demonstration prefixes and uses a frozen inverse-dynamics model to assess action-visual consistency. Training first combines current demonstrations with the highest-quality screened trajectories. RIFAR then compares action predictions before and after this adaptation on identical historical inputs, reselecting trajectories with larger normalized drift from the same screened pool for continued training. Across three LIBERO suites and real-world experiments, RIFAR surpasses the previous state of the art in WAM-based generative replay. On LIBERO-Goal, it achieves 90.97 AUC while retaining only 320 historical time steps per task, approximately 4.9% of the steps retained using 50-demonstration replay.

</details>


### [284] [Peer Influence across Heterogeneous AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03095)
*Frida Nøhr Laustsen,Marie Haahr Petersen,Victoria Popa,Ariel Flint,Romualdo Pastor-Satorras,Andrea Baronchelli,Luca Maria Aiello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究测量多智能体系统中AI模型间的说服效应，发现说服力强且无法由模型规模或孤立确定性预测，主要取决于听众的易感性及具体模型配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统日益混合不同家族与规模的模型，当两个AI智能体意见分歧时，谁说服谁将决定哪些判断在交互后存活，因此需要量化这种说服动态。

Method: 将说服定义为智能体在与持异议同伴单次交流后决策的概率性偏移，测试七个开放权重模型在三个语言理解任务上的表现，分析确定性与模型规模对说服动态的影响。

Result: 说服效应强，接收者常放弃原判断；孤立时决策高度一致的模型也可能极易被说服，小模型作为说服者与抵抗影响的能力可媲美大模型；偏移大小更取决于听众易感性而非说者说服力；说服模式因模型配对而异，小模型可推翻大模型判断。

Conclusion: 交互模型的行为无法从其个体属性推断，必须在实际组合中评估，异质性会放大或抑制说服效果。

Abstract: When two AI agents disagree, who persuades whom? As multi-agent systems increasingly combine language models of different families and sizes, the answer can determine which judgments survive interaction. Measuring persuasion as the probabilistic shift in an agent's decision after a single exchange with a dissenting peer, we test seven open-weight models across three language understanding tasks. We find that persuasion is strong: when models disagree, receivers often abandon their initial judgment after seeing a peer's answer and explanation. Surprisingly, however, neither standalone certainty nor model scale reliably predicts persuasion dynamics. Models producing almost perfectly consistent decisions in isolation can be among the most susceptible to persuasion, and small models can match larger ones as persuaders and resist their influence just as effectively. Furthermore, we show that the size of the shift depends more on the susceptibility of the listener than on the persuasiveness of the speaker. Persuasion patterns are therefore specific to each model pairing, with heterogeneity amplifying persuasion in some combinations and suppressing it in others, allowing a dissenting agent running a small model to overturn the judgments of a much larger one. These findings show that the behavior of interacting models cannot be inferred from their individual properties but must be evaluated in the combinations in which they will operate.

</details>


### [285] [Predictor-Guided Latent Space Codon Optimization for Maximizing Protein Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03098)
*Alberto Caron,Tianyu Cui,Dmytro S. Lituiev,Mangal Prakash,Artem Moskalev,Amina Mollaysa,Bo Zhai,Hirsh Nanda,Daniel M. Poole,Zhongyin Liu,Iman Farasat,Robert Davidson,Nikolay V. Manyakov,Tommaso Mansi,Scott Oloff,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出LSCO方法，将离散的密码子优化问题通过预训练mRNA语言模型的潜在空间转化为连续优化问题，实现基于梯度的搜索，并在湿实验抗体表达数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码子优化对治疗性蛋白生产和mRNA疫苗至关重要，但设计空间离散且组合规模庞大，难以使用梯度方法；现有工具依赖启发式代理指标（如CAI或GC含量），无法准确反映真实表达水平。

Method: LSCO将序列映射到预训练mRNA语言模型的潜在空间，把离散问题重构为连续问题以支持高效梯度搜索。该方法包含四个组件：来自不确定性感知预测器的数据驱动表达目标、用于结构稳定性的最小自由能正则项、来自蛋白到密码子反翻译模型的自然性先验，以及保证蛋白保真度的约束解码。

Result: 在真实湿实验抗体表达数据集上，LSCO在预测表达方面优于简单的频率基线和现代深度生成基线，同时保持了合适的生物物理性质。

Conclusion: LSCO通过潜在空间连续化有效解决了密码子优化难题，结合表达目标、结构稳定性、自然性先验和蛋白保真约束，为治疗性蛋白与mRNA疫苗设计提供了有前景的优化框架。

Abstract: Codon optimization, the process of selecting synonymous codons to improve mRNA translation efficiency and protein expression, is central to therapeutic protein production and mRNA vaccines, yet it remains a hard problem. The design space is discrete and combinatorially large, precluding gradient-based methods, and existing tools rely on heuristic proxies (e.g., Codon Adaptation Index or GC-content) that poorly capture true expression. We introduce Latent-Space Codon Optimization (LSCO), which recasts this discrete problem as a continuous one by mapping sequences into the latent space of a pretrained mRNA language model, enabling efficient gradient-based search. LSCO combines four components: a data-driven expression objective from an uncertainty-aware predictor, a Minimum-Free-Energy regularizer for structural stability, a naturalness prior from a protein-to-codon back-translation model, and constrained decoding for protein fidelity. On a real-world, wet-lab antibody expression dataset, LSCO outperforms simple frequency-based, as well as modern deep generative baselines in predicted expression, while retaining suitable biophysical properties.

</details>


### [286] [Trading Strategy Optimization via Textual Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03128)
*Chaoqun Yang,Qian Wang,Fengbin Zhu,Xinyu Lin,Bingsheng He,Roger Zimmermann,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TradeGrad，一个经验引导的文本梯度框架，用于鲁棒的量化交易策略优化，通过积累经验估计梯度并引入跨期鲁棒目标(CPRO)提升时序稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的文本梯度为量化交易策略迭代优化提供了显式方向，但直接应用面临两大挑战：(1)优化短视，未能充分利用历史评估经验；(2)聚合回测反馈忽视时序鲁棒性，可能偏好仅在特定市场时期表现良好的策略。

Method: TradeGrad利用累积的优化经验估计文本梯度，并采用多尺度修订进行策略探索与精炼；同时引入跨期鲁棒目标(CPRO)，强调在不利历史时期的表现以促进时序鲁棒性。

Result: 在中国A股和美国股票市场的横截面与时序策略设计中，TradeGrad在全部四个设置中取得最佳的样本内和样本外表现。其中国横截面策略年化收益27.99%、最大回撤12.19%、夏普比率1.63，较CSI 300基准高约68%。

Conclusion: 进一步分析验证了各组件有效性，并显示优化过程中样本内外表现持续提升，证明经验引导与跨期鲁棒目标的结合能有效优化稳健的量化交易策略。

Abstract: Quantitative trading strategy design aims to discover trading programs from historical data that remain effective in future markets, which can be viewed as a black-box program optimization problem. LLM-based textual gradients offer a promising approach by providing explicit optimization directions for iterative strategy refinement. However, directly applying textual gradients faces two challenges: (1) optimization is myopic, underutilizing experience from previous evaluations; and (2) aggregate backtest feedback overlooks temporal robustness, potentially favoring strategies that perform well only in specific market periods. To address these challenges, we propose TradeGrad, an experience-guided textual-gradient framework for robust trading strategy optimization. TradeGrad leverages accumulated optimization experience to estimate textual gradients and employs multi-scale revisions for both strategy exploration and refinement. It further introduces the Cross-Period Robust Objective (CPRO), which emphasizes performance in unfavorable historical periods to promote temporal robustness. Experiments on cross-sectional and time-series strategy design in Chinese A-share and U.S. equity markets show that TradeGrad achieves the best in-sample and out-of-sample performance across all four settings. Notably, its Chinese cross-sectional strategy achieves 27.99% annualized return, 12.19% maximum drawdown, and a Sharpe ratio of 1.63, approximately 68% higher than the CSI 300 benchmark. Further analyses validate the proposed components and show consistent improvements in both in-sample and out-of-sample performance throughout optimization. The code is available at https://github.com/transcend-0/TradeGrad.

</details>


### [287] [Keeping JEPA World Models Plannable When Little of the Frame Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03137)
*Florian Strohm,Patrick Wagner,Jannik Schwab,Marco Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SLIM基准（含多个小物体及配对视觉/语言目标的推动任务），发现LeWM世界模型在PushT上成功但在SLIM上失败率超99%，根因在编码器潜空间对动作不敏感；加入逆动力学辅助损失可修复该问题并将成功率从0.003提升至0.35，还能让小型语言目标头在无需重训世界模型的情况下完成语言规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用自然语言而非目标帧指定目标，是基于潜世界模型进行规划的天然接口，但验证这一能力需要语言必须在多个物体间做出区分的场景。现有基准缺乏这种成对的视觉与语言目标设置，因此作者构建SLIM来测试语言条件规划。

Method: 构建SLIM基准：同一场景中放置多个小物体，提供配对的视觉目标与语言目标。在该基准上评估LeWM世界模型（其潜空间来自编码器），并设计一系列探针（probe）诊断失败来源。核心修复手段为一项逆动力学辅助损失，作用于编码器潜变量，并通过一个在测试时丢弃的共享头作用于预测潜变量。此外训练一个小型语言目标头，在不重训世界模型的情况下基于句子做规划；并设计廉价的动作敏感性探针作为无需环境访问的经验性必要条件。

Result: LeWM在SLIM上成功率不足1%（脚本控制器用模拟器状态可解所有层级）。探针显示失败源于编码器：潜空间几乎对动作不敏感，推杆与物体位置均无法解码，rollout不比直接复制当前潜变量更好。加入逆动力学辅助损失后所有探针恢复正常，成功率从0.003升至0.35（困难推动层级为0.16，此时无目标策略得分为0），并在两倍训练视野下改善PushT。控制实验表明修复来自流入编码器的梯度；响应扫描显示原版模型在画面足够多部分对动作有响应时即可规划。修复后的潜空间上，小型语言目标头在导航任务达0.84（视觉目标oracle为1.00），在命名区域被诱饵替换时能跟随命名区域，并面对未见名词时优雅退化。单句目标很少完成一次推动，但将推动作为阶段句序列给出时，该头在中、难推动层级成功率从0.04升至0.25，与目标帧oracle相当，且阶段间切换也能仅从潜变量读出。

Conclusion: 潜世界模型在语言条件规划中的失败可定位到编码器对动作不敏感这一具体缺陷，通过逆动力学辅助损失即可修复，并解锁小型语言目标头在不重训世界模型前提下的语言规划能力。动作敏感性探针可作为经验性必要条件，用于预判模型能否成功规划。

Abstract: Specifying a goal in language rather than as a goal frame is a natural interface for planning with a latent world model, but testing it needs scenes in which language must discriminate between several objects. We build SLIM, a pushing benchmark with several small objects and paired visual and language goals on identical scenes. On SLIM a LeWM world model that solves PushT succeeds on under 1% of trials, although a scripted controller with simulator state solves every tier. Probes locate the failure in the encoder: its latent is nearly action-insensitive, neither pusher nor object positions can be decoded from it, and rollouts are no better than copying the current latent forward. One inverse-dynamics auxiliary loss, applied to encoder latents and to predicted latents through a shared head discarded at test time, restores every probe and raises success from 0.003 to 0.35 (0.16 on the hard pushing tier, where a goal-agnostic policy scores zero), and improves PushT at twice the trained horizon. Controls attribute the repair to the gradient into the encoder, and a response sweep shows that the vanilla model plans once enough of the frame responds to actions. A cheap action-sensitivity probe, computable without environment access, acts as an empirical necessary condition: all configurations below its threshold failed to plan. On the repaired latent, a small language-goal head plans from sentences without retraining the world model: it reaches 0.84 on navigation (visual-goal oracle 1.00), follows the named zone when it is swapped with a decoy, and degrades gracefully to unseen nouns. A single goal sentence rarely completes a push, but given the push as a sequence of stage sentences the head raises success on the medium and hard pushing tiers from 0.04 to 0.25, on par with the goal-frame oracle, also when the switch between stages is read from the latent alone.

</details>


### [288] [Gains and Collapse in On-Policy Distillation:A Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03185)
*Han Cui,Jianhao Yan,Yun Luo,Hongbo Zhang,Zhizhang Fu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从强化学习视角解释on-policy distillation（OPD）导致语言模型生成过长、重复文本的崩溃现象，并提出缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD在语言模型后训练中虽有效果，但有时会崩溃为过长、重复的生成，其内在机制尚不清楚。

Method: 将OPD视为教师隐式奖励学生行为的过程，通过实验分析隐式奖励模型可靠与错位时对学生rollout的影响。

Result: 实验表明OPD提升性能但不扩展学生能力：奖励可靠时使正确回答更易采样；偏好与质量错位时出现reward hacking，放大过长重复输出。训练中掩蔽不健康响应及使用SFT初始化均能缓解崩溃。

Conclusion: OPD放大了教师隐式反馈所偏好的学生行为，研究重点应从教师生成质量转向其评估学生rollout的可靠性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an important approach to language model post-training. However, despite its performance gains, OPD can also collapse into excessively long and repetitive generation, and the mechanism underlying these divergent outcomes remains poorly understood. We explain these outcomes through a reinforcement learning perspective: the teacher implicitly rewards student behaviors, even those it rarely exhibits itself. From this perspective, our experiments show that OPD improves performance without expanding the student's capabilities. When the implicit reward model is reliable, OPD makes correct responses easier to sample. In contrast, when the preference misaligns with quality, reward hacking happens: the implicit reward model amplifies overlong, repetitive student rollouts, even though it rarely generates such text itself. Guided by this diagnosis, we find that masking unhealthy responses during training and using SFT initialization can each effectively mitigate the collapse. Together, these findings show that OPD amplifies student behaviors favored by the teacher's implicit feedback, shifting the focus from how well the teacher generates to how reliably it evaluates student rollouts. Our code is available at https://github.com/HancCui/opd_hacking.

</details>


### [289] [KV$^2$: A Self-Refining KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03198)
*Johannes Wesch,Danni Liu,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KV²，一种基于选择性重建的查询无关KV缓存压缩方法，先用轻量代理打分器筛选信息量大的token，再只对这些子集重算最终淘汰分数，从而在紧预算下大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型中KV缓存的内存占用限制了实际应用，且在"一次预填充、多次查询"的可复用场景下主导成本。此场景下查询无关压缩存在质量与成本的权衡：轻量估计器便宜但不够准确，而全上下文重建评分更准确却需重新处理整个提示。

Method: KV² 采用两阶段选择性重建：先用轻量代理打分器识别上下文中信息量大的token，然后仅对该子集进行重处理以计算最终逐出分数，避免对整个上下文重新处理。

Result: 在RULER、Needle-in-a-Haystack和LongBench上，KV²相对基线的优势随预算收紧而扩大：RULER 16K在2% KV缓存预算下平均分比次优基线提高超过40个百分点；LongBench上在2%-10%各预算下取得最高平均分，且压缩阶段运行时间和峰值内存均低于全上下文重建。

Conclusion: 可复用的KV缓存压缩无需重新处理完整上下文，选择性重建即可在更低成本下取得更优质量。

Abstract: The memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache constrains the practical use of long-context models, and it dominates cost when one prefilled context must later serve many different queries. In this reusable setting, query-agnostic compression trades cost against quality: lightweight estimators are cheap but less accurate, whereas full-context reconstruction scoring is more accurate yet reprocesses the entire prompt. We introduce KV$^2$, a query-agnostic KV-cache compression method based on selective reconstruction. KV$^2$ first uses a lightweight proxy scorer to identify informative in-context tokens, then reprocesses only this subset to compute final eviction scores. On RULER, Needle-in-a-Haystack, and LongBench, KV$^2$'s margin over baselines widens as the budget tightens: on RULER 16K at a 2% KV-cache budget it improves the average score over the next-best baseline by more than 40 percentage points, and on LongBench it attains the highest average across 2%-10% budgets at lower compression-stage runtime and peak memory than full-context reconstruction. Reusable KV-cache compression thus does not require reprocessing the full context. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/KVsquared-0B97.

</details>


### [290] [Toward SLM-based agentic task-tool intent matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03213)
*Chiara Troiani,Arash Salarian,Majed El Helou,Benjamin Ryder,Jean Diaconu,Hervé Muyal,Marcelo Yannuzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨小型语言模型（SLM）是否可作为"任务-工具相关性分类器"，对AI智能体的每一次工具调用独立进行相关性验证，以实现低延迟、可本地部署的逐调用监督，并通过提示优化、SFT与GRPO强化学习进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具驱动的AI智能体规模横向扩张，工具调用交互数量激增，亟需自动化、低延迟、可本地部署的逐调用监督。传统授权机制只能判断智能体是否有权调用某工具，无法评估其底层认知，即该工具选择是否是服务于任务意图的合理、相关步骤。因此被授权的调用仍可能偏离任务意图，例如被恶意智能体利用或诱导其他智能体组合出不符合任务意图的调用，所以每次调用都需要被验证。

Method: 将小型语言模型（SLM）用作任务-工具相关性分类器，对每个被选中的工具独立地对照所分配任务进行评估，输出相关性信号供下游执行机制使用。构建了一个新数据集，其中的多工具任务所需工具跨越不同的MCP（模型上下文协议）服务器。随后通过提示优化、监督微调（SFT）以及基于GRPO的强化学习来优化和专门化SLM。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果，仅说明使用了提示优化、监督微调和基于GRPO的强化学习对SLM进行优化与专门化，并配有跨多个MCP服务器的多工具任务新数据集作为支撑。

Conclusion: SLM有潜力充当每次工具调用的任务-工具相关性验证器，为智能体工具调用提供低延迟、可在本地运行的自动监督，作为传统授权机制的补充。

Abstract: Tool-equipped AI agents use tool calls to access data and act on external systems. Horizontal growth of agentic systems increases the number of these interactions, and further motivates the need for automated, per-call oversight that can operate at low latency and/or on-prem. Conventional authorization schemes can determine whether an agent is allowed to invoke a tool, but cannot assess the agent's underlying cognition, specifically, whether the tool selection represents a logical, relevant step toward satisfying the intent of the task or not. Consequently, an allowed call may still deviate from the task's intent: a rogue agent might deviate the calls or nudge other agents to make a combination of calls that would not align with the intent of the task. Therefore, every call needs to be verified. In this study we investigate the applicability of Small Language Models (SLMs) to this purpose: an SLM functions as a task-tool relevance classifier that evaluates every selected tool independently against the assigned task and returns a relevance signal for downstream enforcement. Equipped with a novel dataset with multi-tool tasks whose required tools span distinct Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, we used prompt-optimization, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning through GRPO to optimize and specialize SLMs.

</details>


### [291] [Learning a Fact Is Not Learning How to Retrieve It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03251)
*Chaemin Jang,Jihee Kim,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过两阶段训练分离"学习事实"与"检索事实"：第一阶段让模型以多种请求形式接触事实，第二阶段以陈述句学习新事实。结果显示，早期的请求形式经验与答案前的"上下文状态"共同决定后续检索表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型能在"The capital of X is"后正确输出"Y"，但在"The capital of X:"后失败。作者希望将"学习事实"与"检索事实"两个过程分离开来，探究请求形式经验如何影响后续对已学事实的检索。

Method: 采用两阶段训练：第一阶段（请求形式训练）中，一个模型以五种形式看到每个事实，另一个模型只以陈述句形式看到相同事实；第二阶段（目标事实训练）中，两个模型对新事实进行完全相同的陈述句训练。随后比较两者在新事实上的检索表现，并分析答案前的隐藏状态（上下文状态），通过改变该状态及在训练时干预该状态来检验其作用。

Result: 两模型从陈述句检索新事实的效果几乎相同，但在其他请求形式上差异显著。五形式训练的模型对同一事实的不同请求形式产生更相似的上下文状态。在检索时改变该状态可开启或阻断已学事实的检索，且该效应能跨事实及事实关系（如首都与货币）迁移。仅在目标事实训练期间改变上下文状态，也能改变之后（测试时不再干预）的检索表现。

Conclusion: 后续检索能力依赖早期的请求形式经验以及事实学习过程中的上下文状态，说明检索方式可以在事实被学习之前就先被习得，且上下文状态是连接学习与检索的关键机制。

Abstract: A model trained on "The capital of X is Y" may produce "Y" after "The capital of X is" but fail after "The capital of X:". We call these different ways of eliciting the same fact request forms. To separate learning a fact from retrieving it, we train two models in two stages. In the first stage (request-form training), one model sees each fact in five forms and the other sees the same facts only as statements. In the second stage (target-fact training), both receive identical training on new facts, all as statements. Both then retrieve the new facts almost equally well from statements, but differ sharply on other request forms. Thus, a model can learn how to retrieve through a request form before it learns the facts. To understand this difference, we examine the hidden state immediately before the answer, which we call the context state. When given two different request forms for the same fact, the model trained on five forms in stage one produces more similar context states than the model trained on statements alone in that stage. Changing this state at retrieval time can enable or prevent retrieval of an already learned fact, and the same effect transfers across facts and factual relations, such as capitals and currencies. To test its role during learning, we change the context state only during target-fact training. This intervention changes later retrieval without intervention at test time. Together, these results show that later retrieval depends on earlier request-form experience and the context state during fact learning.

</details>


### [292] [EVOL: Simulator-Guided Evolutionary Expert Synthesis for Deployment-Free Learning Path Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03273)
*Geonwoo Bang,Dongho Kim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVOL通过知识追踪模拟器生成演化专家演示，并训练免部署的盲规划策略，在学习路径推荐上超越8个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习路径推荐（LPR）的强化学习面临两个耦合障碍：一是策略需一次性提交L个概念的序列且无中间反馈，组合搜索空间随L超指数增长、仅在最终步给奖励（稀疏奖励）；二是专家学习路径本可缓解稀疏奖励问题，但教育数据中并不存在，因为学生日志记录的是学习者"做了什么"而非"应该做什么"。

Method: 从机器人基于模拟器的演示学习引入思路：用知识追踪模拟器既通过演化搜索合成每位学习者的专家演示，又训练一个免部署策略，将这些演示蒸馏为前馈学习器。框架EVOL用非对称actor-critic实现该流程，actor承诺符合部署实际的盲规划，critic在训练时利用特权模拟器状态。还比较了BC、AWR和DAPG三种模仿策略。

Result: 在三个数据集（ASSIST15、Junyi、EdNet；39-189个概念）以及路径长度L=5、10、20上，EVOL超越了涵盖启发式、序列、RL、图增强RL和LLM增强方法的8个基线。三种模仿策略对比显示，最终性能由演化专家质量决定，而非具体模仿目标。

Conclusion: 通过将知识追踪模拟器同时用于合成演化专家演示和训练免部署策略，EVOL有效解决了稀疏奖励与无专家路径的双重难题；演化专家质量是性能的关键决定因素，而非模仿目标的选择。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for learning path recommendation (LPR) faces two coupled obstacles. First, the policy must commit to a sequence of L concepts without intermediate feedback, producing a combinatorial search space that grows super-exponentially with L and provides reward only at the final step. Second, expert learning paths would be the natural cure for sparse-reward RL, but they do not exist in educational data, because student logs record what learners did, not what they should have done. We address both obstacles by importing a recipe from simulator-based demonstration learning in robotics: the knowledge tracing simulator is used both to synthesize per-learner expert demonstrations through evolutionary search and to train a deployment-free policy that distills these demonstrations into a feed-forward learner. Our framework, EVOL, instantiates this pipeline with an asymmetric actor-critic where the actor commits to deployment-realistic blind planning while the critic exploits the privileged simulator state during training. Across three datasets (ASSIST15, Junyi, and EdNet; 39-189 concepts) and path lengths L = 5, 10, and 20, EVOL surpasses 8 baselines spanning heuristic, sequential, RL, graph-enhanced RL, and LLM-enhanced methods. We further compare three imitation strategies (BC, AWR, and DAPG) and show that final performance is governed by the quality of evolutionary experts rather than by the particular imitation objective.

</details>


### [293] [JOVE: Joint Execution and Verification for Resource-Aware LLM Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03296)
*Haoran Zhang,Dongjun Kim,Seohyeon Cha,Kevin S Chan,Ananthram Swami,Gustavo De Veciana,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JOVE是一个在线框架，联合分配执行LLM并对中间输出进行付费验证，在长期预算和单查询延迟约束下平衡执行花费与学习，实现亚线性质量学习遗憾，并在四个推理基准上降低至少3.17倍的平均成本与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂推理查询可分解为有向无环任务图并分配到异构LLM上，通过并行降低延迟，让小模型也能解决复杂任务。但实际中LLM对某子任务的适配性先验未知，且仅靠执行无法判断输出正确性，因此需要研究如何在执行与验证之间做权衡。

Method: JOVE通过求解一系列逐查询混合整数线性规划来同时做执行与验证决策；在线学习基于验证反馈更新任务相关的LLM质量估计，并在分配决策中加入信息增益奖励项以体现学习价值。

Result: 在自然假设下证明了JOVE的亚线性质量学习遗憾；在四个推理基准上，JOVE达到与标准推理基线相当的准确率，同时平均成本与延迟至少降低3.17倍。

Conclusion: JOVE表明将执行分配与异步付费验证联合优化，可在预算与延迟约束下有效学习LLM服务质量并显著降低开销，为异构LLM协作推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Complex reasoning queries can be decomposed into directed acyclic task graphs and distributed across heterogeneous LLMs, reducing latency through parallelism and enabling smaller models to solve complex tasks. In practice, however, the suitability of an LLM for a given subtask may be a priori unknown, and execution alone does not reveal output correctness. We propose JOVE, an online framework that jointly assigns executor LLMs and selects intermediate outputs for paid verification. Verification runs asynchronously and is used to improve future allocations, so the system must balance spending on execution now against learning for later. We study how to optimize this trade-off under a long-term budget and a per-query latency constraint, with stochastic, initially unknown LLM service quality, invocation costs, and execution times. JOVE makes execution and verification decisions by solving a sequence of per-query mixed-integer linear programs. Online learning updates task-dependent estimates of LLM quality based on verification feedback, while an information-gain bonus incorporates the value of learning into allocation decisions. Under a natural set of assumptions, we establish sublinear quality-learning regret for JOVE. Across four reasoning benchmarks, JOVE achieves competitive accuracy against standard inference baselines while reducing average cost and latency by at least 3.17 times.

</details>


### [294] [Optimal Planning in a Dynamic World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03312)
*Devin Wild Thomas,Solomon Eyal Shimony,Wheeler Ruml,Erez Karpas,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Andrew Coles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"任意起始时间规划"问题，用复合到达时间函数（cATF）紧凑编码以起始时间为变量的最优计划，并给出基于启发式图搜索的通用算法，证明cATF规模至多随问题规模线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动障碍物路径规划（如SIPP）或"赶火车"等场景中，可行状态或动作随时间变化，最优计划及其时长依赖于执行起始时间。但实际中起始时间往往在规划完成或接到许可后才已知，而现有规划工作大多忽略动态性或假设起始时间已知，这虽简化了可行性判定，却不适用于部分应用。因此作者放松"已知起始时间"假设，定义任意起始时间规划问题。

Method: 作者提出名为复合到达时间函数（cATF）的数据结构，将最优计划紧凑地编码为起始时间的函数；并给出基于启发式图搜索的通用规划算法，通过沿边传播函数（而非标量代价）来组装cATF。

Result: 证明cATF的大小至多与问题规模成线性关系。针对SIPP问题的实现实验表明：在困难问题上，依赖重规划的智能体常失败，而使用cATF的任意起始时间算法可在执行起始时间确定后快速查得最优计划。

Conclusion: 通过支持时变计划的高效表示与推理，该工作为动态世界中的规划奠定了基础。

Abstract: Background: We address the problem of planning when the set of feasible states or actions changes over time. For example, in the problem of path planning among moving obstacles (sometimes known as SIPP), the feasibility of being at a particular location can change as the obstacles move. Or, the action of boarding a particular train is feasible only while it is stopped at the station. This dynamism means that the optimal plan and its duration can change depending on when execution begins. In practice, execution start time is often unknown until planning has completed or another agent gives the go-ahead. However, most prior planning work either ignores dynamism or assumes a known start time. This makes it straightforward to assess state and action feasibility but is impractical for some applications. Objectives: In this paper, we relax the assumption of a known start time. We define the setting of {\em any-start-time planning} and provide algorithms for it. Methods: We present a data structure called a compound arrival time function (cATF) that compactly encodes the optimal plan as a function of start time. We provide general-purpose planning algorithms, based on heuristic graph search, that assemble cATFs by propagating functions along edges instead of scalar costs. Results: We prove that the size of a cATF is at most linear in the problem size. An experimental evaluation of an implementation for the specific problem of SIPP shows that, on difficult problems, agents that rely on replanning often fail, while any-start-time algorithms using cATFs can quickly look up the optimal plan once the execution start time is known. Conclusions: By enabling efficient representations and reasoning for time-dependent plans, this work provides a foundation for planning in dynamic worlds.

</details>


### [295] [Lightweight, Rubric-Guided Trajectory Evaluation for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03315)
*Linh-An Phan,MingXue Wang,Guangyu Wu,Feng Pan,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LiteTrajEval，一种面向预算受限场景的轻量级 LLM 智能体轨迹评估架构，通过离线规则画像、在线预处理与单次 LLM 评判，在降低成本与时间的同时显著提升故障定位对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹评估对提升 LLM 智能体可靠性至关重要，但生产环境中反复运行成本高昂；且现代智能体轨迹冗长，包含工具调用、观察、重试等，原始 token 并非都同等有用，因此需要一种预算受限的轻量评估方法。

Method: LiteTrajEval 离线推导紧凑的领域专用规则画像；在线阶段对每条轨迹进行预处理，标记启发式失败信号，并在固定全局预算下序列化，最后调用单个由评分标准引导的 LLM 评判器生成结构化诊断报告。

Result: 在公开的 Magentic-One 风格与 τ-bench 风格轨迹数据集上，相比 AgentRx，LiteTrajEval 在 Magentic-One 上故障定位与人工标注的对齐度提升约 20--35 个百分点，在 τ-retail 上提升最高达 23 个百分点；成本降低约 6 倍，评估时间缩短 8 倍以上。

Conclusion: LiteTrajEval 在显著降低评估成本与时间的同时提升了轨迹故障定位质量，并已部署于企业级智能体平台中。

Abstract: Trajectory evaluation is essential for improving the reliability of LLM-based agents, but production use makes it expensive to run repeatedly. Modern agents generate long traces containing tool calls, observations, retries, and external outputs, while not all raw tokens are equally useful for diagnosis. We present \textit{LiteTrajEval}, a lightweight architecture for budget-bounded trajectory evaluation. LiteTrajEval derives compact domain-specific rule profiles offline, then preprocesses each trajectory online, marks heuristic failure signals, serializes it under a fixed global budget, and invokes a single rubric-guided LLM judge to produce structured diagnostic reports. Evaluated on public Magentic-One-style and $τ$-bench-style trajectory datasets, LiteTrajEval improves failure-localization alignment with human annotations by roughly 20--35 percentage points on Magentic-One and up to 23 percentage points on $τ$-retail compared with AgentRx, while reducing cost by about 6$\times$ and evaluation time by more than 8$\times$. This solution has also been deployed in our enterprise agentic platform.

</details>


### [296] [Multi-Task Evolution for Zero-Shot Cross-Problem Generalization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03316)
*Zhouliang Xie,Changliang Zhou,Genghui Li,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MECo——一种LLM驱动的多任务进化框架，实现零样本跨问题启发式泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 启发式设计需大量专家经验与反复搜索；LLM虽可自动化启发式生成与改进，但通常依赖目标问题的评估反馈，仅凭源任务反馈泛化到新问题定义仍是核心难题。

Method: MECo维护按任务条件的启发式种群，利用跨任务种群性能构建"迁移差距"来引导种群交互，实现启发式的迁移与重组；再用互补选择准则，通过奖励各成员对源组合的额外覆盖，构建紧凑启发式集合，直接应用于目标任务而无需额外搜索或适应。

Result: 在车辆路径（VRP）与柔性作业车间调度（FJSP）的32个问题变体上，相同预算下MECo平均成本最低，优于8个自动启发式设计（AHD）基线；在域外问题上，其表现超过各问题族中最强基线；将MECo框架与不同AHD方法结合，也能同时提升其域内与域外性能。

Conclusion: MECo通过多任务进化与迁移引导，实现了有效的零样本跨问题启发式泛化，且可作为通用框架增强多种AHD方法。

Abstract: Designing effective heuristics for diverse combinatorial optimization problems requires substantial expertise and repeated search. Large language models (LLMs) automate heuristic generation and refinement, but heuristic search typically depends on evaluation feedback from the problem being optimized. Generalizing to new problem definitions using only source-task feedback therefore remains a central challenge. We introduce MECo, an LLM-driven multi-task evolutionary framework for zero-shot cross-problem generalization. MECo maintains task-conditioned heuristic populations and uses a transfer gap based on cross-task population performance to guide their interactions. These interactions enable the transfer and recombination of heuristics. A complementary selection criterion then constructs a compact heuristic set by rewarding each member's additional coverage of source combinations. The selected set is applied to target problems without further search or adaptation. Experiments on 32 problem variants across vehicle routing (VRP) and flexible job-shop scheduling (FJSP) show that MECo achieves the lowest mean costs compared with eight automated heuristic design (AHD) baselines under the same budgets. On out-of-domain problems, it outperforms the strongest baseline in each family. Moreover, integrating the framework of MECo with different AHD methods improves their ID and OOD performance in both families, supporting its effectiveness across different methods.

</details>


### [297] [Refinement Buys Intelligibility, Search Buys Identity: What Test-Time Compute Buys in Masked-Diffusion TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03320)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Ayush Pratap Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在扩散式TTS中，模型深度与推理精炼步数并不等价：精炼能弥补86.2%的可懂度差距，但仅弥补46.4%的说话人身份差距（1.86倍不对称），且加深/延长训练无法逆转该差距；二者应对不同瓶颈，需分别优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型TTS包含两种计算资源：模型深度（参数量）与精炼步数（推理预算）。作者希望探究二者是否对各种能力具有同等的扩展效果，即深度与步数是否可互换。

Method: 训练15个掩码扩散编解码器TTS模型，深度19–133M参数、3个随机种子，使用2000小时语音数据；推理时扫描精炼步数T∈[1,16]；用ASR词错误率衡量可懂度、说话人验证衡量身份，在174个留出说话人上做零样本评测。另做3倍/6倍训练计划重训、Best-of-K搜索，并用B(d)B(T)可分离拟合与替换模型对比、Delta AICc检验。

Result: 精炼弥补86.2%可懂度差距，但仅46.4%身份差距，呈1.86倍不对称且对多种误差度量稳健。延长训练计划使差距从1.86倍降至1.84、1.36、1.23倍，但未逆转，因可懂度随步数饱和而身份仍持续提升。Best-of-K搜索在身份上弥补精炼不足，四个独立编码器上胜率64.6–79.0%。可分离B(d)B(T)拟合显著优于替换模型（Delta AICc=+69.3）。分析显示剩余身份缺口中62%来自编解码器而非生成器。

Conclusion: 深度与精炼步数针对不同瓶颈，不可互换，应分别优化；身份质量的主要瓶颈在于编解码器而非生成器。

Abstract: Diffusion language models for text-to-speech combine two forms of computation: model depth (parameters) and refinement steps (inference budget). We ask whether they scale equally across capabilities. We train 15 masked-diffusion codec TTS models varying depth (19-133M parameters, 3 seeds) on 2,000 hours of speech and sweep refinement steps T in [1,16] at inference, measuring zero-shot synthesis via ASR word error rate (intelligibility) and speaker verification (identity) on 174 held-out speakers. Against measured floors, refinement closes 86.2% of the intelligibility range but only 46.4% of the identity range - a 1.86x asymmetry robust across multiple error metrics. Retraining at 3x and 6x schedule attenuates but does not reverse this gap (1.84 to 1.36 to 1.23x), because intelligibility saturates with steps while identity continues improving. Best-of-K search recovers speaker identity where refinement fails, with 64.6-79.0% win rates across four independent encoders. Depth and steps are not interchangeable: separable B(d)B(T) fits significantly better (Delta AICc=+69.3) than substitution models. Analysis shows 62% of remaining identity deficit lies in the codec, not the generator. We conclude that refinement and depth target different bottlenecks and should be optimized separately.

</details>


### [298] [Preserving Mathematical Reasoning in Compressed Diffusion Language Models via Trajectory-Aware Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03326)
*Tian Liang,Zishan Shao,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散语言模型(dLLM)压缩中校准与推理状态不一致的问题，提出轨迹感知的低秩目标与Traj-MC估计方法，在同等压缩预算下更好保持数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM压缩存在已知挑战：校准通常在干净、完全可见的激活上进行，而推理过程会经历部分掩码的中间状态。对低秩压缩而言，这引出两个问题：当近似质量在轨迹分布的多个状态上衡量时，低秩最优性是否仍可刻画；以及校准状态的选择是否影响压缩后数学推理能力的保持。

Method: 作者在腐蚀程度(corruption levels)与掩码实现(masking realizations)上构建轨迹感知的低秩目标，并为此目标的高效估计提出Traj-MC，通过蒙特卡洛采样估计轨迹二阶矩，具备精确的采样状态最优性和总体一致性。

Result: 在匹配的压缩预算下，轨迹感知校准相比干净校准能改善生成轨迹上的重构质量，并在数学推理基准上显著保留更多数学推理能力。

Conclusion: 研究将轨迹感知的低秩最优性与dLLM压缩后保留的推理能力联系起来。

Abstract: Diffusion language model (dLLM) compression faces a known challenge because calibration is typically performed on clean, fully visible activations, whereas inference traverses partially masked intermediate states. For low-rank compression, this raises two questions. First, can low-rank optimality still be characterized when approximation quality is measured over trajectory-distributed states, and second, does the choice of calibration states affect mathematical reasoning preservation under compression? We address these questions by formulating a trajectory-aware low-rank objective over corruption levels and masking realizations. To estimate this objective efficiently, we propose Traj-MC, which estimates the trajectory second moment through Monte Carlo sampling and yields exact sampled-state optimality and population consistency. Under matched compression budgets, trajectory-aware calibration improves reconstruction over the generation trajectory and preserves substantially more mathematical reasoning than clean calibration on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our results connect trajectory-aware low-rank optimality to the reasoning capability retained after dLLM compression. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/traj-mc.git.

</details>


### [299] [ReFract: Benchmarking Perspective Awareness in Language Model Agents with Text World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03356)
*Hainiu Xu,Vítor N. Lourenço,Mohnish Dubey,Yunfei Bai,Yulan He,Caroline Catmur,Aline Paes,Marco Caserta,Akash Chandrayan,Luca D'Angelo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ReFract基准，用于评估LLM智能体在工业维护等高风险场景中的"视角意识"——即根据用户角色调整行为的能​​力。基准包含150条专家验证条目，最好的模型仅能解决69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地被部署在工业维护、设备故障排查等高风险场景中，用户角色多样。智能体必须根据用户角色调整自身行为，尊重该角色的知识能力边界，因为其操作会作用于物理设备，可能造成不可逆的设备损坏、生产损失或人员伤害。然而现有基准大多忽视了智能体推断角色意图、仅通过该角色合法使用的工具行事的能力，作者将其称为"视角意识"。

Method: 作者引入ReFract基准，包含150条经专家验证的条目，智能体须根据用户角色对同一查询作出不同响应。这些条目基于领域支持对话中匿名化的查询，据此构建文本世界模型（Text World Models）来模拟智能体的操作环境，并组装具备视角意识的动作轨迹。

Result: 最先进的LLM最多只能解决69%的任务，且超过50%的轨迹包含尝试执行违反视角的动作。ReFract揭示了视角意识是智能体评估中一个独立的、很大程度上尚未解决的维度。

Conclusion: ReFract凸显了视角意识作为一个独立且基本未解决的评估轴，推动智能体不仅要校准"如何行动"，还要校准"为谁行动"。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as industrial maintenance and equipment fault troubleshooting, where workers occupy a variety of roles. A capable agent must therefore act in a way that is calibrated to user's role: taking actions and providing information that respect the role's knowledge and capability boundaries. Unlike coding, where mistakes are usually recoverable, agent responses in these settings are enacted on physical equipment, and can therefore cause irreversible equipment damage, production loss, or personnel harm. Existing benchmarks, however, largely overlook the need for agents to infer what a role intends and acting only through tools that role may legitimately use, a capability which we term Perspective Awareness. To this end, we introduce ReFract, a benchmark of 150 expert-validated entries in which an agent must act differently in response to the same query depending on user's role. Entries of ReFract are grounded in anonymized queries from domain support conversations, against which we construct Text World Models that simulate the agent's operating environments and assemble perspective-aware action trajectories. State-of-the-art LLMs solve at most 69% of the tasks with more than 50% of their trajectories contain attempts of taking perspective-violating actions. ReFract exposes perspective awareness as a distinct, largely unsolved axis of agent evaluation and motivates agents that calibrate not just how to act, but for whom.

</details>


### [300] [Geometry Meets Physics: Data-Efficient Pre-Training for Unstructured Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03363)
*Luis Medrano-Navarro,Giacomo Baldan,Qiang Liu,Benjamin Holzschuh,Jan Hagnberger,Mathias Niepert,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需预生成数据集的预训练框架，针对稳态与瞬态PDE问题分别采用几何驱动和物理驱动策略，实现更快的收敛、更高的数据效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构3D几何上的PDE神经代理模型泛化能力差，且生成大规模训练数据成本高昂；依赖海量预计算数据的预训练策略在算力和数据上都不经济。

Method: 提出无磁盘数据（disk-data-free）预训练框架：稳态问题采用几何驱动策略，利用内在形状描述子学习复杂3D域的表征；瞬态问题采用物理驱动方法，基于在线生成的合成PDE数据实现可扩展预训练。

Result: 在多项实验中，该方法实现了更快的收敛、更高的数据效率，并在微调阶段（尤其在实际低数据情形下）取得更高精度。

Conclusion: 该方法为大规模仿真提供了通往数据高效神经模拟器的实用路径。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models for Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) on unstructured 3D geometries are often limited by poor generalization and the high cost of generating large-scale training datasets. Consequently, pre-training on massive datasets of related PDE dynamics has emerged as a critical alternative to enhance the robustness and scalability of these models. However, this strategy is neither compute- nor data-efficient, as it relies on massive pre-computed data that is very costly to generate. In this work, we introduce a disk-data-free pre-training framework tailored to both steady-state and transient regimes. For steady-state problems, we propose a geometry-driven strategy that leverages intrinsic shape descriptors to learn representations of complex 3D domains. For transient problems, we introduce a physics-driven approach based on online generation of synthetic PDE data, enabling scalable pre-training without reliance on expensive datasets. Across multiple experiments, our approach achieves faster convergence, greater data efficiency, and higher accuracy during fine-tuning, particularly under realistic low-data regimes. This methodology provides a practical pathway toward data-efficient neural emulators for large-scale simulations.

</details>


### [301] [Multilingual GSM-Symbolic: What determines capability transfer across languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03367)
*Kenneth Enevoldsen,Riley Herchert,Sofie Mosegaard,Dan Saattrup Smart,Simon Enni,Isaac Chung,Sofie Bruun,Ayush Sunil Munot,Max Müller-Eberstein,Adnan El-Assadi,Elisa Bassignana,Gianluca Barmina,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Iben Nyholm Debess,Linda Freienthal,Lukas Galke Poech,Mike Zhang,Nicolas Legrand,Vladimir Salnikov,Yevhen Kostiuk,Zafar Hussain,Sagandeep Kaur,Agnes Toftgård,Marie Mattson,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可扩展的多语言数学数据集 Multilingual GSM-Symbolic（30,000 组题目匹配问答对、覆盖 15 种语言），用以量化跨语言能力迁移的四大决定因素：模型规模、语言资源水平、推理能力与类型学距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界对能力如何从一种语言迁移到另一种语言、以及何种因素主导这种迁移了解甚少；现有评估依赖不可比较且易饱和的数据集，且很少联合考察迁移的决定因素。识别迁移的预测因子，可避免对所有语言对进行穷尽式评估，并帮助开发者聚焦限制低资源语言表现的关键因素。

Method: 构建 Multilingual GSM-Symbolic：一个可扩展的多语言数学数据集，包含 30,000 组题目匹配的问答对，覆盖 15 种语言；采用符号化模板，防止过拟合、保障泛化，可由单一样本生成数百万个高质量变体。基于该数据集对能力决定因素进行联合估计与回归分析。

Result: 量化出能力的主要决定因素：模型规模（β=1.77）、语言资源水平（β=0.77）、推理（β=0.67）与类型学距离（β=-0.25）。联合估计使这些因素可相互换算：32B 模型在马拉地语上的表现相当于 10B 模型在英语上的表现。模型规模与推理能缩小低资源与高资源语言间的差距（β=-0.27 与 β=-0.20），但对类型学上相距较远的语言几乎无效。分析框架解释了 92% 的语言间差异，但仅解释 23% 的"模型×语言"交互差异；对未见语言的性能预测误差在 6.0pp 以内（r=.96），加入目标语言仅 10 个模板的测量后可降至 4.19pp。

Conclusion: 该框架能高精度预测模型在未见语言上的表现，并可用极少的目标语言数据进一步降低误差，从而在缺乏下游数据集的情况下给出合理估计。研究结果对模型开发者具有重要启示：模型规模与推理是缩小资源差距的有效杠杆，但对类型学距离较远的语言作用有限。

Abstract: We understand little about how capabilities acquired in one language carry over to another, or what governs this transfer: evaluations rely on incomparable, saturation-prone datasets and rarely examine its determinants jointly. Identifying what predicts transfer would let us avoid exhaustive evaluation across all language pairs and let developers target the factors that limit performance in low-resource languages. To evaluate cross-lingual capability transfer, we introduce Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, an extensible multilingual mathematical dataset covering 30,000 item-matched question-answer pairs and spanning 15 languages. It utilises symbolic templates to prevent overfitting and ensure generalisation by allowing generation of millions of high-quality variations from a single sample. Using Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, we quantify the largest determinants of capability as model size ($β= 1.77$), language resource level ($β= 0.77$), reasoning ($β= 0.67$) and typological distance ($β= -0.25$). This joint estimation allows these determinants to be expressed in terms of one another: a 32B model evaluated in Marathi performs like a 10B model in English. Our findings have important implications for model developers, showing that model size and reasoning narrow the performance gap between low- and high-resource languages ($β= -0.27$ and $β= -0.20$, respectively), while similar levers have little or no effect on typologically distant languages.
  Overall, our analysis framework explains 92% of between-language variation, but only 23% of the model-by-language variation, and predicts a model's performance on an unseen language within 6.0pp (r=.96). Incorporating measurements from just 10 templates in the target language reduces this to 4.19pp, enabling reasonable estimates of performance with little or no downstream dataset.

</details>


### [302] [CVE2AP: Automated Generation of PDDL-Encoded Attack Paths via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03383)
*Lin Cui,Vincenzo Scotti,Raffaela Mirandola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CVE2AP，利用LLM从自然语言CVE描述自动生成PDDL编码的攻击路径，并通过错误反馈机制迭代优化，实现高质量自动化AP建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击路径（AP）建模是网络安全分析的基础，PDDL被广泛用于将AP编码为形式化、机器可验证的表示以支持自动化推理。但现有AP建模方法主要依赖专家手工构建，难以扩展，也无法跟上快速演变的网络威胁。LLM凭借预训练知识与推理能力，有望将威胁情报自动转化为形式化表示。

Method: CVE2AP采用结构化提示（structured prompting），并引入错误反馈机制，利用规划器报告的语法错误和可解性错误迭代地精炼生成的攻击路径。在多个LLM和生成配置下进行系统实证评估，从语法、可解性和语义三个维度衡量生成质量，同时考察token消耗与生成时间。

Result: CVE2AP能有效生成高质量PDDL编码攻击路径，最高达86.9%语法正确率、78.6%可解性和93.1%语义正确率（在LLM作为专家评估下）；GPT-5.5提供最佳质量-成本权衡，错误反馈带来最一致的质量提升。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的方法（尤其结合错误反馈机制）可有效实现攻击路径的自动化PDDL编码，为可扩展、紧跟威胁演变的网络安全分析提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Attack Path (AP) modeling is fundamental to cybersecurity analysis, where the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) has been widely adopted to encode APs into formal and machine-verifiable representations for automated reasoning about vulnerability exploitation, attack progression, and their potential impacts. However, existing AP modeling approaches largely rely on expert-driven manual construction, limiting their scalability and ability to keep pace with rapidly evolving cyber threats. Large language models (LLMs) are promising candidates, as their extensive pre-trained knowledge and reasoning capabilities enable them to interpret and transform threat intelligence into formal representations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CVE2AP}, an LLM-based approach for automatically generating PDDL-encoded attack paths from natural language CVE (Common Vulnerability Exposure) descriptions. CVE2AP leverages structured prompting and incorporates an error-feedback mechanism that iteratively refines the generated paths using planner-reported syntactic and solvability errors. We conduct a systematic empirical evaluation across multiple LLMs and generation configurations, assessing generation quality across syntactic, solvability and semantic dimensions, together with token consumption and generation time. The results demonstrate that CVE2AP effectively generates high-quality PDDL-encoded attack paths, achieving up to 86.9\% syntax correctness, 78.6\% solvability, and 93.1\% semantic correctness under LLM-as-expert evaluation, while \texttt{GPT-5.5} offers the best quality-cost trade-off and error feedback yields the most consistent quality improvement.

</details>


### [303] [Benchmarking Candidate Coverage in Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03387)
*Jiawen Lu,Tongtong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"配对候选覆盖"基准协议，在AG News、DBpedia、Emotion、TREC四个数据集上评估Laya与Jev两个模型对"缺失参考答案"的拒答行为，发现模型的拒答能力与完整选项下的准确率需要分开测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型在请求时返回选项中的选择或分布，但在完整选项下取得的高准确率并不能证明模型能识别参考答案缺失、也不能证明其不会误拒有效候选，因此需要一个专门的评测协议来区分分类能力、评分排序能力与拒答策略。

Method: 设计配对候选覆盖基准协议：模型接收完全相同的冻结文本与请求（300条校准文本、589条测试文本，每模型23,932条预测）。构建"存在/缺失"配对，二者普通候选数量相匹配；同时构造名称变体，保持描述、成员与顺序不变。据此测量原生拒答行为，并使用仅基于校准集的none分数阈值进行调整，辅以能力条件分析、概率-精度敏感性与接口审查。

Result: 原生拒答行为差异显著：在TREC的5个自然名称候选项下，Laya检出97.2%的缺失答案案例，但误拒69.7%的存在对照；Jev分别为24.8%与0.0%。仅用校准的none分数阈值后，两者变为33.9%/3.7%与45.0%/1.8%。在DBpedia上，Jev较高的覆盖分数AUROC支持更强的操作点；而在Emotion上两模型的完整集准确率均较弱。

Conclusion: 该基准表明分类、分数排序与拒答策略需要分别测量，模型在完整选项下的准确率不能替代拒答能力的评估。本工作为描述性初步基准，仅限参考答案缺失场景，不构成自然域外泛化、因果机制或新拒答方法的结论。

Abstract: Typed decision models return choices or distributions over answer options supplied at request time. Accuracy with complete options does not establish whether a model recognizes that a reference answer is missing or avoids rejecting valid candidates. We present a paired candidate-coverage benchmark protocol and an initial evaluation of Laya and Jev across AG News, DBpedia, Emotion, and TREC. The models receive identical frozen texts and requests: 300 calibration and 589 test texts yield 23,932 predictions per model. Present/absent pairs match ordinary candidate count, and name variants preserve descriptions, members, and order. Native rejection behavior differs sharply: at five TREC candidates with natural names, Laya detects 97.2% of missing-answer cases but falsely rejects 69.7% of present controls; Jev's rates are 24.8% and 0.0%. Calibration-only none-score thresholds change these rates to 33.9%/3.7% and 45.0%/1.8%, respectively. On DBpedia, Jev's high coverage-score AUROC supports a stronger operating point, whereas both models have weak complete-set accuracy on Emotion. Competence-conditioned analysis, probability-precision sensitivity, and interface audits show why classification, score ranking, and rejection policies need separate measurement. This initial benchmark is descriptive and limited to reference-label omission; it does not establish natural out-of-scope generalization, causal mechanisms, or a new rejection method.

</details>


### [304] [Becoming Suspicious Across Borders: Algorithmic Extraterritoriality and AI-Driven Financial Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03425)
*Georgios Pavlidis,Savvas Chatzichristofis,Eleni Gavriil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法域外性"概念，指出AI驱动的金融监控使可疑性从人类在特定法域内作出的情境化法律判断，转变为由跨国数据基础设施中介的数据驱动过程，从而导致可疑性更难定位、解释与抗辩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反洗钱与反恐融资中的"可疑性"概念重要却难以把握，它允许在未达证明标准时即行干预。传统理解将可疑性视为人类行为者在可识别法域内作出的情境化法律判断。作者认为，随着人工智能成为金融监控的组成部分，这一理解已不再充分，需要重新概念化可疑性的产生方式及其空间与认识论意涵。

Method: 理论/概念分析。文章从认识论与空间两个维度考察可疑性的转变：一方面追溯可疑性如何由数据驱动过程生产，另一方面分析AI金融监控如何依托跨国数据基础设施运作，并在此基础上提出"算法域外性"概念，将其界定为一种由数据基础设施中介、而非通过正式管辖权主张来行使的规制权力。

Result: 可疑性 increasingly 通过数据驱动的过程被生产，这一转变既是认识论的，也是空间的；由于AI金融监控依托跨国数据基础设施运行，监管触达不再取决于行为发生地，而取决于该行为能否在数据系统中变得可见；个体日益被构成为"数据化的可疑主体"，通过分散且不透明的评估过程而被治理。

Conclusion: 可疑性的数据化与算法域外性对问责与可争议性构成挑战，因为可疑性变得更难以定位、解释或抗辩。文章呼吁重新思考AI时代金融监控中规制权力的运作方式及相应的问责机制。

Abstract: Suspicion is an important, yet elusive concept in anti-money laundering and counter-terrorist financing (AML/CFT), which allows for intervention below the threshold of proof. In its traditional form, suspicion can be understood as a situated legal judgement by human actors within identifiable jurisdictions. It is argued that this understanding is no longer adequate. As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes an integral part of financial surveillance, suspicion is increasingly produced through data-driven processes. This transformation is epistemic, but also spatial. Since AI-driven financial surveillance operates through transnational data infrastructures, regulatory reach is less a matter of where conduct occurs than a question of whether such conduct becomes visible within data systems. This article develops the concept of algorithmic extraterritoriality, understood as a form of regulatory power mediated by data infrastructures rather than formal assertions of jurisdiction. Moreover, since individuals are increasingly constituted as datafied subjects of suspicion, they are rendered governable through dispersed and opaque processes of evaluation. This constitutes a challenge for accountability and contestability because suspicion becomes more difficult to locate, explain or contest.

</details>


### [305] [Jumping the Line: Exploiting Length Predictions in LLM Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03430)
*Yuyang Dai,Rana Shahout,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JIL，一种针对基于预测的LLM调度器的攻击方法，通过优化对抗后缀使输出长度探针低估请求长度，从而获得更高调度优先级并缩短完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型服务中，高效的请求调度对降低完成时间日益重要。基于长度的策略（如最短作业优先）偏好较短请求，但输出长度在生成前未知，因此实际调度器依赖预测长度。这为操纵调度信号创造了潜在的攻击面，而此前缺乏相关研究。

Method: 以TRAIL为案例，JIL优化一个对抗后缀，使轻量级输出长度探针低估请求长度。在两个数据集、四种LLM及不同请求画像和部署配置下进行评估，考察预测长度变化、端到端完成时间、响应效用以及调度器侧防御（如将长度预测分组为粗粒度区间）。

Result: JIL将预测输出长度最多降低83.4%，对抗请求在端到端服务实验中平均完成速度快至1.53倍。预测长度的降幅远大于实际输出长度的变化，揭示调度器估计与请求真实规模之间的不匹配。响应效用因模型和任务而异，体现调度优势与响应质量之间的权衡。将预测分组为粗粒度区间可削弱JIL的调度优势并缓解对良性请求的延迟。

Conclusion: JIL揭示了基于预测的LLM调度器存在可被利用的安全漏洞，即预测长度与实际长度解耦可被对抗性利用。粗粒度分组的调度器侧防御能有效缓解该攻击，为构建更鲁棒的调度机制提供方向。

Abstract: Efficient request scheduling is increasingly important for reducing completion time in large language model (LLM) serving. Size-based policies such as Shortest Job First prioritize shorter requests, but output lengths are unknown before generation, so practical schedulers rely on predicted lengths. We introduce JIL, an attack on prediction-based LLM schedulers that manipulates the scheduling signal to obtain higher priority and reduce completion time. Using TRAIL as a case study, JIL optimizes an adversarial suffix that causes a lightweight output-length probe to underestimate a request's length. We evaluate JIL on two datasets and four LLMs across varied request profiles and deployment configurations. JIL reduces predicted output lengths by up to 83.4 percent, and adversarial requests complete up to 1.53 times faster on average in end-to-end serving experiments. The reduction in predicted length is substantially larger than the change in actual output length, revealing a mismatch between the scheduler's estimate and the request's realized size. Response utility varies across models and tasks, exposing a trade-off between scheduling advantage and response quality. We also evaluate scheduler-side defenses and find that grouping length predictions into coarse intervals reduces JIL's scheduling advantage and mitigates delays to benign requests.

</details>


### [306] [A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03458)
*Zhe Zhou,Tianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 低监控读数无法证明行为控制：通过前缀训练实验证明，同一零中位数读数下行为可在"混合"与"近乎纯奖励黑客"之间由随机种子决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练中带可验证奖励会诱发奖励黑客，因此人们尝试把监控器嵌入训练目标（而非仅用于离线审计）。但本文要质疑：监控读数值低，是否真的等价于行为被控制住了？作者希望揭示"离线判别通过 + 训练中读数低"这一证据链的不足。

Method: 在代码生成环境中（主要漏洞在推理轨迹起点即可用），用三类通过同一离线门槛的监控器训练策略：域内激活探针，以及两种以"策略多早锁定自己最终答案"为条件的惩罚。比较不同监控族的读数与其对应行为，并用文本层面分析识别前缀失效模式。

Result: 探针分数从首个记录训练步即处于数值下限；所有前缀训练run在终点处训练分数中位数均为零。但同一固定配置、同一零中位数下，仅因随机种子不同，行为即从低黑客占比的混合态跨越到近乎纯奖励黑客。探针run全部落入黑客态，其地板级读数源于"验证探针的位置"与"训练中读取位置"的不匹配。文本分析发现：通用规划与填充外壳把漏洞利用推迟到截断点之后，却未从最终输出中消除。

Conclusion: 离线判别与监控对齐的低读数不足以证明行为控制；低测量承诺度既可能对应低黑客占比，也可能对应漏洞利用的延迟承诺。需要引入带外（out-of-band）的行为检查。本文刻画的是终点读数，而非其演化过程。

Abstract: Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.

</details>


### [307] [Efficient Reasoning Training Does Not Always Harm CoT Faithfulness and Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03509)
*Samuel Lewis-Lim,Xingwei Tan,Mario Sanger,Zhixue Zhao,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察了三种施加长度压力的效率方法（固定生成预算、逐样本长度目标、组相对长度奖励）对思维链（CoT）忠实性与可监控性的影响，发现二者受影响的模式不同：忠实性在多数设置下下降，主要因模型一致性降低；而可监控性更为稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT 推理虽能让人审视大语言模型的决策过程，但推理成本随 token 增加而上升，因此需要训练模型用更少 token 解题。然而人们担心这种训练会让模型跳过关键推理步骤，导致 CoT 不再忠实反映其真实决策。由于不同效率方法对 CoT 施加长度压力的方式各异，且不同任务忠实解释所需 token 量不同，这一现象是否及何时发生尚不明确。

Method: 微调多种模型，采用三种以不同方式施加长度压力的方法：固定生成预算、逐样本长度目标、组相对长度奖励。随后评估高效推理对 CoT 忠实性（CoT 在相关输入上反映模型决策的程度）与可监控性（CoT 是否揭示输入干预改变输出）的影响。

Result: 不同方法对忠实性和可监控性的影响不同。忠实性在多数设置下下降，主要原因是训练后的模型一致性降低；可监控性则更为稳健，即便 CoT 大幅缩短，模型仍会说明输入对其答案的影响。

Conclusion: 以效率为导向的长度压力训练会削弱 CoT 忠实性，但对可监控性影响较小。理解这两种属性的不同动态，有助于在设计高效推理方法时权衡推理成本与模型透明度。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows humans to inspect how large language models reach their answers, and oversee model behaviour. This reasoning comes at an increased inference cost, motivating efficient methods that train models to solve tasks using fewer tokens. However, a common concern is that such training may cause models to skip important reasoning steps, so the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the model's decision. It is unclear whether or when this occurs in practice, since different efficiency methods apply length pressure to models' CoT in distinct ways, and faithfully explaining a model's decision takes more tokens on some tasks than others. To understand these dynamics, we fine-tune a variety of models with three methods that apply length pressure differently, namely a fixed generation budget, a per-example length target, and a group-relative length reward. We evaluate how efficient reasoning affects CoT faithfulness (i.e., how well the CoT reflects model decisions on related inputs) and monitorability (i.e., whether the CoT reveals when input interventions alter the output). We find that it affects faithfulness and monitorability differently. Faithfulness falls in most settings, primarily because the trained models are less consistent. Monitorability is more robust, as models keep acknowledging the influence on their answer even when the CoT is much shorter.

</details>


### [308] [Reasoning Models Are Accurate but Unsound on Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03519)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CERTID形式化识别流水线，用ID算法认证因果效应的可识别性，并对公式进行等价性验证，评估推理模型在因果识别任务上的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在判断因果效应可否从观测数据中恢复时会有两类失败：拒绝可识别的查询，或对不可识别查询给出错误公式。后者更严重，因为没有任何观测数据能验证其声称的公式。以往评测缺乏可证明不可识别的查询，且字符串匹配无法接受等价形式的正确公式。

Method: 构建CERTID流水线：使用完备且可靠的因果识别算法ID认证效应在给定图和查询下是否可识别；用干预分布精确已知的结构因果模型验证返回公式；并发展理论结果缓解结构泄漏、修复不可识别查询、建立评分保证。在1200个认证实例（4至50个顶点）上评估Gemini Flash、Gemini Pro和GPT5.5。

Result: 准确率是不健全性的糟糕代理指标：在相同实例上，不同模型对不可识别查询的虚假声称率相差十七倍。模型在最强模型训练快照之后生成的图上，判断可识别性的准确率达97-100%。

Conclusion: CERTID为因果识别能力评测提供了可认证、可验证、等价形式兼容的评测框架，揭示准确率掩盖了模型健全性的巨大差异。实例、认证流程、验证器及逐实例记录已公开。

Abstract: A reasoning model asked whether a causal effect is recoverable from observational data can fail in two ways: it refuses an identifiable query or answers a nonidentifiable one. The latter is more consequential, as no observational data can validate the claimed formula. Measuring this failure requires queries that are provably non-identifiable, which prior evaluations lack, and grading that accepts correct formulas in any equivalent form, which string matching cannot provide. We build CERTID, a formal identification pipeline that addresses both limitations. CERTID uses the sound and complete causal identification algorithm ID to certify whether an effect is identifiable from a given graph and query, and verifies returned formulas against structural causal models whose interventional distributions are known exactly. CERTID further develops theoretical results to mitigate structural leakage, repair non-identifiable queries, and establish grading guarantees. We evaluate three frontier reasoning models (Gemini Flash, Gemini Pro, and GPT5.5) on 1,200 certified instances spanning 4 to 50 vertices. Accuracy proves a poor proxy for soundness: on identical instances, the false-claim rate on non-identifiable queries varies by seventeen-fold across models. We also find that models decide identifiability with 97-100% accuracy on graphs generated after the strongest model's training snapshot. Instances, the certification procedure, the verifier, and per-instance records are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/certid-D718.

</details>


### [309] [From Benchmarks to Production: A Text-to-SQL System for Complex Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03524)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Bruno Gomes Coelho,Guillaume Michel,Sophia Zhi,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Kristen Howell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLINT是一个面向生产金融数据库的领域专用Text-to-SQL系统，通过查找代理、模板检索和模式链接三个组件，解决了因模式中概念以不透明整数键存储而导致的性能瓶颈问题，并在359个问题的生产数据集上超越多个SOTA基线，已部署上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用Text-to-SQL系统在Spider、BIRD等学术基准上表现优异，但这些基准模式较浅、列值多为人类可读字符串。在生产金融数据库中，概念以不透明整数键形式存储，即使简单查询也需多次连接、过滤条件引用不透明ID，导致通用方法准确率低于50%。

Method: FLINT包含三个关键组件：(1) 查找代理，动态将自然语言概念解析为针对具体问题的引用表约束；(2) 基于嵌入的检索，从紧凑的专家编写查询模板库中检索结构相似的模板；(3) 模式链接，通过遍历外键链将大型表模式裁剪为相关子集，而非仅依赖名称相似度。

Result: 在两个生产金融模式数据集（共359个问题）上评估，FLINT在使用相同LLM的情况下超越了多种最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FLINT作为金融数据检索服务的一部分已在生产环境中部署，验证了领域专用设计在解决生产金融数据库Text-to-SQL难题上的有效性。

Abstract: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems achieve strong performance on academic benchmarks like Spider and BIRD, where schemas are relatively shallow and column values are often human readable. In production financial databases, where concepts are stored as opaque integer keys rather than human-readable strings, these methods fall below 50%, as even simple queries require multiple joins and filter predicates reference opaque IDs. We present Financial LINking Text-to-SQL (FLINT), a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system that closes this gap through three key components: (1) a lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, (2) embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, and (3) schema linking that prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. We evaluate on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas. FLINT outperforms various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service.

</details>


### [310] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"任务-工具链协同演化"框架，通过递归式工具链自我改进（RSI）来生成越来越难的推理问题，并证明其生成的数据对下游训练有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成逐渐变难的推理问题需要合成流程随任务分布演化而自适应。现有任务级递归只用生成的问题作为种子，构建工具链本身保持不变，因此难度提升受限。

Method: 提出任务-工具链协同演化框架：在线自我改进在生成过程中把求解器的中间失败转化为可复用技能；任务后自我改进在每批之后修订技能、提示和工作流，仅当在成本有界增长内能生成更难的合法任务时才采纳候选。模型权重与验证标准保持固定。

Result: 在数学、编程和科学领域，十四个演化轮次中求解器平均准确率从100.0%降至54.8%。消融实验显示组合两种更新计划比固定工具链递归或单独任一计划都能生成更难任务，所生成数据提升下游SFT和GRPO表现，27B学生在10K数学样本上于APEX达62.5% mean-16准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在演化任务的同时也演化合成工具链，能够生成难度递增且具有下游训练价值的数据。

Abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [311] [Knowledge or Calculator? Decomposing the Skill Premium in Verifiable Financial Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03564)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FinSkillBench 评测套件，量化金融 AI 智能体的"技能溢价"，发现其取决于模型、资源与评测框架的整体系统而非模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融 AI 智能体不仅需要检索事实，还需正确执行量化操作、可靠使用程序性资源并产出可审计的结构化结果，但现有评测缺乏点对点、可再生的真实基准来量化"技能包"带来的增益。

Method: 构建 FinSkillBench，包含 12 个子任务、2603 个时点清晰的 episode，覆盖组合构建、风险管理与基本面分析，采用隐藏可再生真值与任务专属确定性验证器；在 9 个模型、3 种资源条件下执行 17820 个 episode，对 8 个模型做配对分析，并区分人工撰写文档与可执行领域工具，使用 10 种评分变体与聚类自助法检验稳健性，另用独立实现的第二套框架复现。

Result: 精选技能包使平均分提升 +16.2 分（0.366→0.528），而单 episode 内生成的技能仅 +0.5 分且耗费更多 token 与回合；拆分后文档单独 +5.6 分、工具单独 +19.5 分，二者组合呈次可加性；增益强烈依赖工作流类型，在 10 种评分变体与聚类自助分析下符号稳定，第二套框架复现方向性模式但幅度受工具与数据暴露方式影响。

Conclusion: 可测量的"技能溢价"是完整模型—资源—评测框架系统的属性，而非仅由基础模型决定；执行可执行工具与提供程序性文档的价值随工作流而异。

Abstract: Financial AI agents must do more than retrieve facts: investment workflows require correct quantitative execution, reliable use of procedural resources, and auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite of 2,603 point in time episodes across 12 subtasks in portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, with hidden regenerable ground truth and task specific deterministic verifiers. Executing 17,820 episodes across 9 models and 3 resource conditions, the paired analysis across 8 models shows that curated skill packages raise mean scores by +16.2 points (0.366 to 0.528), whereas skills generated within a single episode add only +0.5 points while consuming more tokens and turns. We then decompose the curated premium by granting human authored procedural documents and executable domain tools separately: documents alone add +5.6 points, tools alone add +19.5 points, and their combination is subadditive. The premium is strongly workflow dependent: executable tools dominate numerically intensive workflows, documentation matters more when procedural or output schema guidance is the bottleneck, and interpretive tasks benefit from both. The effects are sign stable across 10 scoring variants and cluster bootstrap analyses, and an independently implemented second harness reproduces the directional pattern while showing that effect magnitudes depend on how tools and data are exposed. Overall, a measured "skill premium" is a property of the full model, resource, and harness system rather than of the underlying model alone.

</details>


### [312] [Learning to Assess Heartbeat Observability for mmWave Heart-Rate Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03570)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Jianfei Yang,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HEAR双任务Transformer，从毫米波雷达相位谱中学习心率可观测性评分，实现选择性心率估计，仅用仿真训练即可零样本迁移到真实数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达非接触式心率估计需要判断单次测量是否可靠。由于散射体回波的相干叠加即使在大尺度观测几何相似时也会抑制心跳分量，因此必须直接从采集到的测量中评估心跳可观测性，以支持选择性估计。

Method: 1) 构建可控多散射体FMCW仿真器，生成不同可观测性条件下的训练数据；用以已知心率与心跳频带主峰是否一致自动生成可观测性标签。2) 提出HEAR——紧凑型双任务Transformer，同时预测可观测性分数和心率；输入为频谱幅度及相对于呼吸基频的频率，为呼吸谐波提供上下文。

Result: 仅在仿真观测上训练的HEAR零样本迁移到两个公开真实数据集（60 GHz与120 GHz，共134名受试者）。学习到的分数可同时支持HEAR自身心率头与多种现有估计器的选择性预测。在120 GHz数据集上，基于分数的选择使心率头平均绝对误差从全覆盖率17.9 BPM降至50%覆盖率下1.6 BPM。完整流程在边缘设备上端到端延迟50.8 ms。

Conclusion: 心跳可观测性可从测量中学习并用于选择性心率估计，仿真到真实的零样本迁移有效，且方法轻量、可实时部署。

Abstract: Contactless heart-rate sensing with millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires assessing whether individual measurements support reliable estimation. We study learning to assess heartbeat observability, defined as the readability of the heartbeat component in an acquired phase spectrum, for selective heart-rate estimation. Coherent superposition of scatterer returns can suppress this component even under similar macroscopic observation geometry, motivating assessment directly from acquired measurements. To obtain training supervision across different observability conditions, we develop a controllable multi-scatterer frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) simulator. Agreement between the dominant heartbeat-band peak and the known heart rate provides an automatic observability label for each simulated measurement. We propose HEAR (Heartbeat Estimation with Assessed Reliability), a compact dual-task Transformer that jointly predicts an observability score and heart rate. Its input combines spectral magnitudes with frequencies relative to the respiration fundamental, providing context for respiratory harmonics. Trained solely on simulated observations, HEAR transfers zero-shot to two public real-world datasets collected at 60 and 120 GHz from 134 subjects. The same learned score supports selective prediction with both HEAR's own heart-rate head and multiple existing estimators. On the 120 GHz dataset, score-based selection reduces the HR head's mean absolute error from 17.9 BPM at full coverage to 1.6 BPM at 50% coverage. The complete pipeline achieves an end-to-end processing latency of 50.8 ms on an edge device. Project page: https://yuxuanhu9.github.io/HEAR/.

</details>


### [313] [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03574)
*Alham Fikri Aji,Faiz Rizki Ramadhan,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Seung Hun Eddie Han,Ryandito Diandaru,Qinrong Cui,Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Badrinath Chandana,Peerawat Chomphooyod,Ahmed Attia,Jonibek Mansurov,Emilio Villa-Cueva,Canh Duong Nguyen,Imran Turganov,Minghao Wu,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Irina Nikishina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HyperBrowseComp，一个涵盖13种语言、423道人工编写并验证题目的多语言多模态网页浏览基准，用于评估模型在开放网络上进行持久信息检索的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页浏览基准在多语言覆盖、多模态证据类型以及题目难度上存在不足，难以有效评估模型在开放网络中搜寻晦涩证据、串联多步线索的能力，因此需要一个更具挑战性的测试平台。

Method: 构建包含423道题目的基准，题目由母语或高水平使用者用13种语言编写，答案简洁且可公开验证；通过无联网模型评估过滤掉可用参数知识直接回答的简单题目；采用供应商原生搜索与共享外部检索框架，在统一智能体协议下评测多个模型，并对部分题目进行人工评估。

Result: 题目需定位晦涩证据、追踪多步线索或检查视频、扫描文档、图像、地图等异构来源；无联网模型评估有效筛除了较易题目；多个模型在统一协议下完成评测，并获得人工评估对照。

Conclusion: HyperBrowseComp 为跨语言、跨证据模态的持久信息检索提供了高难度测试平台，其难度源于在开放网络上发现并连接证据。

Abstract: We introduce HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual and multimodal browsing benchmark comprising 423 manually authored and human-validated questions across 13 languages, written by native or highly proficient speakers. Questions are designed to be extremely challenging. Each question targets a concise, publicly verifiable answer whose discovery requires locating obscure evidence, following multi-step clue chains, or inspecting heterogeneous sources such as videos, scanned documents, images, or maps.
