Easier questions are filtered out by evaluating them with models without internet access to reduce the likelihood that they can be answered with parametric knowledge alone. We evaluate several models using provider-native search and a shared external retrieval harness under a common agent protocol. To contextualize model performance and effort, we also conduct a human evaluation on a sample of the questions. HyperBrowseComp provides a challenging testbed for persistent information seeking across languages and evidence modalities, with difficulty arising from discovering and connecting evidence on the open web.

</details>


### [314] [HazardWeaver: Scientific Route Selection for Hazard Analysis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03591)
*Wangshu Zhu,Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出状态依赖的科学路径选择问题，并设计HazardWeaver框架（含Hazard Knowledge Compiler、Hazard Capability Graph与Weaver Agent），实现灾害分析中科学方法的选择、执行与动态修正，并构建HazardWeaver Benchmark进行评估，性能优于现有agent系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害的理解与评估对防灾减灾至关重要。大语言模型推动了灾害分析AI agent的发展，但有效自动化要求agent判断在给定事件下哪些科学方法适用且可执行，而随着新证据与执行结果出现，这些条件会变化，需要重新考虑选择。

Method: 将问题形式化为状态依赖的科学路径选择。HazardWeaver包含三部分：Hazard Knowledge Compiler提取与证据关联的适用性条件；Hazard Capability Graph表示可执行科学能力并检查输入输出兼容性；Hazard Weaver Agent基于这两种表示选择适用且可执行的路径、执行工作流，并随分析状态变化修正决策。

Result: 构建Hazard Weaver Benchmark，包含141个实例，覆盖7个单灾种领域和4类多灾种交互。基准允许多条有效科学路径，评估输出正确性、路径有效性与有依据的弃答。实验显示HazardWeaver优于现有agent系统，在多条可行科学路径的任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: HazardWeaver通过知识条件与能力图谱的互补表示，实现了灾害分析中状态依赖的路径选择与动态修正，并在新基准上取得领先性能，代码已公开。

Abstract: Understanding and assessing natural hazards is essential for disaster preparedness and risk reduction. Recent advances in large language models have spurred growing interest in AI agents for hazard analysis, particularly their ability to integrate scientific data, models, and tools into automated workflows. However, effective automation requires agents to determine which scientific methods are appropriate for a given event and executable with the available data and tools. As new evidence and execution results become available, these conditions can change, requiring agents to reconsider their choices. We formulate this problem as state-dependent scientific route selection and introduce HazardWeaver. Specifically, HazardWeaver first leverages the Hazard Knowledge Compiler to extract evidence-linked conditions governing scientific applicability, then its Hazard Capability Graph represents executable scientific capabilities and checks compatibility between their inputs and outputs. Using these complementary representations, the Hazard Weaver Agent component selects applicable and executable routes, carries out their workflows, and revises its decisions as the analysis state changes. To evaluate both the scientific outputs and the decisions that produce them, we introduce the Hazard Weaver Benchmark, comprising 141 instances across seven single-hazard domains and four multi-hazard interaction classes. The benchmark accommodates multiple valid scientific routes and evaluates output correctness, route validity, and justified abstention. Extensive experiments on this benchmark show that HazardWeaver outperforms existing agent systems, with the largest gains on tasks with multiple eligible scientific routes. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LabRAI/HazardWeaver.

</details>


### [315] [Low-Cost Video--Time Priors as a Strong Baseline for EEG--fNIRS Emotion Regression on Familiar Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03618)
*Minghao Kong,Jiurun Chen,Ying Gao,Xiangbin Meng,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究连续情绪回归（效价与唤醒度），提出以"视频-时间先验"作为强基线，并将脑电-近红外（EEG-fNIRS）视为可选残差修正信号；在熟悉视频部署场景下，先验主导的固定融合仅带来有限增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续情绪回归中，训练参与者特定响应可估计逐刻效价与唤醒度。论文关注在"熟悉视频"部署下，检验生理信号是否能在固定融合中提供额外残差修正，以厘清生理信号相对于视频内容先验的实际贡献。

Method: 采用五折、被试留出（subject-held-out）评估，共24名被试；使用先验主导的固定融合框架，并进行源显式（source-explicit）消融，分离视频身份、视频内时间与EEG-fNIRS信号的贡献。

Result: 融合结果在内、外部评估中分别与基线相差0.05与0.32 MAE以内。消融显示大部分误差下降来自视频身份与视频内时间，而EEG-fNIRS增益较小，且随参与者与视频不同而变化。

Conclusion: 视频-时间先验是一种强且低成本的基线；EEG-fNIRS宜定位为熟悉视频情绪回归中的可选残差信号，而非主要贡献来源。

Abstract: Continuous emotion regression estimates moment-to-moment valence and arousal while a viewer watches a video. In familiar-video deployment, responses fron training participant-specific estimate, and prior-dominating fixed fusion tests whether physiology adds residual correction. In five-fold subject-held-out evaluation on 24was within 0.05 and 0.32 MAE of fusion in the internal and external evaluations, respectively. Source-explicit ablations showed that video identity and within-video tine accounted for most of the reduction, while EG-FNIRS gains were smaller and varied across participants and videos. These results identify the video-time prior as a strong, low-cost baseline and position EEG-fNIRS as an optional residual signal for familiar-video emotion regression.

</details>


### [316] [Depth as Time in One-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03626)
*Arnold Caleb Asiimwe,William Yang,Sanghyuk Chun,Esin Tureci,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一步生成模型并未消除扩散的时间计算，而是将其重组织到网络深度中——提出"深度即时间"现象：多步扩散的去噪计算沿单次前向传播的深度展开，且该深度计算取决于流映射所训练的传输任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步生成模型（经蒸馏或学习流映射压缩多步扩散轨迹）已能生成高质量图像，作者由此提出自然的问题：当生成被压缩为单次前向传播时，多步扩散的去噪轨迹发生了什么？探究这一问题有助于理解一步生成模型的内部机制及其可压缩性。

Method: 采用经验性分析方法：用模型自身的输出头解码中间层，探测单次前向传播中各层的计算；按传输任务比较不同模型（如MeanFlow 与 drifting models）；通过探测更短传输区间以观察 MeanFlow 中单个网络评估内同时出现的去噪与再加噪（denoise-then-renoise）行为；并显式将逐层计算视为流，训练单个时间条件块跨层去噪以实现压缩。

Result: 发现"深度即时间"现象：多步扩散的去噪计算沿网络深度展开，可由解码中间层恢复；该深度计算依赖所训练的传输任务，MeanFlow 在更短传输区间下展现出单次评估内的去噪与再加噪，而无时间索引传输任务的生成器（如 drifting models）不具备同样的深度去噪；呈现深度去噪现象的模型更易跨层压缩——MeanFlow SiT-L/2 可参数压缩16.6倍为单个时间条件块。

Conclusion: 扩散的时序计算并未被一步生成消除，而是被重组织到网络深度之中；作者也给出了先去噪后加噪行为的解释。

Abstract: The recent wave of one-step generative models, which compress the multi-step trajectory of diffusion via either distillation or learned flow maps, has reached an inflection point where they can generate high-quality images. Here, we ask a natural question that follows from these advances: what happens to the denoising trajectory of multi-step diffusion when generation is compressed into a single forward pass? We offer an empirical observation we call \textit{depth as time}: the denoising computation that multi-step diffusion performs across sampling steps appears to unfold across the depth of a single forward pass, and can be recovered by decoding intermediate layers with the model's own output head. Most interestingly, we show that this depthwise computation depends on the transport task a flow map is trained to solve. The most surprising case is MeanFlow, where probing shorter transport intervals reveals both denoising and renoising within a single network evaluation. In contrast, generators trained without a time-indexed transport task, such as drifting models, do not exhibit the same depthwise denoising. Consequently, we show that models that exhibit the depthwise denoising phenomenon are more compressible across the layerwise computation: a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model can be compressed by $16.6\times$ in parameters into a single time-conditioned block. We offer an explanation for this denoise-then-renoise behavior and show that, when we treat the layerwise computation explicitly as a flow, a single time-conditioned block can be trained to denoise across layers, compressing a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model by $16.6\times$ in parameters. Together, these results suggest that the temporal computation of diffusion is not eliminated by one-step generation, but reorganized across network depth.

</details>


### [317] [NeutronGym: Physics-Graded Neutron Instrument Design for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03631)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 NeutronGym——首个可执行的中子仪器设计环境，通过 McStas 光线追踪与分级评分（无 LLM 裁判）评估语言模型能否"做物理"而非"背物理"，并可用于强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以区分语言模型是在真正进行物理设计还是仅仅回忆已知结果，因此需要一个评分客观、不可争辩、且能防止记忆泄漏的可执行环境来检验模型的物理设计与推理能力。

Method: 构建可执行的中子仪器设计环境 NeutronGym：智能体通过验证工具构建仪器，McStas 进行光线追踪，采用分层递进的评分阶梯对语法、运行时、结构与科学性打分；程序化家族提供无限实例并设置保留参数区间；精选切片 McStasBench 包含 16 个已发表仪器任务，并配记忆探测与沙盒机制。此外利用其奖励进行强化学习训练。

Result: 七个模型最多仅复现 16 个任务中的 7 个，无一能检索到参考方案，也无模型达到改进目标。强化学习使 Qwen3-8B 在保留实例上的表现从 11% 提升至 77%（另一随机种子为 69%），超过未训练的 Qwen3-32B，并在三个额外受限家族上各用一个种子复现成功。移除评分阶梯的部分得分后性能骤降 60 个百分点。仅凭奖励训练，模型在智能体仿真预算下达到经典优化器的水平，但前提是给定闭式物理（77% 对 81%，在此规模下差距不显著）；前沿模型仍可解出 98-99%。

Conclusion: NeutronGym 提供了无 LLM 裁判的可信评估与训练环境，表明语言模型智能体在物理设计上仍有明显局限；实验还揭示了分层部分得分的重要性，并公开了导致四种无模型基线即可解出之任务设计的探测工具。

Abstract: Designing a scientific instrument tests whether language-model agents can do physics rather than recall it, provided the grading cannot be argued with. We introduce NeutronGym, to our knowledge the first executable environment for neutron instrument design: agents build instruments through validating tools, McStas ray-traces what they build, and a level-resolved ladder grades syntax, runtime, structure and science with no LLM judge. Procedural families supply unlimited instances of a fixed layout whose design parameters the agent must set, with held-out parameter regimes; a curated slice, McStasBench, adds 16 tasks from published instruments behind memorization probes and a sandbox. Seven models reproduce at most 7 of the 16, none retrieves a reference, and none meets an improvement target. The environment also trains. Reinforcement learning on its reward takes Qwen3-8B from 11% to 77% of held-out instances of a family whose targets come from a hidden design (69% at a second seed), past an untrained Qwen3-32B, and the recipe holds, at one seed each, on three further gated families. The analysis says what that gain is. Without the ladder's partial credit it collapses by 60 points. From reward alone the trained model reaches what a classical optimizer reaches, at the agent's simulation budget, only when handed the closed-form physics (77% against 81%, a gap that does not separate at this size), while frontier models still solve 98-99%. Getting a trustworthy result meant failing four task designs that no-model baselines could solve, and we release the probes that found them.

</details>


### [318] [Credit Where It Matters: Dependency-Aware Policy Optimization for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03634)
*Yu Li,Guangfeng Cai,Long-Fei Li,Shuo Han,Shengtian Yang,Han Luo,Kaibing Yang,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DepGPO，利用命令间执行依赖构建依赖图，从验证器检查的资源反向追溯，将信用分配给相关写操作及其支撑读操作，并据此重分配轨迹优势，提升终端任务性能与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端任务的后续命令常依赖前面命令产生的信息或中间结果，但现有轨迹级和步级信用分配方法未显式追踪命令间的读写依赖，导致训练信号可能被分配给无关操作，削弱对关键步骤的学习。

Method: 从执行轨迹构建命令依赖图，从任务验证器检查的资源出发反向追溯；沿这些路径将信用分配给相关写操作及其支撑读操作；再用该信用在步级别重分配轨迹优势。

Result: 大量对比实验与消融研究表明，DepGPO在复杂终端任务上提升了任务性能与训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用命令间执行依赖进行信用分配，DepGPO能更精准地将训练信号集中到真正影响结果的步骤，从而改善终端智能体的强化学习效果。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents benefit from reinforcement learning (RL) in coding, debugging, and other multi-step terminal tasks. In these tasks, later commands often depend on information or intermediate results produced by earlier commands. However, existing trajectory-level and step-level credit assignment methods do not explicitly trace the read-write dependencies through which commands affect the final outcome. Consequently, training signals could still be assigned to irrelevant operations, weakening learning from relevant steps. In this paper, we propose Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO), which uses execution dependencies between commands to guide credit assignment for terminal agents. Specifically, we construct a command dependency graph from execution traces and trace backward from the resources inspected by the task verifier. We then assign credit to relevant writes and their supporting reads along these paths, and use it to redistribute trajectory advantages across steps. Extensive comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate that DepGPO improves task performance and training stability on complex terminal tasks.

</details>


### [319] [Do Large Language Models Know Colombian Law? A Reliability Benchmark for the Colombian Legal System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03639)
*Rubén Manrique,Michelle Castellanos,Jorge Morales,Juan David Gutiérrez,Antonio Barreto Rozo,Joaquín Vélez Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个经专家验证的哥伦比亚法律体系LLM可靠性基准（1,042题，覆盖10个法律领域、3种题型），评测15个模型发现：闭式题准确率最高0.905，但自由文本事实正确率不超过0.45，且答案相关性与正确性出现解离，仅约半数引用法条正确，说明当前LLM在哥伦比亚法律任务中仍需专家监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于法律实践、教育与研究，但其在美国以外国家法律体系中的可靠性缺乏系统记录。论文旨在填补哥伦比亚法律体系下LLM可靠性评测的空白。

Method: 通过人在回路（human-in-the-loop）流程与多阶段专家评审，构建包含1,042个条目、涵盖十个法律领域和三种题型（封闭多选、半开放、开放式IRAC）的专家验证基准；对15个当代闭源与开源模型采用与题型匹配的指标进行评测，并使用独立评分标准LLM评审与盲态人类专家打分进行交叉验证。

Result: 封闭题准确率跨度大（Gemini 3.1 Pro 0.905 至 0.577）；自由文本事实正确率任何模型均不超过0.45（0–1尺度）。答案相关性与正确性呈负相关（Spearman rho = -0.46），模型常"听起来对但答案错"。封闭题准确率与自由文本正确性排名强相关（rho = 0.94）。LLM评审与人类专家打分复现自由文本排名（rho >= 0.88）。模型引用的法条中仅约一半正确，其余错误或不存在。可靠性随法律领域系统性变化，并随问题复杂度呈倒U型。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在哥伦比亚法律任务中需要专家监督，将答案锚定于权威来源是提升可靠性的可行路径。论文发布基准构建流程以支持可复现评测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support legal practice, education, and research, yet their reliability in national legal systems outside the United States remains largely undocumented. We introduce an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLM reliability on the Colombian legal system. The benchmark comprises 1,042 items spanning ten areas of law and three question formats (closed multiple-choice, semi-open, and open-ended IRAC), built through a human-in-the-loop pipeline with multi-stage expert review. We evaluate 15 contemporary proprietary and open-weight models with format-appropriate metrics. Accuracy on closed questions ranges widely, from 0.905 (Gemini 3.1 Pro) to 0.577, but on free-text legal answers factual correctness never exceeds 0.45 (on a 0-1 scale) for any model. We find a dissociation between answer relevancy and correctness (Spearman rho = -0.46): models reliably sound responsive while frequently being wrong, a pattern of particular concern for non-expert users. Closed-question accuracy and free-text correctness are strongly rank-correlated (rho = 0.94), so cheap multiple-choice screening predicts model ranking but overstates absolute reliability. An independent rubric-based LLM judge and blind human expert scoring both reproduce the free-text ranking (rho >= 0.88). The judge further reveals that only about half of the norms models cite are correct; the rest are wrong or non-existent. Reliability varies systematically by legal area and follows an inverted-U across question complexity. Our results indicate that current LLMs require expert supervision for Colombian legal tasks, and that grounding answers in authoritative sources is a promising path to higher reliability. We release the benchmark construction pipeline to support reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [320] [MRVQ: One Resident Index for Dimension- and Rate-Elastic Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03651)
*Sean Culatana,Shang-En Huang,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MRVQ是一种后处理残差量化器，用单一常驻编码同时支持多种维度与码率，显著降低检索服务内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索服务需随延迟、质量和内存预算变化，在嵌入前缀维度与索引码率间切换；为每个码率单独调优量化器质量最佳，但检索层需同时持有多个码流与量化器状态，内存开销大。

Method: 提出Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization（MRVQ），面向冻结嵌入的后处理残差量化器。其最高码率编码可按两种方式截断：丢弃残差阶段降低码率，丢弃嵌入坐标降低维度。另评估一种低构建成本的PCA-scalar设计。

Result: 在FiQA、NFCorpus、四种嵌入家族及{4,8,16}字节码上，MRVQ为最低RAM设计：内存比三个独立训练的QINCo2索引少17.8-22.0倍，比精简共享模型steelman少1.89-2.02倍。代价是逐码率QINCo2在FiQA上nDCG@10高0.026-0.107；但MRVQ在同等码长下优于PQ、OPQ、AdANNS-OPQ。PCA-scalar质量接近RaBitQ及其扩展，中位构建速度快420倍。两项负面结果：QINCo2高码率训练崩溃；排序界假设未达预设接受标准。

Conclusion: MRVQ是弹性检索的低内存工作点，而非通用质量赢家。

Abstract: Dense-retrieval services must switch among embedding-prefix dimensions and index bit rates as latency, quality, and memory budgets change. Tuning a quantizer separately for each rate gives the best quality, but the retrieval tier then holds several code streams and quantizer states at once. We introduce Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization (MRVQ), a post-hoc residual quantizer for frozen embeddings. Its maximum-rate code can be truncated two ways: dropping residual stages lowers the rate, and dropping embedding coordinates lowers the dimension. One resident artifact therefore serves every (dimension, rate) pair we evaluate. Across FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, and {4, 8, 16}-byte codes, MRVQ is the lowest-RAM design we evaluate. It uses 17.8-22.0x less memory than three separately trained QINCo2 indices, and 1.89-2.02x less than a lean shared-model steelman. The saving is not free: per-rate QINCo2 is 0.026-0.107 nDCG@10 better on FiQA. But MRVQ beats PQ, OPQ, and AdANNS-OPQ at matched code size. We also evaluate a low-build-cost PCA-scalar design that attains quality comparable to RaBitQ and its extension while fitting 420x faster at the median. Finally, we report two negative results: QINCo2 collapses when trained at high rates, and a ranking-bound hypothesis misses its pre-specified acceptance criteria. MRVQ is therefore a low-memory operating point for elastic retrieval, not a universal quality winner.

</details>


### [321] [Transcriptome-informed multi-modal AI for predicting neoadjuvant therapy response from breast cancer biopsies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03693)
*Jungkyu Park,Dhruva Biswas,Joseph Cappadona,Cerise Tang,Ken G. Zeng,Bartosz Machura,Chuwen Liu,Paolo Tarantino,Coral Omene,Francisco J. Esteva,Rohit Bhargava,Marcin Braun,Kamila Paździerz,Jakub Czerwiński,Hanna Romańska-Knight,Albert Grinshpun,Bareket Daniel,Michele Buchinger,Frederick Howard,Piotr Wysocki,Brie Chun,Freya Schnabel,Rich Caruana,Jan Witowski,Krzysztof J. Geras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个两阶段AI模型，先由病理图像推断转录组，再预测乳腺癌新辅助治疗的病理完全缓解（pCR），在多个队列中取得稳定且优于病理生物标志物的判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学中标注数据稀缺限制了深度学习生物标志物的发展。

Method: 两阶段AI模型：第一阶段利用32种癌症类型、8,742例患者的组织病理学图像学习转录组（经病理学家复核并与实测表达的空间一致性验证）；第二阶段基于推断的表达和临床变量预测pCR。开发使用1,080例患者（5个队列），评估使用1,412例患者（9个队列），并进行消融实验。

Result: 模型汇总AUROC为0.79（95% CI，0.73-0.85），可在分子亚型内区分应答者；优于组织病理学生物标志物；在不同瘤内采样和极少活检组织下保持稳定；消融显示转录组范围推断优于仅用临床变量或一阶段病理模型，并规避基因组检测的基因选择限制而具备稳健性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，生物信息驱动的压缩方法可能推广至精准肿瘤学中数据稀缺的应用场景。

Abstract: Scarcity of labeled data limits development of deep learning biomarkers in oncology. We develop a two-stage AI model predicting pathological complete response (pCR) to neoadjuvant therapy in breast cancer. The first stage learns the transcriptome from histopathology using 8,742 patients across 32 cancer types, corroborated by pathologist review and spatial agreement with measured expression. This simplifies the second stage to predicting pCR from inferred expression and clinical variables. Developed using 1,080 patients (five cohorts) and evaluated in 1,412 patients (nine cohorts), the model achieves a pooled AUROC of 0.79 (95% CI, 0.73-0.85), discriminating responders within molecular subtypes. It outperforms histopathological biomarkers, remaining stable across intratumoral sampling and with minimal biopsy tissue. Ablations show transcriptome-wide inference improves discrimination over clinical variables alone or one-stage pathology models, and robustness by avoiding genomic assays' gene selection constraints. These results indicate that biologically informed compression may generalize to data-sparse applications in precision oncology.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [322] [Automating the Application of HCI Principles: Skills for On-Demand UI Construction, the Human-AI Space to Think, and the Future of HCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02369)
*Nathan Conklin,Miranda Capra,Chris North*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出将人机对话构建为"思考空间"，把经典HCI设计知识编码为机器可读的skill.md技能文件，使生成式AI在按需生成界面时自动满足可用性、可访问性与一致性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已能根据自然语言任务描述按需生成功能可用的用户界面，下一步挑战是从"能生成界面"走向"把界面生成得好"。而传统HCI设计知识（启发式规则、可供性、无障碍标准、认知负荷约束等）目前仍以人工清单形式存在，难以被生成式系统直接利用。

Method: 提出一个框架，将用户与AI的对话视为共享的、结构化的认知工作空间（Space to Think），通过任务分解按需生成作为用户思维延伸的界面；同时把Nielsen启发式、Norman可供性、WCAG成功标准、认知负荷约束与混合主动原则编码为机器可读的skill.md技能文件，由生成智能体在运行时作为软件工程工具加载。

Result: 将HCI设计知识转化为声明式、可检查、可版本控制、可编辑且由HCI共同体自主掌握的产物，从而使可访问性、可学习性和一致性成为生成过程的属性，而非最终产品的属性。

Conclusion: 论文勾勒出一条研究议程，展望HCI领域从当前基于人工设计与启发式清单的实践，转向设计技艺机器可读、可执行且开放的未来。

Abstract: Human-computer interaction (HCI) is in the middle of a transition: large language models can now generate functional user interfaces (UIs) on demand from natural-language task descriptions. A user explains what they are trying to accomplish, and the system materializes a working interface to support it. This capability already exists in systems such as Claude and ChatGPT and continues to grow in fidelity as the underlying models improve. The next step along this trajectory is to move from interfaces that are merely generated to interfaces that are generated well. We propose a framework in which the dialogue between user and artificial intelligence (AI) becomes a Space to Think: a shared, structured cognitive workspace in which task decomposition produces an on-demand user interface as an extension of the user's thinking rather than as a separate artifact. Within this paradigm, classical HCI design knowledge (Nielsen's heuristics, Norman's affordance prescriptions, Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) success criteria, cognitive-load constraints, and mixed-initiative principles) is encoded as skills: machine-readable skill.md files that the generating agent loads at runtime as software engineering tools. Skills turn HCI design knowledge into declarative, inspectable, version-controlled, and editable artifacts owned by the HCI community itself so that accessibility, learnability, and consistency become properties of a generative process rather than properties of a finished product. We outline a research agenda depicting a future where the HCI field transitions from today's design and knowledge heuristic checklist towards a future where the craft becomes machine-readable, executable, and open.

</details>


### [323] [Connectedness, Cognitive Load, and Human-AI Oversight in Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02384)
*Nathan Conklin,Peng Gao,Chris North*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出一种"连通性感知"的监督引擎，用环境与连通性遥测作为操作员认知负载的非侵入式代理，将人工监督需求由单纯风险驱动转变为"风险+环境派生的操作员能力"共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助网络态势感知会生成大量机器推理轨迹，需人工复核，但信号与轨迹的到达速度远超操作员处理能力，人工复核成为监督瓶颈。主流做法依据模型侧信号（置信度、不确定性）挑选最危险事件，却忽略了随时变环境剧烈波动的操作员认知容量。

Method: 以系统的环境与连通性遥测（如深度、活跃通信路径数、被跟踪接触图像密度、作战节奏）作为操作员负载的可用且非侵入式代理；该遥测同时决定人机协作能否获得更广泛集体支持；据此提出连通性感知监督引擎的参考架构，并说明其日常用例及在海下网络防御工具包中的整合方式。

Result: 在海事平台场景中，深度、活跃通信路径数、接触图像密度与作战节奏等环境与连通性属性均携带该信号；人工监督需求被形式化为风险与环境派生操作员容量的组合决策，并给出架构与用例演示。

Conclusion: 两点结论：1）操作员的环境状态本身即是一种连通性度量；2）连通性驱动认知负载，并界定人机网络作战中的集体边界。

Abstract: AI-assisted cyber situational awareness triggers machine-generated reasoning traces (step-by-step justifications for anomaly classifications) that a human operator is expected to review. Because cyber signals and their traces arrive faster than any operator can process, human review is the limiting constraint on oversight. The standard approach is to identify the riskiest cyber events for review using model-side signals such as confidence or uncertainty. That framing ignores the operator's cognitive capacity which varies sharply with the operational environment. We propose an alternative where the system's environmental and connectivity telemetry serves as an available, non-invasive proxy for operator load. That same telemetry determines whether the human-AI partnership can reach the broader collective for support. In a maritime platform, environmental and connectivity attributes including depth, number of active communications paths, density of the tracked contact picture, and operational tempo all carry this signal. Need for operator oversight becomes a decision that materializes as a combination of both risk and environment-derived operator capacity. We present a reference architecture for a connectedness-aware oversight engine, demonstrating everyday use cases alongside its intended incorporation into the submarine cyber-defense toolkit. Two themes emerge: 1) the operator's environmental state is itself a connectedness measurement, and 2) connectedness drives the cognitive load and defines a collective boundary in human-AI cyber operations.

</details>


### [324] [Santiago's A.T. Field: Visualizing Urban Accessibility through an Evangelion-Inspired Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02562)
*Eduardo Graells-Garrido,Ignacio Pérez-Messina,Claudio Gaete*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文将《新世纪福音战士》第13集中的虚构诊断界面转译为基于真实数据的城市可达性交互可视化（Santiago's A.T. Field），并总结转译过程中的三类张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科幻界面常被只复制外观，其诊断逻辑很少围绕真实数据重建。作者希望把虚构界面真正"运行化"，探索虚构语言与真实模型对接时的代价。

Method: 分五阶段转译：现象（phenomenon）、场（field）、进程（progression）、界面（interface）、响应（response）。将微观"使徒"转译为关系性城市问题，把虚构扫描格阵转译为用E2SFCA方法计算的可达性场，使虚构显示中的每个元素都对应模型中的具体量。

Result: 实现了一个交互式城市可达性可视化系统（Santiago's A.T. Field）。过程中暴露出三类张力：类型化饱和配色与色觉缺陷的冲突；类型化动效与"减少动态效果"偏好的冲突；逐帧动画的性能开销。此外指出应急美学可能将城市人群呈现为威胁。

Conclusion: 这三类张力即是转译的"成本"，也是此类"虚构界面—真实数据"转译工作可迁移的经验，供后续同类尝试参考。

Abstract: Science-fiction interfaces are often reproduced for their appearance, and their diagnostic logic is rarely rebuilt around real data. Here we describe Santiago's A.T. Field, an interactive visualization of urban accessibility built from a diagnostic interface in Episode 13 of Neon Genesis Evangelion. We translated that interface in five stages: phenomenon, field, progression, interface, and response. A microscopic Angel became a relational urban problem, and a fictional scanning lattice became an accessibility field computed with the E2SFCA method. Every element of the fictional display is tied to a quantity of the model. Three tensions appeared when we made the fictional language operational: the saturated palette of the genre against color-vision deficiency, the motion of the genre against the reduced-motion preference, and the per-frame cost of the animation. The urgency of the fictional command center has its own cost, because emergency aesthetics can cast urban populations as threats. These three tensions are what the translation cost, and they are what transfers to other attempts of this kind.

</details>


### [325] [Asterism: Exploring and Synthesizing Scattered Observations into Literature-Grounded Hypotheses and Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02673)
*Joseph Chee Chang,Michael D'Arcy,Amy X. Zhang,Pao Siangliulue,Sangho Suh,Aakanksha Naik,Jena D. Hwang,Javier Ramos Benitez,Stella Wroblewski,Matt Latzke,Michael Cuoco,Ruben Lozano-Aguilera,Kris Ganjam,Joel Chan,Doug Downey,Peter Jansen,Kyle J. Travaglini,Daniel S. Weld*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Asterism 是一个支持研究者主导理论构建的系统，将数百篇论文中的观察抽取为概念-关系三元组并统一到层级本体中，研究者据此编排证据图、按不同粒度聚合观察，从而在保持自身偏好与研究问题的前提下形成理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论需要把大量独立观察整合进一个框架并提出新假设，但研究者必须综合分散在众多论文中的观察，这些论文描述相关概念却常用不同术语；哪些概念最重要还取决于研究者的偏好与研究问题。现有基于大语言模型的理论综合方法虽能扩展规模，却把研究者的选择与直觉自动化掉了。

Method: Asterism 从数百篇论文中抽取观察，表示为概念-关系三元组，并把概念统一在一个层级本体中；研究者利用该本体编排证据图，并在不同粒度层级上聚合观察，将理论构建集中于感兴趣的特定现象。

Result: 在一项包含 10 名研究者的实地部署中，研究者从观察出发走向理论，并保留了契合其偏好的概念与假设。在免疫学与农业研究者团队的两个案例研究中，他们发现了超出常规分析范围的机制，并构建了值得后续实验验证的假设。

Conclusion: Asterism 在保留研究者选择与直觉的同时实现了大规模理论综合，支持研究者从观察走向理论，并在真实科研场景中帮助跨领域团队发现新机制、提出可跟进验证的假设。

Abstract: A theory draws many independent observations into one framework with novel hypotheses. A researcher building such a theory must synthesize observations scattered across many papers, each describing related concepts but often in different terms. Which concepts matter most also depends on their preferences and research questions. Recent approaches scale theory synthesis with LLMs, but automate away choices and intuitions from researchers. We present Asterism, which extracts observations from hundreds of papers as concept-relation triples, with concepts unified in a hierarchical ontology. Researchers curate an evidence graph using the ontology and aggregate observations at different levels of granularity to focus theory formation on specific phenomena of interest. In a field deployment (n=10), researchers worked from observations to theories, and kept concepts and hypotheses fitting their preferences. In two case studies, teams of immunology and agriculture researchers discovered mechanisms outside their standard analyses and constructed hypotheses worth follow-up experiments.

</details>


### [326] [Characterizing the Performance Gap in Human Activity Recognition for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02711)
*Hossein Khayami,Sungjin Hwang,Eshed Ohn-Bar,David E. Conroy,Amanda Lazar,Eun Kyoung Choe,Hernisa Kacorri*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 腕戴加速度计HAR模型多以年轻成人数据集为基准，本文用老年数据集MyMove检验其泛化性，发现基准提升难以迁移至老年人，自监督表征可缩小差距但未消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴HAR模型主要在年轻成人数据集上开发与评估，尚不清楚基准上的进展是否能泛化到不同年龄群体，尤其是老年人。

Method: 利用自建自由生活老年人HAR数据集MyMove（平均年龄71），在留一被试（LOSO）与跨数据集迁移两种设定下评估多种深度学习架构与训练策略，并引入在年龄多样化UK Biobank上预训练的冻结自监督特征进行对比。

Result: 年轻成人基准上的性能提升无法同等迁移到老年人数据，存在持续甚至扩大的性能差距；更丰富的表征（尤其冻结自监督特征）显著提升老年人数据表现并持续缩小差距，但对年轻成人性能有适度损失，差异仍未完全消除。

Conclusion: 仅靠基准提升与架构扩展不足以全面反映可穿戴HAR的进展，未来需发展能更好捕捉人群多样性的表征，并结合对个体运动模式与日常习惯的个性化适配。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) from wrist-worn accelerometers is increasingly used for health and behavioral tracking. Yet, most wearable HAR models are developed and evaluated on datasets dominated by younger adults, leaving it unclear whether benchmark progress generalizes across age groups. In this work, we leverage MyMove, our carefully annotated, free-living older-adult HAR dataset (mean age 71), to evaluate deep-learning architectures and training regimes under both leave-one-subject-out and cross-dataset transfer. We find that improvements on younger-adult benchmarks fail to transfer equally to data collected from older adults, resulting in a persistent and often widening performance gap. However, richer representations, particularly frozen self-supervised features pretrained on the age-diverse UK Biobank dataset, substantially improve performance on data from older adults and consistently narrow the performance gap, at modest cost to younger-adult performance, though disparities remain. These findings suggest that benchmark gains and architectural scaling alone provide an incomplete picture of progress in wearable HAR, and broader advances may require representations that better capture population diversity, alongside personalized adaptation to individual movement patterns and routines.

</details>


### [327] [Co-Designing AI For Mental Health Support With Young Adults of Color (YOC): Needs, Expectations, and Implications for AI Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02812)
*Elaine Dabin Jeon,John Bosco S. Bunyi,Hannah Kim,Renkai Ma,Yaman Yu,Michal Luria,Jason Yip,Alexis Hiniker,Katie Davis,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对13名18-24岁亚裔、黑人和西班牙裔/拉丁裔青年的两天协同设计工作坊，研究发现少数族裔青年希望AI聊天机器人提供身份知情式个性化支持，而非泛泛建议，并揭示了隐私、AI素养与问责方面的设计缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少数族裔青年面临更高的心理健康挑战与就医障碍，但他们处于青少年保护机制与成人导向AI系统之间的空白地带。目前鲜有研究了解他们如何使用AI聊天机器人获得心理健康支持，以及社会文化背景如何塑造其期望、担忧与设计偏好。

Method: 招募13名18-24岁的亚裔、黑人和西班牙裔/拉丁裔青年，开展为期两天的协同设计工作坊（co-design workshop）。

Result: 参与者认为通用聊天机器人建议会抹平其真实生活体验；他们更希望获得身份知情式自我披露的机会，而非被做出错误假设。对以少数族裔青年为中心的个性化偏好也暴露出隐私与AI素养方面的缺口。参与者就聊天机器人的治疗角色进行协商，并寻求更强的AI问责性与用户自主权，凸显临床与非临床AI中介支持之间的边界模糊。

Conclusion: 研究建议将AI素养与心理健康素养相结合，并在隐私权衡（privacy calculus）中纳入少数族裔青年的经验，为AI心理健康支持的设计提供方向。

Abstract: Young adults of color (YOC) face heightened mental health challenges and barriers to care while navigating developmental and life transitions. Situated between youth-oriented safeguards and adult-oriented AI systems, little is known about how they use AI chatbots for mental health and well-being support or how sociocultural contexts shape their expectations, concerns, and design preferences. We conducted a two-day co-design workshop with 13 Asian, Black, and Hispanic/Latino/a young adults aged 18--24. Participants found generic chatbot advice to flatten their lived experiences; rather than making incorrect assumptions, they wanted more opportunities for identity-informed disclosure. Preferences for YOC-centered personalization also revealed gaps in privacy and AI literacy. Participants negotiated different therapeutic roles for chatbots and sought greater AI accountability and user agency, highlighting blurred boundaries between clinical and non-clinical AI-mediated support. Findings suggest directions for integrating AI literacy with mental health literacy and centering YOC's experiences in the privacy calculus.

</details>


### [328] [Tracking Human Daily Cognitive Activity from EEG and Biometric Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02971)
*Alina Gutoreva,Zhaniya Omar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个多模态框架，用脑电、可穿戴生理信号、行为情境与自我报告数据追踪真实环境中的认知活动，并通过3名被试、280个10分钟标注片段的小规模试点研究验证其可行性，发现了动机与能量的昼夜节律模式及多指标预测关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常生活中的认知活动具有动态、情境依赖和多模态特征，传统实验室研究难以捕捉真实世界的认知过程，而单一模态方法只能提供对认知状态的部分洞察。可穿戴传感技术的进步使得采集多源异构数据流成为可能，因此有必要构建一个能够在真实环境中全面追踪人类认知活动的多模态框架。

Method: 作者构建了一个多模态认知活动追踪框架，整合脑电（EEG）、可穿戴生理信号、行为情境以及自我报告测量。开展了一项初步试点研究，采集3名参与者在两周内共280个标注的10分钟时间片段的数据，覆盖九个活动领域。分析方法包括时间模式分析、相关性分析、线性回归建模（以唤醒度、注意力、能量和压力预测动机）以及基于滞后的时间依赖分析。

Result: 结果显示出一致的时间模式：13:00出现动机与能量的明显午间下降，随后下午回升；工作与IADLs的flow状态比例最高（51%和50%），ADLs最低（15%）；动机与唤醒度（r=0.78）和注意力（r=0.74）强相关，与感知压力呈较弱负相关（r=-0.33）；线性回归模型预测动机达到R²=0.76（MAE=9.84，RMSE=12.85）；滞后分析表明先前能量水平可正向预测后续动机，证实了认知动态中的时间依赖性。

Conclusion: 研究表明在真实环境中进行多模态认知活动分析具有可行性，并强调了整合生理与行为指标的重要性。该框架为未来大规模多模态系统及应用型智能解决方案奠定了基础。

Abstract: Understanding human cognitive activity in everyday life remains challenging due to the dynamic, context-dependent, and multimodal nature of cognition. Laboratory-based studies often fail to capture real-world cognitive processes, while single-modality approaches provide only partial insight into cognitive states. Advances in wearable sensing now enable the collection of heterogeneous data streams for a more comprehensive view of daily cognition. This paper presents a multimodal framework for tracking human cognitive activity using electroencephalography (EEG), wearable physiological signals, behavioral context, and self-reported measures. A preliminary pilot study was conducted with observational data from three participants (N = 3) over 280 annotated 10-minute intervals spanning nine activity domains across two weeks.
  Results reveal consistent temporal patterns, including a discernible mid-day decrease in motivation and energy at 13:00, followed by afternoon recovery. Work and IADLs yielded the highest flow state rates (51% and 50%), while ADLs produced the lowest (15%). Motivation correlated strongly with arousal (r = 0.78) and attention (r = 0.74), whereas perceived stress showed a weaker negative relationship (r = -0.33). A linear regression model predicting motivation from arousal, attention, energy, and stress achieved R2 = 0.76 (MAE = 9.84, RMSE = 12.85). Lag-based analysis indicates that prior energy levels positively predict subsequent motivation, confirming temporal dependencies in cognitive dynamics.
  These findings demonstrate the feasibility of multimodal cognitive activity analysis in real-world environments and highlight the importance of integrating physiological and behavioral indicators. The proposed framework provides a foundation for future large-scale multimodal systems and applied intelligent solutions.

</details>


### [329] [The Effects of Air-Conditioning and Road-Traffic Noise on Perceived, Cognitive, and EEG Responses in a University Classroom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03210)
*Yuanzhi Su,Cynthia Hou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在教室空调与交通噪声实验中，认知成绩在55/60/65 dBA条件下无显著差异，但主观感知（响度、愉悦度、唤醒度、工作负荷）和任务期EEG功率在特定任务与脑区存在差异，表明无成绩差异不等于条件等效，教室噪声评估应结合主观响应与EEG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教室中空调声与道路交通噪声常见，通常以认知成绩判断其影响；但成绩未受影响的噪声仍可能被不同地感知或改变脑活动。本研究旨在检验主观感知、认知与EEG三类响应是否一致，以及感知与EEG能否区分成绩无法区分的条件。

Method: 采用被试内设计，16名学生在大学教室内于空调声与交通噪声下完成认知任务，各声源分别以55、60、65 dBA播放并设无噪声对照。每种条件后评定声学评价与感知工作负荷，任务期间同步记录EEG。

Result: 各任务认知成绩在条件间均无显著差异。相比之下，噪声被评为更响、更不愉悦、更唤醒，且三项评分随声压级变化，噪声下感知工作负荷更高。任务期EEG功率在特定任务与头皮区域呈声源或声级差异。被试内相对EEG频带功率主要与响度和愉悦度共变：更响、更不愉悦伴随更高相对theta与更低相对gamma功率；与唤醒及成绩的关联很少，与工作负荷无关联。

Conclusion: 三类响应仅部分一致，主观感知与EEG可区分成绩未能区分的条件。成绩无显著差异并不意味着条件对学生等效，教室空调与交通噪声评估应在认知成绩之外纳入主观感知，并以EEG提供任务期神经活动的补充信息。

Abstract: Air-conditioning and road-traffic noise are common in classrooms, and their effects are often judged by cognitive performance. However, a sound that leaves performance unchanged may still be perceived differently or alter brain activity. In a within-participant design, this study tested whether perceived, cognitive, and EEG responses are consistent, and whether perceived and EEG responses distinguish conditions that performance does not. Sixteen students completed cognitive tasks in a university classroom under air-conditioning and road-traffic noise, each played back at 55, 60, and 65 dBA. Participants rated acoustic appraisal and perceived workload after each condition, and EEG was recorded during the tasks. The results show that cognitive performance did not differ significantly between conditions in any task. By contrast, noise was rated louder, less pleasant, and more arousing than a no-noise control, and all three ratings varied with sound level; perceived workload was also higher under noise. Task-period EEG power also showed significant differences between sources or levels in specific tasks and scalp regions. Within participants, relative EEG band power covaried mainly with loudness and pleasantness; louder and less pleasant ratings coincided with higher relative theta and lower relative gamma power. Associations with arousal and performance were rare, and none were found for workload. The three response types were thus only partly consistent, and perceived responses and EEG distinguished conditions that performance did not. Non-significant performance differences do not mean the conditions were equivalent for students. Assessments of air-conditioning and road-traffic noise in classrooms should therefore consider perceived responses alongside cognitive performance, with EEG providing complementary information on neural activity during tasks.

</details>


### [330] [Seeing through the Eyes of AI: Situated Explainability in Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03232)
*Ana Stanescu,Lucchas Ribeiro Skreinig,Tobias Langlotz,Stefanie Zollmann,Peter Mohr,Dieter Schmalstieg,Mark Billinghurst,Denis Kalkofen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用增强现实（AR）技术在用户物理工作空间中实时呈现可解释性信息，以增强对AI模型的理解，并初步展示了现有可解释性方法在AR中的应用及用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI方法多通过2D显示器呈现基于预录制数据的解释，用户需将显示信息与物理对象和真实世界位置对应起来，导致数据探索与现实环境的脱节，在物理环境中的AI系统解释难以结合场景理解。

Method: 提出使用增强现实（AR），在用户探索世界的过程中，将可解释性信息直接实时叠加到用户的工作空间中，并展示如何将已有的可解释性方法应用于AR环境。

Result: 展示了现有可解释性方法在AR中的应用方式，并提供了用户在此类应用中的体验洞察。

Conclusion: AR为实现情境化、实时的可解释性提供了一条新路径，能够帮助用户更直观地理解物理环境中AI模型的决策行为。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (AI) enables humans to understand and interpret decisions of AI models. Instead of having a black box, explainability supports humans in understanding AI models' behavior. Existing explainable AI approaches often present explanations on 2D displays using pre-recorded data, requiring users to relate the displayed information back to the physical objects and real world locations involved in a model's decision. Users are forced to decouple data exploration and capture from AI model interpretation. For AI systems that work within physical environments, this separation can make explanations difficult to interpret in context. We propose using Augmented Reality (AR) to enhance the understanding of AI models by enabling spatial explainability information directly in a user's workspace, in real time, as they explore the world. We show how known explainability methods can be applied in AR and provide insights into user experiences with such an application.

</details>


### [331] [Interactive Machine Learning Interfaces for Disease Risk Prediction: Effects on Risk Perception and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03511)
*Tiffany Ngai,Max Homm,Matthew Bradbury,Anamaria Crisan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过15名参与者的混合方法探索性实验，考察用户对交互式2型糖尿病风险界面的理解程度及其对行为改变态度的影响，发现用户实际理解优于自我评估，但术语不清、风险表述模糊、模型输入解释不足仍是主要障碍，并提出设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习风险模型正日益应用于面向患者的健康工具，但用户对这些系统所呈现信息的理解程度尚不明确，这一空白可能影响工具的可用性、信任与公平性。

Method: 采用探索性混合方法研究，招募15名参与者，让其使用交互式2型糖尿病风险界面；对比参与者主观感知的理解水平与客观实际理解水平，并对定性访谈内容进行主题分析。

Result: 参与者对界面的实际理解往往好于其自身预期，但仍面临三类障碍：术语不清晰、风险表述方式模糊、对模型输入的解释有限。

Conclusion: 研究提出针对面向患者的机器学习界面的设计指南，并讨论信任与公平等更广泛议题；强调直观视觉设计、熟悉的呈现形式与清晰解释的重要性。

Abstract: Machine learning risk models are increasingly being used in patient-facing health tools, but it remains unclear how well users understand the information these systems present. In this work, we study how people interpret an interactive Type 2 Diabetes (T2D) risk interface and whether interacting with it influences their attitudes toward behavioural change. Through an exploratory mixed-methods study with 15 participants, we compare participants' perceived understanding with their actual understanding and identify key themes from qualitative interviews. We find that participants often understood the interface better than they initially believed, but still faced important barriers related to unclear terminology, ambiguous risk framing, and limited explanations of model inputs. Finally, we propose relevant design guidelines and discuss broader issues surrounding trust and fairness. Our findings highlight the importance of intuitive visual design, familiar presentation, and clear explanations in patient-facing ML interfaces.

</details>
